### [1] [CCAaaS: An Orchestration Platform for High-Velocity Design, Development, and Deployment of Data Center Transports at Meta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33231)
*Prankur Gupta,Balasubramanian Madhavan,Miao Xu,Prashanth Kannan,Jie Meng,Neil Spring,Rajiv Krishnamurthy,Srikanth Sundaresan,Martin Lau,Theophilus A. Benson*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CCAaaS is an eBPF-based ecosystem that lets hyperscale data centers design, deploy, and operate production-grade congestion control algorithms with modular BPF-TCP, disruption‑free orchestration, and lightweight observability, achieving native‑level performance and up to 20× lower P99 latency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data center traffic is diverse and evolving, especially with AI workloads, requiring flexible, scalable, and low‑overhead ways to develop and manage many congestion control algorithms.

Method: The paper presents a modular BPF‑TCP framework, a deployment and orchestration layer for zero‑downtime CCA updates, and a lightweight observability tool, using eBPF to implement and run three active CCAs that handle over 97% of server‑to‑server traffic.

Result: The eBPF‑centric CCAs match native in‑kernel performance in retransmissions, bandwidth, and tail latency while incurring negligible CPU and memory overhead, and one custom CCA cut cross‑region P99 latency by up to 20×.

Conclusion: An eBPF‑based CCAaaS approach enables rapid, production‑grade development and operation of congestion control algorithms with comparable performance, low resource cost, and significant network and application improvements.

Abstract: Today, data center networks run multiple congestion control algorithms (CCAs), each tailored to distinct traffic characteristics, from latency-sensitive intra-region RPCs to high-throughput cross-region AI workloads. The rapid evolution of AI training and inference further accelerates this trend by continually introducing new traffic characteristics that demand new CCAs.
  This paper introduces CCA-as-a-Service (CCAaaS), our eBPF-based ecosystem for the rapid design, deployment, and operation of production-grade CCAs at hyperscale. We present (1) a modular BPF-TCP framework and general lessons for designing modular CCAs; (2) a deployment and orchestration layer that provides disruption-free CCA updates, along with best practices for managing a zoo of CCAs at scale; and (3) a lightweight observability tool for CCAs and a blueprint for scaling CCA observability. CCAaaS manages over 97% of our server-to-server traffic using three active eBPF-based CCAs; two of the five CCAs we have built have since been deprecated. We show that our eBPF-centric approach to CCAs development delivers the same application-level performance (retransmissions, bandwidth, and tail latency) as their native in-kernel counterparts, and that our custom CCAs add negligible CPU and memory cost over their eBPF baselines. In terms of realized benefits, the CCA built with CCAaaS significantly improved network, and application metrics, for example, one of our CCAs reduced P99 latency for cross-region services by up to 20X.

</details>


### [2] [Concurrent Task Orchestration: Hierarchical Trusted Collaborator Group Formation for Constrained Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33295)
*Jiazhi Chen,Xianbin Wang,Xuemin "Sherman" Shen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信任协作者分组与资源分配的框架，利用时空图神经网络预测任务信任并结合公平感知深度强化学习解决多UAV协同任务编排问题，显著提升并发任务完成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的移动智能机器（如UAV）在复杂任务中需要有效协作，但并发任务需求、分布式资源有限及协作者动态可用性使得满足任务特定完成目标极具挑战。

Method: 将协作任务分解为受动态资源可用性和子任务依赖约束的子任务；构建任务效用模型支持协作者群评估与子任务分配；将并发任务编排建模为长期效用最大化下的α-公平问题；设计分层匹配策略先粗粒度聚类再细粒度分组分配；利用时空GNN预测任务特定信任并嵌入公平感知DRL框架持续更新匹配策略。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该策略在资源约束下较代表性基线将并发任务完成性能提升了22%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过信任感知的分层匹配与深度强化学习，有效解决了资源受限环境下多UAV协同编排的公平性与效率问题，为复杂协作系统提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Effective collaboration among resource-constrained mobile intelligent machines, such as Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), is essential for the completion of complex tasks. However, concurrent task demands, limited distributed resources, and dynamic collaborator availabilities make it challenging to satisfy task-specific completion goals in diverse collaborative systems. To address these challenges simultaneously, we propose a new framework of trusted collaborator group formation and the supporting resource allocation strategy for concurrent task orchestration. Specifically, a collaborative task is decomposed into subtasks subject to dynamic resource availability and inter-subtask dependencies. A task utility model is developed for collaborator group evaluation support subtask allocation according to task completion goals and unified value-risk analysis. Based on this model, concurrent task orchestration is formulated as a long-term utility maximization problem under $α$-fairness. To solve the problem efficiently, we develop a hierarchical matching strategy that first clusters tasks and collaborators into coarse-grained sets and subsequently performs fine-grained collaborator group formation and task allocation within matched sets. Task-specific trust is predicted through spatial-temporal Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and embedded into a fairness-aware Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) framework to continuously update the matching policies. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed strategy improves concurrent task completion performance by 22% over representative baselines under resource constraints.

</details>


### [3] [Analytical Modeling of Latency and Energy Trade-offs in FiRa-Like BLE-UWB Ranging and Localization Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33423)
*Adelina Giurea,Jorg Wieme,Ben Van Herbruggen,Jeroen Hoebeke,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种BLE辅助UWB系统的统一分析模型，涵盖从设备到设备测距到基础设施定位的完整工作流，给出发现、参数交换和测距的闭式表达式，并评估其延迟与能耗，指出BLE发现阶段在短时间UWB交互中占据主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB可提供精确位置和距离信息，但接收模式功耗过高，使得连续信道监测对受能源约束设备不现实，因此系统常依赖BLE进行对等发现和会话建立。尽管两种技术已被广泛独立研究，但仍缺乏捕捉完整工作流延迟和能耗的统一框架。

Method: 建立统一分析模型，包含设备到设备测距与基础设施定位场景，给出发现、参数交换和测距的闭式表达式，并对三种代表性BLE配置下的延迟和能耗进行评估。

Result: 扫描器每次发现消耗的能量是广告发布者的28-57倍；99百分位发现延迟是均值的2-3.4倍，在最低功耗配置下可达110秒；BLE一次性设置开销在24-562轮测距后摊销。

Conclusion: BLE发现阶段可主导短时间UWB交互的延迟和能量预算，发现配置与测距方案同等重要。

Abstract: Accurate position and distance information provided by Ultra-Wideband (UWB) can enhance a wide range of applications. However, its receive-mode power consumption makes continuous channel monitoring impractical for energy-constrained devices. Consequently, UWB systems often rely on Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) to discover peers and establish sessions before ranging begins. Although both technologies have been studied in isolation, an framework capturing the latency and energy consumption of the complete workflow is still lacking. To address this gap, we develop a unified analytical model for BLE-assisted UWB systems, from device-to-device ranging to infrastructure-based localization. The model includes closed-form expressions for discovery, parameter exchange, and ranging, and evaluates the resulting latency and energy consumption. Evaluation of three representative BLE configurations shows that the scanner consumes 28-57x more energy per discovery than the advertiser. In addition, the 99th-percentile discovery latency is 2-3.4x higher than the mean and reaches up to 110 s in the lowest-power configuration. The one-time BLE setup overhead is amortized after 24-562 ranging rounds, depending on the discovery configuration. Overall, the analysis demonstrates that the BLE discovery phase can dominate both the latency and the energy budget during short UWB interactions, making discovery configuration as important as the ranging scheme.

</details>


### [4] [Learning-Accelerated Joint AP/RIS Optimization for Low-Latency Indoor mmWave XR Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33945)
*Ali Elghariani,Mohammed Elbamby,Anis Elgabli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对扩展现实（XR）应用提出RIS辅助室内架构，在多接入点共享信道下联合优化AP预编码与RIS相位移，并通过交替优化与监督学习相结合提升和速率并降低计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR对低延迟和高速率要求高，RIS可改善室内覆盖，但多AP多用户耦合导致和速率最大化为非凸问题，传统迭代式交替优化随系统规模增长难以满足实时性需求。

Method: 首先制定耦合AP预编码和RIS相位移的非凸和速率最大化问题，提出集中式交替优化（AO）框架进行联合设计；随后利用AO生成的解训练监督学习模型，以显著加速收敛。

Result: 理论证明AO可保证和速率单调递增并收敛至坐标向稳定点；仿真表明在显著减少AO迭代次数的同时实现近最优性能。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾性能与计算效率，特别适合延迟敏感的RIS辅助XR部署，监督学习辅助AO能有效缓解实时性瓶颈。

Abstract: This paper studies a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted indoor architecture for extended reality (XR) applications, where multiple access points (APs) simultaneously serve multiple users over a shared frequency channel under a centralized AP controller. We formulate a non-convex sum-rate maximization problem with coupled AP precoders and RIS phase shifts, and develop a centralized alternating optimization (AO) framework for joint design. We rigorously prove that the proposed AO guarantees monotonic improvement of the sum rate that converges to a coordinate-wise stationary point, despite the non-convexity of the resulting subproblems. Although AO provides reliable performance, its iterative nature limits real-time applicability as the system dimension grows. To overcome this limitation, we propose a supervised learning-assisted approach that leverages AO-generated solutions to significantly accelerate convergence. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves near-optimal performance with substantially fewer AO iterations, which makes it well-suited for latency-critical RIS-assisted XR deployments.

</details>


### [5] [Garlic in a Box: I2P Network Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34004)
*Siddique Abubakr Muntaka,Faiza Hussein,Boutros Farah,Anthony Kashi,Jacques Bou Abdo,Jacques Demerjian*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Garlic-in-a-Box 是一个可安装的 I2P 仿真器，可在单机或集群上部署隔离的未修改 I2P Java 路由器网络，支持可复现的匿名网络安全研究和攻击实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 I2P 的路由和对等选择机制需要可控、可复现的环境，现有依赖签名重播种服务器和真实地理分布难以满足。

Method: 使用 Linux 网络命名空间隔离每个路由器，文件系统引导移除重播种依赖，合成地理多样性激活位置感知对等选择代码路径；部署 50 个路由器跨 5 个地理位置，稳定约 25 分钟，内存约 21 GB；建模对等选择为两阶段过程并进行声誉攻击实验。

Result: 五次独立试验中，攻击者以完美一致性进入受害者合格选择集，并被选为隧道的次数比匹配对照组多 1.38 倍。

Conclusion: 平台及测量管道已开源，支持可复现的 I2P 安全研究和实战网络防御训练。

Abstract: The Invisible Internet Project (I2P) is a peer-to-peer anonymity network whose routing and peer-selection mechanics underlie a growing family of state-sponsored covert relay architectures. Studying these mechanics demands a controlled, reproducible environment. We present Garlic-in-a-Box, an installable emulator that deploys a fully isolated network of unmodified I2P Java routers on a single host or cluster of hosts. Each router runs in its own Linux network namespace. A filesystem bootstrap removes the dependency on signed reseed servers, and synthetic geographic diversity activates the same location-aware peer-selection code paths present on the live network. A fifty-router deployment across five geographic locations stabilises in about twenty-five minutes and uses roughly 21 GB of memory. To demonstrate the platform's value, we model I2P peer selection as a two-stage process and conduct a reputation attack. Across five independent trials, the attack promotes the adversary into the victim's eligible selection set with perfect consistency, and the adversary is chosen for 1.38x more tunnels than matched controls. The platform and its measurement pipeline are released to support reproducible I2P security research and hands-on cyber-defence training.

</details>


### [6] [Kinetic Handover Modeling in Nonstationary Cellular Networks with Fluid Spatiotemporal Stochastic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34211)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Zheng Lin,Jinfeng Xu,Yuxin Zhang,Zihan Fang,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动量框架，用于推断非静态 cellular 网络中几何诱导的 Serving 区域交叉。该框架通过连接边界法向速度统计与基于密度的流体时空随机几何（F-STSG），实现对双向人口移动的推断。研究揭示了两类信息限制：人口密度演化不能唯一确定物理成对传输，且一阶运输无法解析反向移动交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人口密度演化并不能完全描述跨越 cell 边界的个体用户移动，推断定向的 handover 流量仍具挑战。这一问题的解决对于优化 cellular 网络的资源分配与提升用户体验具有重要意义。

Method: 建立了基于动量的框架，结合有符号的人口净传输与毛毛人口双向交叉，并引入 F-STSG 方法进行密度基础运输重建。同时，开发了相干与高斯机动闭合方法估计定向交叉率，以及在受限法向速度下构建的矩约束尖边界，不假设特定速度分布。

Result: 数值实验表明：在 47 个留出的车载仿真窗口中，F-STSG 相比三个基线方法具有更低的 net-flow 重建误差；在匹配的高斯机动下，辅助速度统计信息能改善定向估计精度；同时，构建的误差界明确了定向估计精度与运输重建及未解析机动信息之间的关系。

Conclusion: 所提框架在信息限制下提供了潜在运输的精确恢复条件，为 directional crossing 率的估计提供了有效闭合方法；F-STSG 方法在真实仿真场景中展现出优越的重建性能，验证了该方法的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Inferring directional handover traffic from aggregate mobility observations remains challenging because population density evolution does not fully describe the movement of individual users across cell boundaries. This paper develops a kinetic framework for geometry-induced serving-region crossings in nonstationary cellular networks, connecting boundary-normal velocity statistics with fluid spatiotemporal stochastic geometry (F-STSG) for density-based transport reconstruction. The framework distinguishes signed net population transfer from gross bidirectional crossings and reveals two information limits: density evolution does not uniquely determine physical pairwise transport, while first-order transport alone cannot resolve counter-moving crossings. We establish exact recovery conditions for potential transport and develop coherent and Gaussian mobility closures for estimating directional crossing rates. Sharp moment-constrained bounds further characterize the remaining uncertainty under bounded normal velocities without assuming a specific velocity distribution. An error bound links directional estimation accuracy to transport reconstruction and unresolved mobility information. The formulation also recovers the classical stationary Poisson--Voronoi handover law. Numerical experiments examine the information limits, reconstruction sensitivity, and closure accuracy. On 47 held-out vehicular simulation windows, F-STSG achieves lower net-flow reconstruction errors than three baselines, while auxiliary velocity statistics improve directional estimates under matched Gaussian mobility.

</details>


### [7] [6G Reasoning Agent Societies: Composition, Scaling, and Consistency-Time Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34357)
*Hatim Chergui,Carolina Fernández-Martínez,Mustapha Benjillali,Mehdi Bennis,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于细胞丛模型的6G控制面LLM智能体社会理论框架，确立了三条基本定律（组合定律、扩展定律、一致性时间定律），用于分析和约束大语言模型智能体之间误导信息（hallucinations）在网络中的传播与一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G时代控制平面引入大语言模型（LLM）智能体用于信息解释与推理，其通信内容可能存在“幻觉”（hallucination）问题，即使传输链路无错误，误导性结论仍可扩散。现有方法仅在本地进行审计，无法捕捉智能体社会层面的集体行为，因此亟需一种网络全局的理论框架来量化误差传播与一致性时延。

Method: 研究首先将LLM智能体社会建模为一个细胞丛（cellular sheaf）数学结构，并在此拓扑结构上建立统一的刻画框架。通过数学推导，提出以下三条定律：第一，组合定律——网络总分歧超过各代理审计分数的总和，等于它们的隐藏总相关性；第二，扩展定律——一致性时间在多项式生长拓扑上以n^(2/d)增长，路径拓扑在13个节点时超过实时预算；第三，一致性时间定律——忽略相关性所致的定时误差有向偏差，由最差链路决定。随后通过仿真拓扑与真实部署的LLM智能体验证该理论。

Result: 三条定律在模拟与真实部署场景中得到了验证。组合定律揭示了多达23%的联合分歧被掩盖，并因拓扑结构虚假提升信任1.14dB；扩展定律表明一致性时间在扩展性拓扑上增长受限，路径拓扑在13节点时触发近实时预算超限，而扩展子与半径为3的本地审计仍保持有界；一致性时间定律指出定时误差仅偏向放大，但受最差链路限制。

Conclusion: 该研究为6G控制面中基于LLM智能体的网络一致性问题提供了坚实的数学基础与可证明的最坏情况时延上限，特别是在近实时应用场景下具有重要实际价值，为未来设计可靠智能控制平面提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Emerging 6G control planes would compose Large Language Model (LLM) agents that exchange interpreted conclusions, so hallucinations can propagate even over error-free transport. Recent architectures audit each message locally against the sender's observation mask, but isolated audits miss collective behavior. Modeling the agent society as a cellular sheaf, we establish three laws valid on arbitrary topologies. First, the \emph{composition law}: network-wide divergence exceeds the sum of per-agent audit scores by exactly their hidden total correlation. This blind spot masks up to 23\% of joint divergence and, because the declared signal cancels in the cognitive signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), overstates it by $1.14$\,dB purely through topology; the resulting trust inflation preserves valid consensus states but slows convergence. Second, the \emph{scaling law}: reconciliation decouples across metric counters, so the worst-connected dimension sets the pace. Consistency time grows for a society size $n$ as $Ω(n^{2/d})$ on polynomial-growth topologies of degree $d$, and paths breach a $1$\,s near-real-time budget at $n^\ast=13$ agents, whereas expanders ($Θ(1)$) and radius-3 local audits ($O(1)$, $\approx300$\,ms) stay bounded. Third, the \emph{consistency-time law}: the timing error from omitted correlations is one-sided and capped by the worst-measured link. These laws, validated on simulated topologies and deployed LLM societies, give provable worst-case latency ceilings for near-real-time operation.

</details>


### [8] [LunarRF: A Terrain-Aware Propagation Engine and Pole-Wide 3GPP Surface Coverage Atlas of the Lunar South Pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34554)
*Christopher J. Santorelli,Alexander M. Wyglinski*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了LunarRF，一个用于评估月球南极阿耳忒弥斯任务中3GPP蜂窝链路地形感知表面覆盖的GPU系统。通过模拟1.1万个候选桅杆在1.8至25.375 GHz频段下的传播，生成了高精度的南纬80°以南20米分辨率覆盖图集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿耳忒弥斯月球表面任务将依赖3GPP蜂窝链路进行通信，但目前缺乏公开数据来展示这些链路在月球南极地区的实际物理可行性和具体覆盖范围，亟需高精度的地形感知覆盖预测工具。

Method: 利用GPU加速的足迹引擎，从11,827个候选桅杆位置投射8192条径向剖面，结合视距（viewshed）分析，并使用ITU-R P.1812 Delta-Bullington传播模型评估连续无线电地平线传播。通过无序解析器将结果合并至20米网格，最终生成存储有最佳服务器功率、冗余度等信息的极地图集，并使用抛物方程求解器、射线追踪和解析解进行验证。

Result: 在所有候选桅杆激活时，1.8 GHz、5.855 GHz和25.375 GHz的可覆盖率分别为90.5%、59%和10%（考虑引擎乐观偏差后调整为84%、43%和5%）。九个阿耳忒弥斯候选区域在1.8 GHz下的覆盖率达85-99%。模型对传播模型替换或引入地形噪声具有鲁棒性（平均影响低于1%）。

Conclusion: LunarRF提供了月球南极通信覆盖的全面高精度评估，图集和候选桅杆表已通过DOI公开发布。分析表明，虽然高频段覆盖率下降，但通过合理部署桅杆可以克服地形限制，通信可行性主要取决于链路预算和桅杆部署策略。

Abstract: Artemis surface operations will depend on 3GPP cellular links, yet no public product shows where such links are physically possible across the lunar south pole. We present LunarRF, a GPU system for terrain-aware surface coverage, and the pole-wide atlas produced with it. A footprint engine casts 8192 radial profiles from one of 11,827 candidate masts enumerated by stated rules on the orbit-adjusted LOLA elevation model, takes the viewshed, and evaluates ITU-R P.1812 Delta-Bullington propagation on every profile, continuous through the radio horizon. An order-independent resolver merges the footprints per 20 m cell, making the run shardable and resumable. The atlas covers the disc south of 80°S at 20 m for four SFCG-recommended surface bands and the IM-2 demonstration band, 1.8 to 25.375 GHz, under a stated link budget, storing per cell the best-server power and identity, redundancy count, line of sight and coverability. The engine is validated against a parabolic-equation solver, ray tracing and analytic solutions. Swapping Deygout for Delta-Bullington, or adding terrain noise at the level of the published height-error map of the DEM, moves the coverable class on under 1% of a mast's disc in the mean. With every candidate active, 90.5% of the disc is coverable at 1.8 GHz, 59% at 5.855 GHz and 10% at 25.375 GHz, or 84, 43 and 5% after the measured 3-4 dB engine optimism. A mast covers about 90% of its visible area within its budget-set range, and no band is terrain-limited. The nine current Artemis regions are 85-99% coverable at 1.8 GHz and 22-85% at 5.855 GHz. The atlas and candidate table are released with a DOI.

</details>


### [9] [Denial of Service Attacks on Beam Allocation in Large LEO Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34631)
*Ali Abbasi,Pasha Barahimi,Marco Paolieri,Leana Golubchik*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究了星座中波束分配算法易受需求通胀攻击的影响。攻击者注入虚假终端，评估了贪心算法和最大公平分配算法的脆弱性，并结合了等量共享和需求比例共享。发现贪心比例算法最易受攻击，但在正常情况下性能最佳。需求比例共享导致未获得服务用户比例增加高达34%，而等量共享的增加幅度限制在13%。共享方式还影响了最严重的攻击策略。最大公平分配迫使攻击者需要攻击更多小区才能造成严重破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析星座波束分配算法易受注入式服务拒绝攻击的影响，评估两种分配算法与两种容量共享策略的组合表现，并揭示鲁棒性与性能之间的权衡。

Method: 构建了注入式虚假终端的攻击模型，分别测试了贪心算法和最大公平分配算法，两种算法均与等量共享和需求比例共享策略结合。使用平均需求满意度、覆盖率和满足请求容量阈值的用户比例等指标衡量脆弱性，并采用多种攻击策略。

Result: 贪心比例算法最易受攻击，但在正常情况下性能最佳。需求比例共享导致未获得服务用户比例增加幅度高达34%，而等量共享仅增加13%。共享规则还影响了最严重的攻击策略：需求比例共享奖励将注入终端集中到最拥塞小区，而等量共享下攻击者更倾向于将终端分散到最不拥塞小区。最大公平分配迫使攻击者需要攻击更多小区才能造成严重破坏。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在改善系统性能的同时加强鲁棒性需要仔细权衡。采用等量共享可以降低攻击影响和降低攻击者组织复杂性，但会牺牲性能。建议在设计星座波束分配机制时平衡鲁棒性和性能，并考虑在存在注入式威胁时采用最大公平分配算法。

Abstract: We study the vulnerability of beam allocation approaches in large low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite constellations to adversarial demand inflation resulting in denial-of-service attacks. We consider an attack model in which an adversary places (injects) additional terminals and evaluate these attacks against greedy and max-min-fair beam allocation algorithms, each combined with either equal or demand-proportional intra-cell capacity sharing. Vulnerability is measured using mean demand satisfaction, coverage, and the fraction of legitimate users receiving at least a threshold fraction of their requested capacity under several attack heuristics. There is a trade-off for the system between robustness and performance, meaning the algorithm with the most vulnerability to the attacks (greedy-proportional) has the highest performance before the attacks. Demand-proportional sharing increases the fraction of under-served users up to 34% under both allocation algorithms, whereas equal sharing limits the increase up to 13%. The sharing rule also changes the most damaging attack allocation in nearly all cases: demand-proportional sharing rewards concentrating injected terminals in the most loaded cells, while equal sharing performs better when distributing these terminals across the least loaded cells. Furthermore, the max-min-fair beam allocation algorithm forces the attacker to use a higher number of targeted cells to result in extensive damage to the system.

</details>


### [10] [Satellite Intelligence: Distributed Training, Collaborative Inference,and LLM Adaptation in LEO Constellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35282)
*Zheng Lin,Zhanwei Wang,Yuxin Zhang,Yafei Wang,Ons Aouedi,Zihan Fang,Jinfeng Xu,Jing Yang,Vu Nguyen Ha,Yuanwei Liu,Mohamed-Slim Alouini,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次联合综述了低轨卫星网络中的分布式训练、协同推理和在轨大语言模型适配与服务，提出了卫星智能的设计原则和研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代AI训练范式与卫星网络动态拓扑、间歇链路及严格SWaP约束之间的根本矛盾，推动卫星网络从透传中继向智能机载处理转变。

Method: 系统梳理三大前沿范式：分布式训练（联邦学习、分割学习实现星地协同训练）、协同推理（单星、多星、星地架构满足实时需求）、在轨LLM适配与服务（应对严苛轨道约束下的部署挑战）。

Result: 提出了卫星智能部署挑战与设计原则，对比了三大范式的优劣，识别了开放挑战与未来方向，搭建了连接AI与卫星网络的研究路线图。

Conclusion: 该综述为空间原生AI技术的实施提供了全面指导，推动卫星网络向智能化、自主化演进。

Abstract: The proliferation of orbital sensing data and the real-time demands of emerging global services are pushing low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks from bent-pipe relays toward intelligent onboard processing. This shift confronts a fundamental tension, where modern artificial intelligence (AI) training paradigms require intensive computation, abundant memory, and stable connectivity, all of which are scarce under the dynamic topologies, intermittent links, and severe size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints of satellite networks. Addressing this tension demands rethinking AI workflows, from training and inference to the emerging deployment of large language models (LLMs), under a constraint-aware design philosophy. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first survey that jointly covers all three, whereas prior surveys examine these aspects in isolation. We begin by identifying the deployment challenge of satellite intelligence (SI) and deriving design principles for SI. We then examine three advancing paradigms: i) distributed training, where federated learning (FL) and split learning (SL) enable satellite-ground co-training under resource limitations; ii) collaborative inference, which exploits single-satellite, multi-satellite, and satellite-ground architectures to meet real-time service demands; and iii) on-orbit LLM adaptation and serving, which tackles the frontier challenge of deploying LLMs under severe orbital constraints and resource-aware serving. Finally, we identify open challenges and future directions. By bridging the AI and satellite networks, this paper provides a research roadmap for the implementation of space-native AI technologies.

</details>


### [11] [Trim: Fee Level Matters More Than Skew in Lightning Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35296)
*Huiping Sun,Peilu Guo,Yiming Zhu,Yueqi Wu,Zhong Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出 Trim 模型，将支付通道视为有补货成本的双边库存，给出最优费用为需求与补货决定的基准费用水平加上流动性偏斜。实证结果显示，该规则接近收益前沿，误差仅几百分点，且仅在补货频繁时偏斜才显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Lightning 路由费用设定缺乏理论依据，不清楚费用的哪一部分驱动收入。本研究旨在通过正式建模揭示费用、余额和补货成本之间的互动，以最大化收益并最小化被拒绝的转发。

Method: Trim 通过两-sided inventory 模型，将费用拆解为基准费用水平和余额偏斜。基准费用为一阶决策变量，对需求和补货敏感；偏斜为二阶保险机制，在补货成本高时才激活。通过分析收入-成功前沿确定最优设定。

Result: 在 2026 年合成支付快照中，无调参工具能超过 Trim 达的收益前沿超过几个百分点。基准费用若偏离最优两倍，收益将损失几十 percent；而平滑费用在中位数处可接近最优 1.6%。偏斜仅贡献几个百分点的毛收益变化。

Conclusion: 费用水平是决定收益的关键一阶因素，应根据需求和补货成本设定；余额偏斜为二阶修正，仅在补货成本高时使用。Trim 的规则提供了一种实用的近似最优费用设定方法，在实际场景中表现稳健。

Abstract: Lightning routing nodes lend their channel balance for a posted fee. Operators' tools move that fee with the balance around a hand-set level, and which part of the fee drives revenue is unclear. We present Trim, which models a channel as a two-sided inventory with costly refills. Its optimal fee is a level, set by demand and replenishment, plus a balance skew, the shadow price of liquidity. The level is first order: no fee rule beats the ceiling demand and replenishment set, and on exactly solved channels the best flat fee comes within $1.6\%$ of the optimum at the median, whereas a level off by a factor of two loses tens of percent. The skew is second-order insurance: it adds at most what the flow-balancing flat fee loses to refused forwards. So the level should be chosen: Trim's rule traces the revenue--success frontier, posts one level on every channel, and adds a skew only where refills stay frequent and costly. On a 2026 snapshot with synthetic payments, no tuned tool beats that frontier by over a few percent, and a skew changes gross revenue by a few percent.

</details>


### [12] [Uncertainty-Guided Counterfactual Sensor Selection and Safety-Gated Recognition for Autonomous Sensing Agents in IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35344)
*Shirong Guo,Hongming Zhou,Muhammad Attique Khan,Jinfeng Xu,Yonis Gulzar,Manar Mousa Altamimi,Kunfu Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: We analyze a paper proposing PGIK-Fusion, a counterfactual sensing framework for autonomous IoT systems under sensor faults and communication loss. It constructs class evidence using physics-guided invariant-kinematic fusion, selects actions (acceptance, sensor request, handoff) based on calibrated risk and cost-sensitive utility, and reduces unsafe automation. On the Logistic Activity Recognition dataset, it outperforms XGBoost+1D-CNN by 2.49% accuracy and 2.00% Macro-F1. Across degradation conditions, it reduces unsafe acceptance from 33.0% to 13.8% and raises automation accuracy by 18.5 percentage points versus random policy. In wind-turbine auxiliary study, it achieves 0.975 Macro-F1, improves by 1.17pp, and reduces unsafe acceptance from 20.1% to 8.0%. The paper demonstrates that a unified perception–action loop combining calibrated recognition, targeted evidence acquisition, and conservative deferral can reduce unsafe automation under sensor degradation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous IoT systems relying on distributed sensing face challenges from sensor faults and communication loss that leave multichannel evidence incomplete or unreliable. Agents must classify measurements, assess evidence sufficiency, select a sensor to reacquire, and hand off unsafe decisions. The paper aims to address these challenges through a counterfactual sensing framework with controlled alternative and pre-request utility models.

Method: PGIK-Fusion constructs class evidence using physics-guided invariant-kinematic fusion. A degraded window defines the factual state, each candidate restoration defines a controlled alternative, and a pre-request utility model estimates loss reduction. Calibrated risk and cost-sensitive utility then select acceptance, sensor request, or handoff from factual information.

Result: On the Logistic Activity Recognition Challenge dataset, PGIK-Fusion achieves 0.768 accuracy and macro-averaged F1 score (Macro-F1) of 0.696, exceeding XGBoost—1D-CNN fusion by 2.49 and 2.00 percentage points respectively. Across seven predefined degradation conditions on held-out subjects, the policy reduces unsafe acceptance from 33.0% to 13.8%; relative to an action-rate-matched random policy, it raises automation accuracy by 18.5 percentage points and lowers expected cost by 24.7%. In the within-event wind-turbine study, PGIK-Fusion reaches 0.975 Macro-F1, improves on XGBoost—1D-CNN by 1.17 percentage points, and reduces unsafe acceptance from 20.1% to 8.0%.

Conclusion: The results demonstrate that calibrated recognition, targeted evidence acquisition, and conservative deferral can form a unified perception–action loop that reduces unsafe automation under sensor degradation. Within the evaluation scope, this approach effectively handles predefined fault families and severities.

Abstract: Autonomous IoT systems rely on distributed sensing, but sensor faults and communication loss can leave multichannel evidence incomplete or unreliable. Their agents must therefore classify measurements, assess evidence sufficiency, select a sensor to reacquire, and hand off unsafe decisions. We formulate counterfactual sensing: a degraded window defines the factual state, each candidate restoration defines a controlled alternative, and a pre-request utility model estimates loss reduction. Physics-Guided Invariant-Kinematic Fusion (PGIK-Fusion) constructs class evidence, while calibrated risk and cost-sensitive utility select acceptance, sensor request, or handoff from factual information. On the Logistic Activity Recognition Challenge dataset, PGIK-Fusion achieves 0.768 accuracy and a macro-averaged F1 score (Macro-F1) of 0.696, exceeding extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost)--one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1D-CNN) fusion by 2.49 and 2.00 percentage points, respectively. Across seven predefined degradation conditions on held-out subjects, the policy reduces unsafe acceptance from 33.0% to 13.8%; relative to an action-rate-matched random policy, it raises automation accuracy by 18.5 percentage points and lowers expected cost by 24.7%. In the auxiliary within-event wind-turbine study, PGIK-Fusion reaches 0.975 Macro-F1, improves on XGBoost--1D-CNN by 1.17 percentage points, and reduces unsafe acceptance from 20.1% to 8.0%. The evaluation characterizes the controlled responses of a frozen recognition model to specified sensor restorations under predefined fault families and severities. Within this scope, the results demonstrate that calibrated recognition, targeted evidence acquisition, and conservative deferral can form a unified perception--action loop that reduces unsafe automation under sensor degradation.

</details>


### [13] [Routing Around the Queue: An Evaluation of Eco-Routing Protocol on different Traffic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35479)
*Leandros Maglaras,George Boustras,Azidine Guezzaz,Naghmeh Moradpoor*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 评估了ErouVe协议，发现在轻度交通时其整体效果不明显，但在严重拥堵时能显著降低CO2排放。提出了三种纠正方法，分别解决知识不对称、噪声数据导致的决策波动以及每秒CO2指标奖励怠速的问题，从而恢复了协议的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估ErouVe协议的有效性，识别其在不同交通条件下的表现不足，并解决 crowdsourced 路由信号的通用问题（如冷启动、噪声数据和每秒CO2指标）。

Method: 在Veins框架内实现ErouVe协议，利用SUMO的路由功能，而非自定义的 shortest-path 搜索。通过在包含一条受信号控制的短路和一条无信号的长绕行路的网络上进行模拟，测试了两种交通场景。根据模拟结果，识别了协议的不足之处，并提出了三种纠正方法。

Result: - 轻度交通时，ErouVe的整体减排效果不明显。
- 在拥堵交通下，协议成功实现了中等程度的车队-wide CO2 减排，绕行车辆的个人燃料消耗则更高。
- 提出的方法（知识门控的成本接受规则、绕行锁定规则和距离归一化的 CO2 成本函数）针对性地解决了冷启动时的知识不对称、噪声数据下的决策波动以及奖励怠速的每秒 CO2 指标问题。
- 这三种方法均有效地恢复了协议的有效性。

Conclusion: 评估结果揭示了ErouVe协议的优势和局限性。通过引入知识门控成本接受、绕行锁定和距离归一化的 CO2 成本函数等方法，可以解决 crowdsourced 路由信号中常见的挑战，从而增强协议在各种交通条件下的鲁棒性和环境效益。

Abstract: We evaluate ErouVe, a vehicular eco-routing protocol in which roadside units (RSUs) collect per-segment travel-time and CO2 reports from passing vehicles and answer route requests with the lowest-cost alternative, across a range of road architectures and traffic scenarios. We conduct the evaluation with a working implementation of the protocol inside Veins, adapted to drive SUMO's own router rather than a hand-rolled shortest-path search, on a network offering a short, signal-controlled route and a longer, signal-free bypass, under two traffic scenarios. This evaluation reveals clear strengths and weaknesses of the protocol. Under light, uncongested traffic, ErouVe's fleet-wide effect is light. Under traffic dense enough to stress the signalized route's throughput and produce a real, growing queue, the same protocol succeeds a medium fleet-wide CO2 reduction, with diverted vehicles individually saving even more fuels. We use these findings to propose three targeted corrective methods; a knowledge-gated cost-acceptance rule, a diversion-locking rule, and a distance-normalized CO2 cost function, that respectively address a cold-start knowledge asymmetry, decision flapping under noisy crowd-sourced data, and a per-second CO2 metric that rewards idling. Each of these weaknesses are generic pitfalls for any crowd-sourced routing signal and all proposed methods measurably restore protocol effectiveness.

</details>


### [14] [RLNC vs Reed-Solomon in Satellite Channels: Crossover Analysis and the Bridging Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35649)
*Angeline Szczepanski,Vipindev Adat Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RLNC展现了桥接属性：RS在临界丢包率p*≈0.21以上崩溃，而RLNC持续工作，开销按1/(1-p)增长；杂交码相比纯RLNC降低19%开销。在实际卫星信道下，RS(255,204)不切实际，RLNC恢复1000包需15.5 Mbit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星通信在偏远地区和6G网络中至关重要，需在高往返时延和丢包环境下使用前纠错码。

Method: 在三跳卫星信道上评估RS、RLNC及RS+RLNC混合编码，信道损失统计基于轨道几何和链路预算计算而非假设，比较匹配码率0.800的RS与RLNC。

Result: 核心发现是RLNC的桥接属性：所有RS码率在临界丢包率p*≈0.21以上崩溃，RLNC穿越所有失效区间且开销随1/(1-p)增长。在率0.800下，RS(255,204)尽管平均丢包率仅1%仍不切实际，RLNC恢复1000包需15.5 Mbit；RS+RLNC混合码相比纯RLNC在BER=10^{-3}下降低19%开销。

Conclusion: 作者首次在物理派生卫星信道模型下，给出了RS与RLNC之间的码率归一化临界p*的解析推导，并首次验证了桥接属性。

Abstract: Satellite communication systems are increasingly relied upon for connectivity in remote areas and are expected to play a pivotal role in 6G networks, operating under high round-trip times and packet loss where forward error correction is essential. This work evaluates Reed-Solomon (RS) coding, Random Linear Network Coding (RLNC), and a combined RS+RLNC hybrid over a three-hop satellite channel whose loss statistics are derived from orbital geometry and link-budget calculations rather than assumed, comparing RS and RLNC at matched code rates of 0.800. The central finding is the \emph{bridging property} of RLNC: every RS code rate collapses above a crossover threshold $p^*$, while RLNC operates continuously through all failure regions with overhead growing as $1/(1-p)$. At rate~0.800, $p^* \approx 0.21$. Under the derived satcom channel with mean burst duration 166~minutes, RS$(255,204)$ is impractical despite an average loss rate of 1%, while RLNC requires 15.5~Mbit to recover all 1000 packets. An RS+RLNC hybrid reduces overhead by 19% relative to pure RLNC at BER$=10^{-3}$. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first analytical derivation of the rate-normalised crossover $p^*$ between RS and RLNC and the first experimental confirmation of the bridging property under a physically derived satellite channel model.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [15] [Beyond Correctness: Attribution of Natural-Language Documentation for Formal Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33094)
*Sirui Jiang,Dongming Xiang,Shichao Wu,Yuxuan Zhou,Yihong Wu,Fangze Zhu,Wang Lin,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DocAuth通过归因检查文档语句是否与函数实际行为相符，提升可归因句子比例并提高验证率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正式语言规范难以阅读，开发者依赖自然语言文档，但文档语句可能与函数行为脱节，需确保文档真实反映实现。

Method: DocAuth设计一种基于归因的文档生成方法，判断句子是否可归因，并为每句附上可检查的正式属性证据，实现文档的可验证性。

Result: 在150个ACL2库函数上，三个大语言模型产生约三分之一不可归因属性；DocAuth的可归因句子率与基于变异的过滤相当，但可归因句子比例提升37%，且二次模型确认可归因比例从82.9%提升至94.7%。

Conclusion: 证明句子真实并不保证文档反映函数实际行为，归因是文档生成和评估的必要补充标准。

Abstract: Formal languages are widely used in software engineering to specify and verify program properties. Because their mathematical notation and logic are hard to read, developers often rely on natural-language documentation to understand the functions in formal libraries. Yet a documentation statement can be provably true and still hold after the function's behavior changes. We call a statement's dependence on the function's specific behavior attribution, and we give a method to check it. We design and implement DocAuth, a documentation-generation method that uses attribution to decide what to publish and attaches to each published sentence the formal property recovered from it, together with checkable evidence. On 150 functions from the Community Books library of the ACL2 theorem prover, three large language models produce proved but non-attributable properties for about a third of the functions. DocAuth matches or exceeds the attribution rate of a mutation-based filter while delivering 37% more attributable sentences. Compared with a pipeline that checks truth alone, it raises the share of published sentences that a second model confirms as attributable from 82.9% to 94.7%. These results show that provable truth does not guarantee that documentation reflects what a function actually does. Attribution complements truth as a criterion for generating and evaluating documentation for formal libraries.

</details>


### [16] [ToolContractGate: Versioned Trusted Contracts for Drift-Safe Agent Tool Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33108)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolContractGate 通过版本化的合约将开放API指纹与访问、模式和行为声明绑定，实现十项检查的决定性运行时评估，能够在合成基准上检测全部 35 个不安全故障，决策覆盖率高，延迟极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 Agent 工具批准和输入校验无法单独将请求绑定到已批准的源代码文件，需要一种能够在运行时强制执行合约的机制。

Method: ToolContractGate 使用每个版本化的 ToolContract，包含 OpenAPI 指纹、访问、模式和行为声明，实现十项检查（含缓存的启动时溯源结果），并通过四种执行模式和五种评估配置支持基线、削减和非阻塞影子分析，在 45 条合成案例上验证。

Result: 在 35 条不安全案例和 10 条安全案例的基准上，完整管道检测到全部 35 个故障，产生 29 条阻塞、3 条审查、3 条警告的决策，安全案例无误报，平均延迟 0.18 ms，p95 为 2 ms。

Conclusion: 合约决策覆盖率得到验证，后续强制执行需依赖支持返回决策的宿主，而生产流量、语义后端漂移和动态溯源刷新仍是后续研究的机会。

Abstract: Agent tool approval and input validation do not, by themselves, bind a request to an approved source artifact. ToolContractGate provides a deterministic runtime contract evaluator for OpenAPI-derived agent tools. Each versioned ToolContract combines a stored OpenAPI fingerprint with access, schema, and behavioral declarations. The full evaluator applies ten checks, including a cached boot-time provenance result; four execution modes and five evaluation configurations support baseline, ablation, and non-blocking shadow analysis. On a synthetic benchmark of 45 cases (35 unsafe cases across 12 drift classes and 10 safe cases), the full pipeline detects all 35 faults: 29 BLOCK, 3 REVIEW, and 3 WARN decisions, with no false blocks on the safe cases. The archived run reports a mean gate latency of 0.18 ms and a 95th percentile (p95) of 2 ms using integer-millisecond measurements. The open-source artifact includes seven OpenAPI specifications, twelve enterprise contracts, 29 unit tests, and a 900-record evaluation. Results characterize contract-decision coverage; downstream enforcement requires a host that honors the returned decision. Production traffic, semantic backend drift, and dynamic provenance refresh remain evaluation and integration opportunities.

</details>


### [17] [Navigating Epistemic Parity in LLM Agents: A Benchmark for Cross-Source Conflict Resolution Between Memory and Procedural Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33128)
*Prakhar Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基准测试，用于衡量LLM代理在面对记忆（episodic memory）与技能（skill modules，如SKILL.md文件）之间矛盾指令时如何做出裁定。研究发现，不同模型在不同冲突类型与注入顺序下表现不一致，未能形成统一的优先级模式，且绝大多数情况下代理会静默解决冲突而不向用户透露信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生产级LLM代理结合运行记忆管道与技能模块的趋势增多，这两类指令常被同时注入上下文，但缺乏明确的优先级顺序，可能导致指令间矛盾，形成所谓的‘认知平等’（epistemic parity）问题。研究旨在量化并分析这种跨源冲突下的代理行为，以推动更安全可靠的代理设计。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含五十二个场景的基准测试，每个场景由记忆注入、技能注入和模凌虚词的用户任务组成。他们提出了四类矛盾类型（事实性、格式、程序性、权限），并在三种上下文条件下运行测试：一种隔离位置偏见，一种测试显式优先级指令的效果，一种分类披露行为。所有评估几乎完全依赖确定性检查器对工具调用载荷和输出结构。涵盖三种开源模型共1,383次运行。

Result: 结果显示，无单一信息源能始终胜出；裁定结果共同受冲突类型、模型和注入顺序的影响。将技能块靠近任务可提升其在事实性和程序性冲突中的胜率（ sign 检验，p = 0.031），但对格式冲突却反向。显式“技能优先”指令虽在较强模型上提高了技能遵循度，但仍有3%~10%的不遵从率，且对小模型可能适得其反。在98.8%的工具调用场景中，代理未输出任何文本；89%的运行对冲突保持缄默，静默裁定成为默认行为。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理在处理记忆与技能之间的指令冲突时缺乏一致性与透明度，现有机制（如显式优先级指令）无法完全解决问题。未来需设计更鲁棒的指令层次机制与披露策略，以增强代理的可解释性与可控性。

Abstract: Production LLM agents increasingly run episodic memory pipelines and procedural skill modules (e.g., SKILL.md files) in tandem. Both are injected into the same context region with no defined precedence, so they can issue contradictory instructions that no instruction hierarchy resolves, a condition we term epistemic parity. We introduce a benchmark for measuring how LLM agents adjudicate such cross-source conflicts. We (i) develop a four-way taxonomy of memory-skill contradictions (factual, format, procedural, permission); (ii) instantiate 52 scenarios under a tripartite test-case anatomy (memory injection, skill injection, ambiguous user task), scored almost entirely by deterministic checkers over tool-call payloads and output structure; and (iii) run each scenario under three context conditions to isolate positional bias, measure the efficacy of an explicit precedence directive, and classify disclosure behavior, quantifying silent adjudication: resolving a conflict without telling the user. Across 1,383 runs on three open-weight models, neither source wins uniformly: resolution depends jointly on conflict type, model, and injection order. Moving the skill block nearer the task raises its win rate in all six factual and procedural cells (sign test, p = 0.031) but reverses for format conflicts, indicating that adjudication tracks token placement rather than source semantics. An explicit "skill takes absolute precedence" directive raises skill compliance on the two stronger models yet leaves 3-10% residual non-compliance, and moves the smallest model's factual and procedural resolution in the wrong direction. Most consequentially, in 98.8% of runs where the agent acted through a tool call it emitted no accompanying text at all, and 89% of all runs disclosed nothing about the contradiction: silent adjudication is the default. Code, scenarios, and raw outputs are public.

</details>


### [18] [Speculative Forking: Turning Idle Wait into Parallel Exploration for Repository Environment Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33129)
*Yuling Shi,Mingchu Zhong,Zhensu Sun,Weihan Peng,Zhi Chen,Shilin He,Chengcheng Wan,Kai Cai,Hongyu Zhang,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecBuild 是一种基于 LLM 的环境配置系统，通过推测性分叉并行探索多个配置分支，显著提高成功率并缩短配置时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理采用顺序试错方式配置环境，命令执行耗时长，导致整体过程缓慢且一次只能探索一条路径，无法兼顾速度与可靠性。

Method: 提出 SpecBuild，在两个粒度应用推测性分叉：策略级分叉并行尝试不同基础镜像和依赖策略；命令级分叉预测命令失败并预先准备修复分支。系统并行运行分支，提交首个成功环境并丢弃其余。

Result: 在 Multi-Docker-Eval 基准（334 任务，40 仓库，9 语言）上，SpecBuild 在三个骨干模型上将成功率提升 6.4–12.0 个百分点，平均配置时间缩短 22.4%–41.6%。

Conclusion: 并行推测性探索使 LLM 环境配置代理能同时实现更快速度和更高可靠性，无需在两者间权衡。

Abstract: Before developers can use a software project, they must first get it to run. This often requires installing the right language runtime, system packages, build tools, and test commands. Recent LLM-based agents automate environment setup by inspecting a repository, writing setup scripts, running them, and repairing the resulting errors. However, these agents still follow a sequential trial-and-error loop: they try one setup route, wait for slow commands to finish, observe the failure, and only then decide what to try next. Because environment commands often take long to run, this sequential workflow makes setup slow and limits exploration to one route at a time.
  We present SpecBuild, an LLM-based environment setup system designed to be both faster and more successful through speculative forking. SpecBuild explores candidate setup branches in parallel, returns the first committed environment, and discards the rest. It applies this design at two granularities: strategy-level forking explores alternative setup routes, such as different base images and dependency policies, while command-level forking predicts failures of running commands and prepares repair branches before those failures are observed.
  We evaluate SpecBuild on Multi-Docker-Eval, covering 334 tasks from 40 repositories and 9 programming languages. Across three backbone models, SpecBuild improves the success rate by 6.4 to 12.0 percentage points over the strongest baseline, while reducing average setup time by 22.4% to 41.6%. These results show that LLM-based environment setup need not trade speed for reliability: parallel speculative exploration can make agents both faster and more successful.

</details>


### [19] [WebUniverse: Scaling Verified Training Data for Agentic Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33149)
*Yaorui Ding,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Yuetai Li,Zhangchen Xu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Xuanyu Lu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出WebUniverse，一个从开源代码库和真实网页构建可验证前端开发任务及轨迹的管道，发布15K可执行环境和15K带验证记录的开发轨迹，并通过微调模型显著提升多项前端网页基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前端网页代理训练需要代码编辑、执行、浏览器检查与修订的演示数据，但公开的可重用任务环境和轨迹资源有限，阻碍了该领域代理的训练与评估。

Method: 构建WebUniverse管道，从开源仓库和真实网页生成可验证任务，覆盖文本驱动的独立与迭代应用开发以及跨页面、跨视口的视觉驱动还原；收集并验证代理轨迹，发布WebUniverse-Env（15K可执行任务环境）和WebUniverse-Mix（15K带验证记录与得分的开发轨迹）。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B上微调后，Vision2Web平均得分从51.7提升至68.3，WebGen-Bench准确率从34.5%提升至63.9%，Web-Bench pass@2从12.2%提升至23.1%；在相同配置和样本预算下优于公开数据基线，且消融实验表明文本与视觉轨迹互补，数据从5K规模扩展到15K时性能持续提升。

Conclusion: WebUniverse提供了可扩展、可验证的前端Web开发训练资源，验证了高质量文本与视觉开发轨迹对提升网页代理能力的有效性及可扩展性。

Abstract: Training agents for front-end web development requires demonstrations of code editing, execution, browser inspection and revision, yet public trajectories and reusable task environments remain limited. We introduce WebUniverse, a pipeline that constructs verifiable tasks with verified development trajectories from open-source repositories and real web pages. The tasks cover text-grounded application development in standalone and iterative settings and vision-grounded reproduction across pages and viewports. We collect agent trajectories for these tasks and verify the resulting applications against task-specific functional or visual requirements. We release WebUniverse-Env, containing 15K executable task environments, and WebUniverse-Mix, containing the corresponding 15K development trajectories with verification records and scores. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on WebUniverse-Mix raises the Vision2Web average score from 51.7 to 68.3, WebGen-Bench accuracy from 34.5% to 63.9%, and Web-Bench pass@2 from 12.2% to 23.1%. The resulting model also outperforms a public-data baseline trained with the same configuration and sample budget across all reported metrics. Ablations show complementary benefits from text and visual trajectories and continued gains as training data scales from 5K to 15K trajectories.

</details>


### [20] [Cross-Platform Application Migration to HarmonyOS: An Agentic Pipeline with Artifact-Derived Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33197)
*Ergun Biçici,Mehmet Kaygusuz,Fatih Turan Gündoğdu,Emre Filiz,Çağatay Kızıldağ,Bünyamin Eymen Alagöz,Emincan Özcan,Sadi Çelik,Tolunay Öztürk,Ege Bulut,Said Aldemir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HMOS~Migrator 是一个代理化管道，通过阅读应用源代码、生成 ArkTS 项目并通过真实设备上的交互重放进行验证，自动化实现 HarmonyOS NEXT 的迁移。它使用无参考实现的质量估计，定义了七个加权度量指标，确保测量减少无法人为提高分数。在六个开源应用的 twelve 次运行中，结构复现率高（完整性 83.3%–100.0%），但设备内部行为（4.3%–63.1%）和 UI 保真度（21.1%–63.2%）仍是主要限制。分数随时间稳步提升：整体每天 +0.45 分（R²=0.44, p=0.019）以及模型无关子集 +0.47 分（R²=0.39, p=0.029），对应角度分别增加 +8.4° 和 +11.7°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Android 或 iOS 上编写的应用迁移至 HarmonyOS NEXT 需要完整重写 ArkTS。尽管大型语言模型可以自动化源代码到 ArkTS 的翻译，但语法编译尚不够，功能保证是 HarmonyOS NEXT 迁移中的关键公开挑战。

Method: 我们提出 HMOS~Migrator，一个代理化管道，它阅读应用源代码、生成 ArkTS 项目，并通过在真实设备上重放记录的交互来验证结果。我们将评分问题设定为无参考实现的质量估计；仅使用源应用的记录行为和管道产生的 artifacts 来计分。我们定义了七个加权指标，每个指标的构造均确保测量减少无法人为抬高分数。

Result: 在十二次跨六个开源应用的运行中，管道的结构复现率很高（完整性 83.3%–100.0%），而设备内部行为（4.3%–63.1%）和 UI 保真度（21.1%–63.2%）仍然是主要限制。分数随时间稳步提升：整体每天 +0.45 分（R²=0.44, p=0.019）以及模型无关子集 +0.47 分（R²=0.39, p=0.029），对应的拟合角度分别为 +8.4° 和 +11.7°。

Conclusion: HMOS~Migrator 管道展示了对 migrated ArkTS 项目的有效结构复现，但设备内部行为和 UI 保真度仍是功能保证的主要障碍。随时间推移分数的稳步提升表明，迭代式的管道增强可以逐步缩小这些差距，但需要针对设备级执行和界面渲染的针对性工作才能实现生产就绪的迁移。

Abstract: The migration of applications written for a different platforms like Android or iOS to HarmonyOS NEXT requires a complete rewrite in ArkTS. Although LLMs automate the translation of source constructs into ArkTS, syntactic compilation is not enough and functional assurance is a critical open challenge in HarmonyOS NEXT migration. We present HMOS~Migrator, an agentic pipeline that reads an application's source code, generates an ArkTS project, and validates the result by replaying recorded interactions on a real device. We cast the scoring problem as quality estimation without a reference implementation; we score a migration using only the source application's recorded behaviour and the artifacts produced by the pipeline. We define seven weighted metrics, each constructed so that reduced measurement cannot artificially raise the score. Over twelve runs across six open-source applications, the pipeline's structural reproduction is high (completeness \pct{83.3}--\pct{100.0}), while on-device behaviour (\pct{4.3}--\pct{63.1}) and UI fidelity (\pct{21.1}--\pct{63.2}) remain the primary limits. Scores improve steadily over time: $+0.45$~points per day overall ($R^2=0.44$, $p=0.019$) and $+0.47$ over the model-free subset ($R^2=0.39$, $p=0.029$), with corresponding to fit angles of $+8.4^{\circ}$ and $+11.7^{\circ}$ respectively.

</details>


### [21] [Operationalizing Governed Agentic Workflows in Regulated Finance: A Customer Zero Case Study using Zafin AIOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33253)
*Shahir Daya,Rob Dunlap,Alexandre Boulenger,Ahmed Abdelmoneim,Salvatore Vella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Zafin AIOS 是一个用于受监管行业的端到端 AI 编排平台，目标是将 AI 辅助软件开发从实验推向生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融、医疗等受监管行业在 AI 软件开发治理方面面临关键挑战，阻碍 AI 辅助开发采用和实验到生产转型。

Method: 通过架构原则和内部企业工作流案例（Customer Zero）开展案例研究，比较 AI 辅助与人工为主 workload，并用两个生产代码库评估 Zafin AIOS。

Result: 在定价执行平台降低 38% 工作量并提供完整可观测性；在客户交易谈判平台降低 76% 报告工作，同时保持数据隐私、角色访问和审计护栏。

Conclusion: 严格受控的 Agentic 编排可能在受监管行业提升企业 AI 能力而不牺牲控制，但结果属于受控场景的描述性证据。

Abstract: Regulated industries such as finance and healthcare face critical governance challenges that hamper the adoption of AI-assisted software development and hinder the transition from experimental pilots to production work. This paper presents Zafin AIOS, an end-to-end orchestration platform and control plane designed to deploy AI within regulated industries such as finance. We provide the architectural principles and an empirical comparison between AI-assisted and human-led workloads. To validate this framework empirically, we conduct a case study analysis of Zafin's internal enterprise workflows, which we refer to as Customer Zero. This paper contributes a production case study of AI-assisted software engineering in regulated industries. We evaluate the results of using Zafin AIOS with two different production codebases, one for the pricing execution platform and the other for the client deal negotiation platform. Our contribution is a customer case study for regulated industries with production codebases and governance, which includes proof of work. The results demonstrate that strictly governed agentic orchestration increases enterprise capacity without compromising control. In the pricing execution platform workflows, the platform provided complete observability and reduced effort by 38\%. In the client deal negotiation platform, it reduced reporting work by 76\% while maintaining all predefined data privacy, role-based access, and audit guardrails. This paper bridges the gap between AI-assisted software engineering theory and the operational reality of compliance. Closing this gap is important to help enterprises take advantage of AI technologies. These results are descriptive of the benefits of using governed workflows in regulated industries.

</details>


### [22] [Responsibility Boundaries for Agentic Systems: A Comparative Evaluation of Probabilistic Agentic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33312)
*Khadim Lo,Soukaina Alaoui Ismaili,Jean-Sébastien Dessureault*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率代理编程（PAP）和Touba语言，旨在为语言模型代理显式定义责任边界（授权、权限、影响和证据）。通过引入调解内核（mediation kernel）、形式化验证方法以及大规模实验，证明了该框架能有效约束代理行为并保障条件安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理的权限分散在提示词、宿主代码、配置和日志中，责任边界模糊，导致难以在运行时实施有效的安全控制和责任划分。

Method: 提出概率代理编程（PAP）抽象，定义了连接授权、权限、影响和证据的显式边界。设计了包含工具执行前检查、操作级审批、资源限制和证据收集的调解内核。通过归纳证明建立条件安全保证，并利用向后模拟、人工检查点和有限状态探索进行验证。最后，对7个业务案例、7个模型、5种方法进行了5次重复实验，共获得1225个评估观察值。

Result: 原生Touba在6个模型上完成了全部35个任务，在另1个模型上完成30个；结构化Touba完成数在21至35之间。原生Touba在各模型上的最高观测计数匹配或并列最佳表现，且所有配对案例区间均包含零，表明未引入显著负向偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作将责任契约、执行规则和证据紧密结合，表明基于PAP的显式编程抽象和内核级调解机制，能够在保持运行时权限边界固定的前提下，实现对任意模型响应的条件安全保证。

Abstract: Language-model agents combine reasoning, tools, memory, delegation, and external effects. Authority is often distributed across prompts, host code, configuration, and logs. Probabilistic Agentic Programming (PAP) makes responsibility boundaries explicit programming abstractions connecting mandate, authority, effects, and evidence. We compare PAP with programming paradigms, agent-oriented and BDI systems, workflow and policy languages, and LLM tool-use frameworks along seven dimensions. A mediation kernel formalizes checks before tool execution, operation-bound approval, resource limits, and evidence. Inductive proofs establish conditional safety for arbitrary model responses, assuming correct guards and complete mediation. A backward simulation matches graph executions to source executions in a defined sequential Touba subset. Its human checkpoints release program continuations; operation-bound approval remains a separate kernel contract. Finite-state exploration checks the model. Scripted enforcement tests exercise selected refusal paths and permitted controls. An exploratory study combines seven business cases, including two three-agent workflows, seven models from three providers, five methods, and five repetitions per case and method. Shared English instructions and exact checks of stored outcomes yield 1,225 assessed observations. Native Touba completes all 35 observations on six models and 30 on the remaining model; structured Touba ranges from 21 to 35. Native matches the highest observed count for each model, including ties with the frameworks. All paired case intervals include zero. These observed counts describe the evaluated tasks; the runtime authority boundary remains fixed. The results connect responsibility contracts, execution rules, and evidence.

</details>


### [23] [From Cosmetic to Structural: A Paired Experiment on Metric-Guided Agentic Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33343)
*Nadim Hagatulah,Markus Borg,Emma Söderberg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提供静态分析反馈（代码气味和CodeHealth评分）可以引导AI代理从浅层的 Cosmetic修改转向结构性重构，有效提高修复技术债务的成功率，但代价是需要更多的Token和更多的交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI代码代理的职责不断扩展，但现有研究表明它们主要执行表面的一致性修改，而无法进行必要的结构性重构以解决技术债务。研究旨在通过提供静态分析反馈来弥补这一差距。

Method: 研究团队通过CodeScene MCP服务器向开源的gptme代理提供代码气味报告和CodeHealth评分（1‑10分）。他们进行了一项配对实验，比较了24,620份C++、Java和Python单文件竞争编程解决方案的受指导和非受指导重构操作。

Result: 指导操作将代理从外观修改转向结构性操作，将有效重构率提高了21‑26个百分点。  优势取决于初始代码质量，达到最佳效果的代码健康度得分（CH）范围为4.9‑7.1（因语言而异）；低于这一阈值时，结构性修改的风险更大。  指导运行后的失败模式变为逻辑测试失败，耦合气味增加，令牌消耗增加3‑5⅗倍，交互轮次增加约两倍。

Conclusion: 与人类开发者一样，向AI团队提供静态分析反馈可以促进结构性TD缓解，从而提高其有效性，尽管需要更高的Token开销和更多的交互轮次才能实现这一目标。

Abstract: The role of coding agents is expanding beyond writing new code toward maintaining existing systems as AI teammates. Yet recent studies show that agent refactorings favor superficial, consistency-oriented edits over structural transformations needed to resolve Technical Debt (TD). We investigate if explicit static-analysis feedback closes this gap. Through the CodeScene MCP server, we give an open-source agent (gptme) access to code smell reports and CodeHealth (CH) scores (1--10, higher is better), and compare guided against unguided refactorings in a paired experiment covering $24{,}620$ single-file competitive programming solutions in C++, Java, and Python. We find that guidance shifts the agent from performing cosmetic edits toward structural operations, raising the rate of effective refactorings by $21$--$26$ percentage points. The benefit depends on initial code quality, with language-dependent break-even points at CH $4.9$--$7.1$, below where the structural transformations pushed by guidance increasingly risk regression, indicating where the agent needs more support to refactor. Guided runs also shift the failure profile toward logical test failures, introduce new coupling smells, and consume $\sim$$3$--$5\times$ more tokens over $\sim$$2\times$ as many interaction turns. Our results indicate that AI teammates can benefit from similar static-analysis feedback that human developers already use, enabling structural transformations toward more effective TD remediation, at a higher token cost and more interaction turns.

</details>


### [24] [EVM-Anchored Provenance for AI-Assisted Code Evolution: A Smoke-Scale Deployment Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33350)
*Einar César Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: A smoke-scale deployment of an EVM-anchored provenance framework for AI-assisted code generation streams is described, anchored by a Solidity contract on Base Sepolia with IPFS pinning and a rebuildable SQLite mirror; key properties (existence, ordering, non-equivocation) are defined as attested, while truthfulness/fabrication-prevention is not — a gap that orthogonal supply-chain controls must close. Empirically, the median commit costs ~103K gas, ~$0.002-0.004, at ~540 ms (p50) latency for create operations; the live deployment shows 14 verifiable events, a real operational throughput (98,977 gas committed), and clear descriptive limitations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To anchor AI-assisted code generation state streams on-chain as immutable proofs of transformation (existence, ordering, non-equivocation), and to do so efficiently enough for production use.

Method: The architecture has two parts: (1) codebase-state-manager, an open-source MCP server (MIT v0.2.1) that exposes codebase state, transition, and reward operations to MCP-compatible assistants; (2) CodebaseStateRegistry, a Solidity contract deployed on Base Sepolia emitting per-state commitment events. Payloads pinned to IPFS, indexed through a rebuildable SQLite mirror resynchronized by replaying contract events. The paper provides explicit property-by-property comparison against OpenTimestamps, Sigstore Rekor, Centrifuge AnchorRegistry, in-toto/SLSA, Radicle, and Lalli et al.

Result: Median commit cost: 103,282 gas (~US$0.0018-0.0035 per commit; US$17.9-34.8 per 10,000 states). Simulator latency for create operation: 539.8 ms (p50) and 725.0 ms (p95). Live deployment verified 14 StateCommitted events on-chain. Last consumed 98,977 gas at 1 gwei (≈US$0.30). Emphasizes no superlative claim against baselines, single author run only, no testnet latency reported.

Conclusion: The system delivers a practical, gas-conservative EVM provenance framework with demonstrable throughput on a local simulator, clearly defining its attestation boundaries (existence, ordering, non-equivocation) while flagging the unaddressed truthfulness gap as requiring orthogonal supply-chain controls. A receipts-trie consumption pattern and BountyEscrow use case are proposed but not implemented.

Abstract: We report a smoke-scale deployment of an EVM-anchored provenance framework for the state stream produced by AI-assisted code generation. The system comprises codebase-state-manager, an open-source Model Context Protocol (MCP) server (MIT, v0.2.1) that exposes codebase state, transition, and reward operations to any MCP-compatible assistant, and CodebaseStateRegistry, a Solidity contract deployed on Base Sepolia that emits per-state commitment events. Payloads are pinned to the InterPlanetary File System (IPFS) and indexed through a rebuildable SQLite mirror that resynchronizes by replaying contract events. We delimit precisely what anchoring does and does not attest (existence, ordering, and non-equivocation by the committer key; never truthfulness or fabrication-prevention) and outline the orthogonal supply-chain controls required to close that gap. Under an author-produced synthetic workload on a local Anvil simulator, the median commit costs 103,282 gas, modeled at US$0.0018-0.0035 in rollup fees per commit (US$17.9-34.8 per 10,000 states), and simulator latency for the create operation is 539.8 ms (p50) and 725.0 ms (p95) under loopback storage and instant mining. The live deployment holds 14 StateCommitted events verifiable on-chain; the last consumed 98,977 gas at 1 gwei (about US$0.30). All empirical figures are descriptive quantiles from a single author run; no testnet latency is reported, and no claim of superiority is made against any baseline. A receipts-trie consumption mechanism, a BountyEscrow use case, and a FAIR-aligned dataset publication pattern are specified as outlook and are not implemented. A property-by-property comparison against OpenTimestamps, Sigstore Rekor, Centrifuge AnchorRegistry, in-toto/SLSA, Radicle, and Lalli et al. places this work relative to the surrounding provenance landscape.

</details>


### [25] [OneCodeReviewBench: Typed Decisions for Code Review and Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33389)
*Mohamad Mohebifar,Alex Bit*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OneCodeReviewBench提出了1968个跨12种编程语言和YAML的合成代码审查案例，用于评估模型查找功能Bug、内存安全错误、安全弱点和性能问题的能力。十种模型-适配器系统在同一案例和契约上比较，OpenAI Decisions在布尔任务上达81.3%平衡准确率，Kev-27B在选择任务上达76.8%准确率，MAE 0.367。排除两个契约不完整的家族后，上述领导者仍保持稳定。版本化 artifact 可重现分析并保留85个与工程师答案匹配的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码审查需要跨语言和软件栈检测功能Bug、内存安全错误、安全弱点和性能问题。现有工具仅能表达有界部分的工作，缺乏一个系统的基准来统一评估模型在不同语言和契约下的表现。

Method: 构建了1968个显式契约的合成案例，涵盖运行时假设、源范围和答案标准。案例包含布尔、分类和有序三类答案，并提供单独的证据记录支持参考审查。单人审查150个案例，两位工程师审查其中的100个，用于后续比对和争议解析。

Result: OpenAI Decisions达成81.3%布尔平衡准确率；Kev-27B达成76.8%选择准确率和0.367的均方绝对误差。排除两个契约不完整的家族后，两者仍为领导者。针对586个原始符号搜索案例和40个有界Python导入解析器案例，后者在其支持的范围内全部匹配参考。版本化 artifact 重现了分析并保留了85个与工程师答案匹配的案例。

Conclusion: 在特定任务上，模型表现突出但受契约完整性影响显著。剩余的审查者 disagreement 和修复输入需要进一步解决。规模化的仓库转移和匹配的延迟/成本评估仍为 future work。该基准和 artifact 为未来的模型评估和基础设施研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Code review requires finding functional bugs, memory-safety errors, security weaknesses, and performance problems across languages and software stacks. Tools can express bounded parts of this work as decisions about supplied code. We present OneCodeReviewBench, 1,968 synthetic cases across 12 programming languages and YAML, with Boolean, categorical, and ordered answers. Explicit contracts specify runtime assumptions, source scope, and answer criteria; separate evidence records support reference review. One author reviewed 150 cases; two additional engineers reviewed 100 of those cases. We compare ten model--adapter systems on the same cases and contracts: OpenAI Decisions reaches 81.3% Boolean balanced accuracy; Kev-27B reaches 76.8% Choice accuracy and Score mean absolute error of 0.367. Excluding two families with incomplete contracts preserves these leaders. Additional comparisons cover 586 cases with raw symbol search and 40 with a bounded Python import resolver; the resolver matches all 40 references within its supported profile. A versioned artifact reproduces these analyses and retains the 85 cases with matching engineer answers; the reported review subset excludes two incomplete-contract cases. Remaining reviewer disagreements and repaired inputs require resolution; repository-scale transfer and matched latency/cost evaluation remain future work.

</details>


### [26] [Can Agentic Code Review Catch Vulnerabilities Before They Are Merged?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33509)
*Yihan Yang,Chenyuan Yang,Zijie Zhao,Anh Nguyen Hoang,Ziqi Zhang,Weidong Wang,Zhiheng Lyu,Lingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为SecPRBench的新基准，用于评估LLM代理在真实世界bug引入提交上的安全代码审查能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多基于后续修复而非真实bug引入提交，无法准确评估安全审查效果。

Method: 构建自动化代理管道采集、构建并验证来自9个关键系统（如Firefox、V8、OpenSSL）的265个真实bug引入提交，并采用LLM-as-judge进行评估。

Result: 8个前沿模型的召回率为32.5%-78.1%，Grok 4.6与GPT-6 sol表现最佳；68%的漏报为文件正确但机制判断错误。

Conclusion: SecPRBench填补了真实bug引入提交评估的空缺，有助于更准确衡量LLM安全代码审查能力。

Abstract: LLM agents have been increasingly adopted for security-focused code review, but existing benchmarks mostly derive the reviewed changes from their later fixes instead of the real-world bug-introducing commits. In this paper, we present SecPRBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating agentic security review on validated real-world code changes. More specifically, we have built an automated agentic pipeline to autonomously harvest, build, and validate massive real-world bug-introducing commits for popular software systems. To ensure precise evaluation, we also adopt LLM-as-a-judge to credit the findings matching ground-truth vulnerabilities. To date, we have collected 265 tasks from nine critical systems, including Firefox, V8, and OpenSSL with millions of lines of code. Across eight frontier models, recall ranges from 32.5% to 78.1%, with Grok 4.6 and GPT-6 sol ranking at the top. Our study also shows that most misses are not simply oversights: in 68% of them, the agent reports on a file that holds the flaw but argues for a different mechanism. Security-focused harnesses also behave differently: OpenAI's Codex Security lowers recall by 8.3 points, mostly by abstaining, while Anthropic's Defending Code harness raises it by 7.7 points, at a higher cost.

</details>


### [27] [Developers as Managers of AI Agents: A Workplace Shadowing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33588)
*Yi-Hung Chou,June Hyung Kim,Vasisht Kartik,Victoria Jackson,Tobias Schimmer,Thomas Zimmermann,James A. Jones*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究观察了 11 名专业开发人员在交互式 AI 助手时的日常实践，发现他们将约 33% 的时间用于编写提示和检查 AI 响应，任务切换率极高。作者提出了三种模式：“监督者”(监核型工作)、“借治者”(依访交话城法工作)、“学生者”(使用并互书到用类)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图警在一个 “自动化 AI的设念中设念引导、序合和检查于日常职业开发身中的學已工作。

Method: 对 11 个老开发身进行了班影，也带人得个，对 255 个提交稿穆进行结构并一致付行话影，一常话进行床记一次远回试(远时时分：4.6 小时).

Result: 对象表现了开发身中待 AI时的三种象表：“监核者”（盒比城属牐4），“借治者”（对话属牐8），“学生者”（使用说烂世界)。设念中的提交属牑4%的个时遇大文档，，跨次偏在 0 到 101.6 次，象表或漫身序，患业属牐9% 的个时属于从 AI试知，如还从工作中的农业属牑2% 的个时属于提交，，开发身在商业个序数据和通业提交中，等原无后对 AI 水流的内容和结构，负业最结序也序合也允听连接通业，负业引导中的内容和完成度.

Conclusion: 本现稿穆记于全局个序合在工作流的提起，话进行，路径上记于“监核者”（工作试试)，“借治者”（话属牐8），“学生者”（用并结构理解)，象表提起了主要中大数的异步牐4、跨次偏高，象表中从 AI 获取教师中的技能信息，，主要要求属于工作进行中，工作在胆量中属于 AI 任务和工具，，使用应用上“声语控制，下旁等”和包含，应该记于“试应班影”和“对 AI 水流的害流：”证举个序为其他象譓和新化提交时可能的武師内容和结成.“

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, developers increasingly direct, coordinate, and check their work. Yet little is known about these practices in everyday professional development. We shadowed 11 professional developers across approximately 15 hours of work, coded their activities and task transitions, analyzed 255 submitted prompts, and conducted 4.6 hours of follow-up interviews.
  Developers spent an average of 33.4% of observed time formulating prompts or checking AI responses, with task-switching rates ranging from 0 to 101.6 switches per hour. Beyond producing artifacts, prompts supported understanding, requirements clarification, agent configuration, and coordination. We identified three recurring patterns: \textit{Supervisor}, involving direct artifact inspection; \textit{Delegator}, involving dialogue-based coordination and delegated implementation and checking; and \textit{Learner}, involving agents primarily to build understanding. Developers sought technical information from agents and organizational context, authority, and alignment from colleagues. Our findings suggest future studies of how developers manage attention across concurrent agent tasks and tools that support verification, coordination, and learning.

</details>


### [28] [Build or Adopt? Custom-Built vs. General-Purpose LLM Agent Harnesses for Automotive GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33636)
*Arsham Gholamzadeh Khoee,Ruyu Fu,Jiaqi Wei,Ruikun Deng,Robert Feldt,Dhasarathy Parthasarathy,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究对比了定制与通用LLM代理框架在车载信息娱乐系统GUI测试中的表现，发现通用框架虽推理成本较高，但总成本更低且在开放任务中恢复能力更强，采用通用框架将维护重心转移至工具和指令，但测试人员仍需检查代理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织面临是否继续开发内部LLM代理框架或采用现有通用框架的决策，本研究在Volvo Group的Android车载信息娱乐系统GUI测试场景下探讨这一决策。

Method: 对比生产环境的定制框架与两个通用框架，保持通过MCP访问的工具和模型一致，使用最多四个模型在Volvo的IVI测试用例和公开应用（Google Maps, Booking.com）的开放任务上评估，并采访十位从业者（开发人员、产品负责人、测试人员）。

Result: 在分步IVI测试中，通用框架成功率与定制框架相当但运行间差异更大；单任务推理成本高2.6-10.7倍，但节省的工程工作量使夜ly回归套件总月成本降低3-66%；在开放任务中通用框架成功更多，主要因能更好地从错误动作恢复；访谈显示外部进步可能使内部框架变成技术债，采用通用框架将维护从代理循环转移到工具和指令，但团队对模型输入的控制减少；无论框架如何，测试人员都希望检查代理动作并确保遵循规定测试场景。

Conclusion: 采用通用LLM代理框架可降低总体成本并提升开放任务表现，但引入了运行时变异性和对模型输入控制的减少；组织需权衡工程维护负担与控制需求，同时测试人员对代理行为的可审查性仍是核心需求。

Abstract: As general-purpose LLM agent harnesses mature, organizations must decide whether to continue developing in-house systems or adopt existing alternatives. We study this decision at Volvo Group for GUI testing of Android-based in-vehicle infotainment (IVI) systems. We compare a production, custom-built harness with two general-purpose harnesses, Claude Code and OpenCode, keeping the tools, accessed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and the models the same. We evaluate the harnesses with up to four models on Volvo's IVI test cases and on open-ended tasks in public apps (Google Maps, Booking.com), and interview ten practitioners: developers, the product owner, and testers. On Volvo's stepwise IVI tests, general-purpose harnesses match the custom-built harness in success but vary more across runs. Their inference cost per task is 2.6-10.7 times higher, yet the engineering effort they save outweighs this: for a nightly IVI regression suite, total monthly cost, including engineering effort and inference, is 3-66% lower. On open-ended public-app tasks, they succeed more often, mainly because they recover better from incorrect actions. Interviews suggest that external advances can turn an in-house harness into technical debt even without deterioration. Adopting a general-purpose harness shifts maintenance from the agent loop to tools and instructions, but gives teams less control over what the model receives. Regardless of the harness, testers want to inspect the agent's actions and ensure that it follows the prescribed test scenario.

</details>


### [29] [Covered Is Not Checked: Which Branch Outcomes Would Your Tests Notice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33708)
*Md Nafiu Rahman,Cemil Canbaz,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Veritas 是一种无切片方法，直接测试覆盖的行为是否被检查，通过强制执行相反结果并观察测试失败，揭示了许多覆盖结果未被检查，且切片方法存在漏报和误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码覆盖率只反映测试执行了哪些代码，不能保证故障检测能力。基于动态切片的检查覆盖率虽然解决了这个问题，但切片开销大，且 Java 切片工具难以处理现代语言特性和复杂程序，需要更实用的方法。

Method: Veritas 以对象分支覆盖（OBC）为目标，对于每个被覆盖的对象分支结果，强制执行相反结果，仅当该变更被执行、原本通过的测试失败、且失败可归因于该结果时，才认为该结果被检查。无需动态依赖分析。

Result: 在 11 个开源 Java 项目（含 51,382 个对象分支结果）上评估：18% 的覆盖结果可改变而不导致测试失败；存活变异体中约一半从未被测试输入激活，其余虽被激活但未被检测。Veritas 发现 4,763 个切片方法未记为检测但测试实际检测到的案例；19% 被切片记为检测的案例实际改变后无失败。削弱断言会扩大差距并降低故障检测。弱检查结果控制的代码位置比已检查结果更可能包含未被发现的独立故障（高出 12.4–54.7 个百分点）。

Conclusion: 直接测试结果提供了超越结构覆盖的可行性证据，能更准确地评估测试预言的有效性。

Abstract: Code coverage reports what tests execute, not whether tests detect faults. Checked coverage addresses this gap by tracing code that influences test oracles through dynamic slicing, but slicing is costly, and Java slicers struggle with modern language features and complex programs. We present Veritas, a slicing-free approach that directly tests whether covered behavior is checked. We target object branch coverage (OBC), a compiled-code structural criterion with fault-detection effectiveness comparable to source-level Modified Condition/Decision Coverage (MC/DC), which is required at the highest assurance level of DO-178C. For each covered object-branch outcome, Veritas forces the opposite outcome and credits checking only when the change is executed, a previously passing test fails, and the failure is attributable to that outcome. Thus, Veritas distinguishes execution from demonstrated fault detection without dynamic dependence analysis.
  We evaluated Veritas on eleven open-source Java projects containing 51,382 object-branch outcomes. We find that 18% of covered outcomes can be changed without failing any test, exposing weaknesses hidden by coverage. Among surviving mutants, about half are never activated by test inputs, while the rest are activated but undetected. Compared with slicing-based checked coverage, Veritas identifies 4,763 cases where tests detect a change that slicing does not credit, while 19% of cases credited by slicing produce no detected failure when changed. Weakening assertions increases this gap and reduces fault detection in every project. Moreover, code locations controlled by weakly checked outcomes are 12.4--54.7 percentage points more likely to have an undetected independent fault than locations controlled by checked outcomes. These findings show that directly testing outcomes provides actionable evidence of oracle effectiveness beyond structural coverage.

</details>


### [30] [Software Engineering LLMs: Why We Need Them and How Software Engineering Helps Build Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33851)
*Amir M. Ebrahimi,Mohammed Mehedi Hasan,Seyed Shayan Daneshvar,Jiahuei Lin,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过软件工程知识聚焦训练，利用RefactorForge管线为92B码码LLM构建验证性重构训练数据，微调后在RefactorBench和SWE-Bench等基准测试上分别提升18.00和7.06个百分点，展现了SE在转化码LLM中的实证价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究软件工程专业知识如何指导大型码模型的训练，塑造其工程能力，并通过工程化手段提升模型在仓库级重构和问题解决等实际 workloads 的性能。

Method: 提出了RefactorForge管线，结合重构知识、SE工具和验证技术，从306个Python仓库生成5,704个可执行任务并保留4,572条验证教师轨迹（72.51M tokens）；采用仅占先前码混合训练数据0.46%的增量监督微调。

Result: 增量微调使RefactorBench性能提升18.00个百分点，SWE-Bench ProMax提升7.06点；在OpenCode下，SWE-Bench Verified提升4.97点，SWE-Bench Pro提升1.23点，改进在其他码代理框架中持续，而greenfield仓库生成结果不一。

Conclusion: 基于工业经验，提出SE转化码LLM的研究议程：将SE知识融入模型训练，应用工程方法进行模型开发与维护，并系统定义模型应习得的工程能力。

Abstract: Software engineering (SE) expertise can shape how coding large language models (LLMs) are trained and which engineering capabilities they acquire. We examine this role through an industrial case study of capability-focused training for a 92B-parameter coding LLM serving more than 100,000 developers. We demonstrate how established SE knowledge can guide the selection of engineering capabilities, the construction of verifiable training data, and the evaluation of model improvements. To target repository-level refactoring, we developed RefactorForge, a pipeline that combines refactoring knowledge, established SE tools, and verification techniques to construct training data. Across 306 Python repositories, RefactorForge generated 5,704 executable tasks and retained 4,572 verified teacher trajectories totaling 72.51 million tokens. Incremental supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories, accounting for 0.46% of the preceding coding mixture's tokens, improved RefactorBench performance by 18.00 percentage points and SWE-Bench ProMax performance by 7.06 points. Under OpenCode, these improvements extended to SWE issue resolution, with gains of 4.97 points on SWE-Bench Verified and 1.23 points on SWE-Bench Pro. Improvements also persisted across other coding-agent harnesses, while greenfield repository generation showed mixed results. We report lessons from constructing capability-focused training data and evaluating its effects across engineering workloads. Building on this industrial experience, we propose a research agenda for expanding SE's role in transforming coding LLMs into SE LLMs: incorporating SE knowledge into model training, applying engineering methods to model development and maintenance, and systematically defining the engineering capabilities these models should acquire.

</details>


### [31] [ArchGuard-APR: Architecture-Constraint-Aware Multi-Agent Program Repair Using Repository Graphs and Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33853)
*Majid Feiz Deilami*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ArchGuard-APR attempts architecture-aware automated program repair but all four completed repair tasks failed: 0 successful repairs under baseline and ArchGuard conditions, with all outputs classified as invalid patches. The study is exploratory and provides no useful inferential basis, though it demonstrates engineering feasibility for composing architecture constraints with a reproducible repair pipeline.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The goal was to evaluate whether architectural constraints can be composed into an automated repair pipeline to improve repair success beyond localized fixes that may violate dependency boundaries or unsafe paths.

Method: ArchGuard-APR integrates deterministic repository analysis, immutable repository graphs, explicit architecture profiles, graph-guided context retrieval, role-based repair orchestration, patch-policy enforcement, isolated execution feedback, provider cost controls, and canonical source-free evidence. An exploratory paired feasibility study was conducted with six planned executions (four completed).

Result: Across two valid paired tasks, both baseline and ArchGuard conditions produced 0/2 successful repairs. All four completed outputs were invalid patches. Total token usage was 38,168 tokens (~USD 0.13) at negligible cost. No effectiveness advantage was observed, and the sample size is too small for inferential claims.

Conclusion: The study fails to demonstrate effectiveness of architecture-aware repair, but highlights patch validity and infrastructure robustness as the principal targets for future work; the engineering feasibility of composing architecture constraints with a reproducible pipeline is established.

Abstract: Repository-level automated program repair must reason about more than a localized failing statement. A candidate change can satisfy a narrow test while violating dependency boundaries, modifying an unsafe path, or relying on context that omits relevant repository structure. ArchGuard-APR is an architecture-aware repair prototype that integrates deterministic repository analysis, immutable repository graphs, explicit architecture profiles, graph-guided context retrieval, role-based repair orchestration, patch-policy enforcement, isolated execution feedback, provider cost controls, and canonical source-free evidence.
  We evaluate the implemented pipeline through an exploratory paired feasibility study, not a confirmatory effectiveness experiment. Six executions were planned; four completed, and two black-4 conditions ended in pre-provider infrastructure attrition. Across two valid paired tasks, baseline and ArchGuard conditions each produced 0/2 successful repairs, and all four completed outputs were classified as invalid patches.
  The completed executions consumed 35,431 input tokens and 2,737 output tokens, for a total of 38,168 tokens, at a total cost of USD 0.1296325. No effectiveness advantage was observed, and the sample provides no useful inferential basis.
