The study instead establishes the engineering feasibility of composing architecture constraints with a reproducible repair pipeline and identifies patch validity and infrastructure robustness as the principal targets for future work.

</details>


### [32] [DRILS: Dependency-based Requirement Change Impact Analysis Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33920)
*Romina Etezadi,Ikram Darif,Lionel Briand,Ramesh S,Parkish Peranandam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DRILS是一个基于LLM的智能体框架，用于需求变更影响分析，结合多智能体架构、RE-RAG和需求依赖图，在三个数据集上达到平均召回率87.1%、精确率76.3%、F1 81%，显著提高精确率并降低成本，并发布了三个标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统变更不可避免，手动识别变更影响因需求量大、自然语言复杂、依赖关系错综而易错耗时；LLM虽有潜力但结合先进技术的效果未充分探索。

Method: 提出DRILS框架：多智能体架构生成候选影响集，LLM-as-a-judge促进多样性选择；两大核心组件：RE-RAG（精心设计的RAG技术提供领域上下文）和需求依赖图（RDG）作为额外知识源。

Result: 在三个手工整理数据集上评估，平均召回率87.1%、精确率76.3%、F1 81%，成本更低；精确率提升显著，分别比最近基线高41.1%、83.4%、149.8%，减少误报和人工验证工作；贡献三个标注CIA数据集。

Conclusion: DRILS通过多智能体、RAG和依赖图有效提升需求变更影响分析性能，尤其在精确率上大幅超越基线，降低人工成本，并发布数据集推动后续研究。

Abstract: Change is inevitable in software systems, where requirements must continuously adapt to evolving stakeholder needs, regulations, and project scopes. Managing such changes is a fundamental software engineering challenge, as manually identifying the impact of a change request, given high requirements volume, Natural Language (NL) complexity, and intricate interdependencies, is error-prone and time-consuming. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising solutions for requirements change impact analysis (CIA), yet their effectiveness when enhanced with advanced techniques remains underexplored. We present DRILS, an LLM-based agentic framework for requirements Change Impact Analysis, employing a multi-agent architecture to generate candidate impact sets, with an LLM-as-a-judge promoting diversity during selection. DRILS features two key components: RE-RAG, a carefully engineered RAG technique providing focused domain context, and a requirement dependency graph (RDG) as an additional knowledge source. Evaluation across three manually curated datasets demonstrates superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average recall of 87.1%, precision of 76.3%, and F1 score of 81% at a lower cost. Precision gains are particularly significant, 41.1%, 83.4%, and 149.8% over the closest baseline per dataset, as higher precision directly reduces false positives and manual validation effort. We also contribute three annotated CIA datasets to support future research.

</details>


### [33] [Why Does Structured Output Fail? An Empirical Study of Prompt-Enforced Pydantic Schemas Across Nine LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33949)
*Amit Verma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种细粒度的模式一致性失败分类体系和确定性分类器，用于分析宿主语言模型生成的结构化输出失败模式。通过分析3,997次生成尝试，发现相同的有效率可能掩盖不同的失败模式，Markdown格式的代码块是最常见的失败类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的管道可靠性指标往往只使用单一的有效率来总结，无法揭示为什么输出失败以及哪些干预措施有帮助。需要更细粒度的分析来理解失败模式和改进策略。

Method: 研究者构建了失败分类体系和分类器，将模型响应和Pydantic验证错误进行分类。应用于9种OpenAI和Anthropic模型/API配置，使用HotpotQA和FinanceBench数据集进行3,997次生成尝试分析。

Result: 主要发现包括：Markdown代码块是最常见的失败类型（681个，仅48%在移除代码块后通过验证），失败模式随约束和任务变化；当抑制代码块时，失败向长度违规转移；聚合有效率掩盖了失败组成的变化。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了更细粒度的失败分析的重要性，并提出了四项工程实践建议，包括验证器、模式、重试和评估设计。分类器、完成和标注数据已公开。

Abstract: Pipelines that require structured outputs from hosted language models often specify a Pydantic schema in the prompt, validate the response, and retry when validation fails. Although native structured-output modes provide an alternative, application-side validation remains important for enforcing custom constraints. Yet pipeline reliability is often summarized by a single validity rate, which obscures why outputs fail and which interventions may help. We introduce a fine-grained taxonomy of schema-conformance failures and a deterministic classifier that assigns failure categories from model responses and Pydantic validation errors. We apply this framework to 3,997 generation attempts across nine OpenAI and Anthropic model/API configurations, using questions from HotpotQA and FinanceBench. Our results show that similar validity rates can mask different failure patterns, and that dominant failure categories vary with schema constraints and task. Markdown-fenced JSON is the most common failure: among 681 fenced failures, only 48.0% pass validation after fence removal, while the remainder conceal additional violations, predominantly field-length violations. In an exploratory prompt ablation, variants that suppress fences appear to shift failures toward length violations, but the shift is consistent with exposing violations that were already present, illustrating how aggregate validity rates can obscure changes in failure composition. We derive four engineering practices for validator, schema, retry and evaluation design, three supported by our data and one, on retry feedback, proposed but not tested, and release the classifier, completions, and annotations.

</details>


### [34] [How Do Small Coding Agents Use Code Graphs and Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33958)
*Saurav Bellam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在 SWE-bench Verified 上用小型开放权重模型检验“代码仓库结构需由嵌入式搜索和代码图工具补全”的前提，通过消融定位工具集并按代码图跳跃深度分层，发现结构化工具对小模型帮助明显但随规模增大而递减，且模型对仓库的认知主要是一跳且偏词法，难以进行多跳图推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证代理赋予嵌入检索和代码图工具的假设是否成立：模型缺乏仓库结构，工具应提供定位所需结构信息，从而提升 issue 到金补丁代码的定位与修复能力。

Method: 在深度平衡的 160 项任务及全部 500 项 SWE-bench Verified 任务上，消融定位代理工具集（仅 grep；+嵌入式检索；+图工具；全部）。使用十款模型：Qwen3 六个规模 0.6B-32B，各五次解码，FP8 对照，三款 Gemma 4 规模，共 79k 次评测。按伴随论文定义的决定性跳跃深度分层，并用线性探针在锚点内、按度分层协议下探测直接调用边是否可从权重和对话中解码。

Result: 结构化工具使 Qwen3-8B 和 Gemma 4 E4B 的仅 grep 准确率翻倍；收益在 1.7-4B 峰值约 17-21 点，32B 降至 4-5 点（每翻倍参数比值约 0.77-0.82）。8B 以上约一半收益来自提交形式而非定位目标；14B 到 32B 的下降源于等 grep-only 准确率下的工具采用率下降；同一 4B 检查点收益随版本而异。每多一跳，正确定位比率乘以 0.50-0.67，所有规模和工具集均未扁平化该梯度。图工具收益主要体现在跳 0 的名称查找和跳 1 的一次展开，只有 12-14% 评测中代理会链式展开。固定修复模型从这些定位中解决 160 项中的 6-13 项（oracle 26）。线性探针直接边 AUC 0.74（对话内 0.61-0.66），低于词法基线 0.99，两跳边 0.57。

Conclusion: 小型代理在权重和使用中对仓库主要保留一跳、以词法为主的图景，嵌入与图工具不能根本改变多跳定位困难；工具收益随模型增大而衰减，且很大程度上受提交格式和工具采用率影响，而非真正的跨跳代码图推理。

Abstract: Coding agents are given embedding search and code-graph tools on the premise that a repository's structure is something the model lacks and the tool supplies. We test that premise on SWE-bench Verified with small open-weight models, stratifying tasks by a deterministic hop depth (the shortest code-graph path from code the issue mentions to code the gold patch changes) that a companion paper defines and validates. First, we ablate a localization agent's tool set (grep only; +embedding search; +graph tools; all) on a depth-balanced 160-task split and on all 500 tasks for ten models (six Qwen3 sizes from 0.6B to 32B with five decodings each, an FP8 control, three Gemma 4 sizes; 79k episodes). Structured tools double grep-only accuracy for Qwen3-8B and Gemma 4 E4B; their gain peaks at 17-21 points at 1.7-4B and falls to 4-5 points at 32B (odds ratio 0.77-0.82 per doubling of parameters). From 8B up, about half of that gain is the form of the submission rather than finding the target; the fall from 14B to 32B is a fall in tool uptake at equal grep-only accuracy; and the same 4B size gains or does not gain from a tool depending on the checkpoint. Each extra hop multiplies the odds of correct localization by 0.50-0.67 at every size, and no tool set flattens that gradient: traces show the graph tools' gain is name lookup at hop 0 and one expansion at hop 1, and agents chain expansions in only 12-14% of episodes. A fixed repair model resolves 6-13 of 160 tasks from these localizations (oracle 26). Second, linear probes find direct call edges decodable under a within-anchor, degree-stratified protocol (AUC 0.74; 0.61-0.66 inside agent conversations), but below a lexical baseline (0.99), with two-hop edges at 0.57. Small agents carry a one-hop, largely lexical picture of their repository, in weights and in use. We release the agent, probes, logs and traces.

</details>


### [35] [Securing Agent-Generated Code with Its Own Development History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34098)
*Prateek Kumar Rajput,Shahzeb Khan,El-Hacen Diallo,Yewei Song,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TRACER is a workflow that leverages agent development history to improve code review for security vulnerabilities. It tracks changes at the symbol level, uses agent rationale claims as verification targets, and accepts repairs only after assessment and functional validation. Symbol-level tracking successfully identifies 94.6% of vulnerable symbols in review scope versus 13.4% with scanners alone. Two configurations tested: one includes agent rationale, the other also adds AST history—no improvement in verified repairs. A funnel analysis reveals where remaining candidates are lost. In structural-biology software testing, repair memory plus boundary check achieves 9/10 task success without reintroducing vulnerabilities, while functional feedback alone gets 4 successes and 3 failures with vulnerability reintroduction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Developers need effective methods to catch missing security guards in agent-generated code changes. Static analysis alone misses many issues; leveraging agent history could provide evidence for safer reviews.

Method: TRACER workflow using development history (agent trajectory with assumptions and structural differencing), symbol-level tracking, two investigator models (one with agent rationale claims, one adding AST history), funnel analysis, executable constraint checks for subsequent changes, and functional validation gates. Experiments on SusVibes benchmark and structural-biology software tasks.

Result: Symbol-level tracking finds vulnerable symbols in scope 94.6% vs 13.4% for scanners. Agent rationale configuration yields maximum verified repairs with functionality preserved. Adding AST history does not increase verified repairs. Funnel analysis identifies where candidates are lost. Repair memory + boundary check achieves 9/10 success in structural biology tasks without reintroducing vulnerabilities; functional feedback alone yields 4/10 success and reintroduces vulnerability in 3 cases.

Conclusion: TRACER demonstrates that leveraging agent development history via symbol-level tracking significantly improves vulnerability detection rate compared to static analysis, and that combining repair memory with boundary constraints effectively prevents vulnerability reintroduction during subsequent feature work.

Abstract: Pull requests written by coding agents can pass a project's test suite while omitting a guard or a validation step. To find the missing condition, a reviewer currently relies on static analysis of the final code. An agent-generated change, however, is accompanied by a development history that a human commit does not provide. The agent's trajectory records each edit together with the agent's stated assumptions, and structural differencing shows how each changed function evolved. We present TRACER, a workflow that uses this development history as evidence for security review. TRACER tracks changes at the symbol level, treats the agent's recorded rationale as claims to be verified against the current code, accepts a repair only after assessment and functional validation, and attaches to each accepted repair an executable check that later changes must satisfy. On the SusVibes benchmark, symbol-level tracking places the vulnerable symbol in the review scope for 94.6% of agent patches, compared with 13.4% for the primary scanner warning. With two investigator models, the configuration that includes the agent's rationale yields the largest number of verified security repairs while preserving functionality, and adding AST history to it does not increase that number. A funnel analysis identifies where the remaining candidates are lost. In a study of structural-biology software, the combination of repair memory and a boundary check allows a subsequent feature request to succeed in nine of ten tasks without reintroducing the repaired vulnerability, whereas functional feedback alone succeeds in four and reintroduces the vulnerability in three.

</details>


### [36] [Bug Severity Classification with Fine-Tuned Open LLMs: A Paired Comparison with TF-IDF and CodeBERT Baselines on Eclipse Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34127)
*Asset Kanatov,Talgar Bayan,Askar Boranbayev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了可在笔记本电脑上运行的开源轻量级大型语言模型（LLM）在Eclipse Bug严重程度自动预测任务上的表现，并与TF-IDF和CodeBERT等传统方法进行了对比。结果表明，微调后的LLM（如Llama 3）性能可与微调的CodeBERT相媲美，并优于TF-IDF分类器，但零样本LLM表现较差。此外，LLM对训练数据中的重复样本具有更强的鲁棒性。研究团队发布了完整的实验 artifacts 和本地部署工具 CyberTriage。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源大语言模型（LLM）体积不断缩小、可在普通笔记本电脑上运行，它们成为了自动进行Bug严重程度预测（如将Eclipse Bug报告分类为Critical、Major、Minor、Trivial四级）的潜在候选方案。研究旨在量化这些轻量级LLM相较于更廉价、高效的TF-IDF分类器和专用代码模型（CodeBERT）的实际性能增益，并探究零样本与微调设置的效果差异及数据重复对模型的影响。

Method: 研究从361,006份公开的Eclipse Bug报告中抽取了20,000条类别平衡样本作为训练集，并预留2,000份作为测试集。实验对比了9种模型类型的11种配置，包括6种TF-IDF分类器、微调的CodeBERT编码器，以及Llama 3 8B和Qwen 2.5 7B（分别进行零样本推理和QLoRA微调）。所有模型均在同一测试集上评估，采用macro-F1、bootstrap置信区间和McNemar检验。此外，针对数据集中存在的约8%重复报告，研究也评估了去除重复对各模型性能的影响。

Result: 1. 零样本LLM的macro-F1（0.305和0.321）低于所有TF-IDF模型（0.430-0.489）；2. 微调后的LLM性能提升至0.526和0.485，但由于运行环境差异，该提升不能完全归功于微调；3. 微调后的Llama 3（0.518）与CodeBERT（0.526）无统计显著差异，而微调后的Qwen 2.5表现较弱；4. 类别级分析显示，Llama 3对严重（Critical）报告的识别率为36%，Qwen 2.5为69%，但对轻微（Minor）报告的识别率仅为21%；5. 去除重复数据使TF-IDF得分降低高达0.031，但对LLM几乎没有影响。

Conclusion: 微调后的轻量级开源LLM在Bug严重程度预测任务上能达到与专用代码模型（CodeBERT）相当的水平，显著优于传统TF-IDF方法，尽管其零样本表现不佳。同时，LLM对数据重复具有更强的鲁棒性。为了推动研究和实际应用，团队公开了数据拆分、预测结果、分析脚本，并提供了本地分类服务工具 CyberTriage。

Abstract: Open large language models (LLMs) small enough to run on a laptop are an obvious candidate for automatic bug severity prediction. We measure what they add over cheaper classifiers on one task: assigning one of four Bugzilla severity levels (Critical, Major, Minor, Trivial) to Eclipse bug reports. From a public dump of 361,006 unique Eclipse reports we draw a class-balanced sample of 20,000 rows and compare eleven configurations of nine model types: six TF-IDF classifiers, a fine-tuned CodeBERT encoder, and Llama 3 8B and Qwen 2.5 7B, each run zero-shot and after QLoRA fine-tuning. All eleven are scored on the same 2,000 held-out reports, with bootstrap intervals and McNemar tests. The zero-shot runs reach macro-F1 0.305 and 0.321, below every TF-IDF model (0.430-0.489). The fine-tuned runs reach 0.526 and 0.485, but the zero-shot and fine-tuned setups also differ in checkpoint, serving runtime and decoding, so this gap cannot be credited to fine-tuning alone. Fine-tuned Llama 3 shows no detectable difference from CodeBERT (0.518; difference +0.007, 95% CI -0.017 to +0.032), and fine-tuned Qwen 2.5 is below it on macro-F1. The models differ more by class than in aggregate: fine-tuned Llama 3 finds 36% of Critical reports, Qwen 2.5 finds 69% but only 21% of Minor ones. The dump repeats 60,000 rows, and 8% of the test reports duplicate a training report; removing them lowers TF-IDF scores by up to 0.031 and leaves the LLMs almost unchanged. We release split identifiers, per-report predictions and the analysis script at https://github.com/tbayan/bug_finetuning_llm_artifacts, together with CyberTriage, a tool that serves the fine-tuned classifier locally.

</details>


### [37] [SpeC2Rust: Specification-Driven Repository-Level C-to-Rust Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34141)
*Junjie Yao,Xiongzheng Guan,Jia Feng,Yujia Chen,Hongliang Tian,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpeC2Rust是三阶段管道，用于将C仓库转换为安全的Rust代码，分为项目意图建模、生成式规范指导和分层反馈修复三个阶段。它结构化地提取并表达存储库的语义意图，利用实时符号注册表保持跨文件的一致性，并在编译和测试驱动的修正过程中高效地进行诊断修复。评估结果显示，它成功地将八个C存储库转换为编译通过原始端到端测试的Rust代码，且没有原始指针声明、解引用或不安全代码。同时，与基于LLM的基线相比，它将翻译成本降低了66.1%，输入令牌使用量减少了84.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统C代码库是系统软件和性能关键型基础设施的核心，但它们缺乏内存安全性，导致了安全和维护风险。现有的C到Rust的翻译系统（包括基于LLM的翻译器）在自动迁移方面取得了进展，但仍然难以恢复存储库级别的意图，避免原始指针和不安全构造，维护跨文件接口，并高效地修复编译和验证故障。需要一个规范驱动的方法，以实现大规模、安全的C到Rust迁移。

Method: SpeC2Rust包含三个阶段：(1) 项目意图建模：将C仓库分解为语义上连贯的模块，并生成持久的仓库和模块级规范；(2) 规范引导生成：使用实时符号注册表和规范来翻译每个Rust模块，以保持跨文件一致性；(3) 分层反馈修复：批量执行修复，并仅注入与失败相关的诊断、规范上下文和源代码证据，以实现高效的编译和测试驱动的修正。

Result: 在对八个仓库级别的C项目进行评估后，SpeC2Rust生成了编译通过并通过原始端到端测试的Rust项目，且没有原始指针声明、解引用或不安全代码。与基于LLM的基线相比，它将翻译成本降低了66.1%，输入令牌使用量减少了84.7%。

Conclusion: SpeC2Rust为安全C到Rust的迁移提供了一种可扩展的规范驱动方法，能够生成高质量的代码，避免不安全模式，并显著降低翻译成本和令牌使用量，有助于减轻传统代码库的安全和维护负担。

Abstract: Legacy C codebases remain central to system software and performance-critical infrastructure, but their weak memory safety imposes security and maintenance risks. Recent C-to-Rust translation systems, including LLM-based translators, make automated migration increasingly feasible, yet they still struggle to recover repository-level intent, avoid raw pointers and unsafe constructs, maintain cross-file interfaces, and repair compilation and validation failures efficiently. We propose SpeC2Rust, a specification-driven pipeline for repository-level C-to-Rust translation. It has three stages: (1) Project-Intent Structuring, which decomposes a C repository into semantically coherent modules and generates persistent repository- and module-level specifications; (2) Spec-Guided Generation, which translates each Rust module against these specifications with a live symbol registry to preserve cross-file consistency; and (3) Layered-Feedback Repair, which batches repairs and injects only failure-relevant diagnostics, specification context, and source evidence for efficient compilation and test-driven correction. We evaluate SpeC2Rust on eight repository-level C projects. It generates Rust projects that compile and pass the original end-to-end tests, with no raw-pointer declarations, raw-pointer dereferences, or unsafe lines. On projects shared with an LLM-based baseline, SpeC2Rust reduces cost by 66.1% and input-token usage by 84.7%.

</details>


### [38] [The Policies of Programming Language Ecosystems: A "Qualitative" Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34187)
*Jukka Ruohonen,Rahime Yilmaz,Krzysztof Sierszecki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文考察了编程语言生态系统（PLE）的政策，发现各生态系统的政策普遍呈现分歧而非趋同，但在恶意软件、知识产权和内容移除等方面存在共识；细粒度类别如包大小限制、指标操纵、拼写错误抢注和二进制上传则表现出明显差异，填补了现有 PLE 研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLE（如 CRAN）极大加速了软件复用，但也增加了软件供应链风险；与此同时，热门 PLE 的政策还涉及非法内容、内容审核、骚扰和性内容等非网络安全问题，现有研究对这些政策缺乏系统考察。

Method: 三位作者对 8 个流行 PLE 政策中的 38 个不同政策类别进行编码，作者间达成中等一致度，并基于此开展定性分析。

Result: 政策总体上呈现分歧而非趋同；关于恶意软件、知识产权和内容移除的政策类别被普遍认可；分歧主要出现在更细粒度的类别，如包大小限制、指标操纵、拼写错误抢注和二进制上传等。

Conclusion: 这些相关的定性结果补充了现有 PLE 研究中的显著空白，为理解和比较不同编程语言生态系统的治理政策提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Programming language ecosystems (PLEs), such as CRAN for R, have greatly accelerated software reuse. At the same time, they have increased software supply-chain risks and incidents. However, the problems are not limited to cyber security; everything from illegal content and content moderation to harassment and sexual content are mentioned in many policies of popular PLEs. The paper examines these policies. According to a moderate agreement between the three authors on 38 distinct policy categories and eight PLE policies, the policies generally exhibit divergence rather than convergence. However, some policy categories, such as those about malware, intellectual property, and content removal, are universally recognized. The divergence is thus seen in more fine-grained policy categories, such as those related to package size limits, manipulation of metrics, typo-squatting, and binary uploads. These and other related qualitative results patch a notable gap in existing PLE research.

</details>


### [39] [Honesty Testing of LLMs and AI Agents: A Vision for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34239)
*Ying Xiao,Xinjian Wu,Lukas Twist,Yaoqi Guo,Yepang Liu,Zhenpeng Chen,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出将 LLM 和 AI 代理的诚实测试作为软件工程新研究领域，旨在将 faithful reporting 设为可指定、可测试的软件质量要求，并定义 honesty bugs 作为行为缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 自主性增强使得 faithful reporting 对维持人类控制至关重要，现有报告中冲突于系统自身判断的声明可能掩盖失败、延误干预并导致严重危害。

Method: 定义 honesty bugs 为 LLM/代理在被要求诚实沟通时，作出与自身判断冲突的明确声明的行为缺陷。提出涵盖 LLM 级别受控输入测试与 agent 级别完整系统测试的框架，并将其连接至测试工作流与 oracle 证据。

Result: 框架贯穿 LLM 与 agent 两个层级，提供测试工作流与 oracle 证据构建方法，旨在在保证任务正确性的同时支持有效人类监督。

Conclusion: 确立 honesty testing 作为软件工程新研究方向，并提出基准、模型/harness 分析、证据构建和生命周期测试等方向，以支持 faithful reporting 与任务正确性的平衡。

Abstract: Human oversight of large language models (LLMs) and artificial intelligence (AI) agents increasingly depends on their reports. Claims that conflict with a system's own judgments can conceal failures, prevent timely intervention, and contribute to severe harm. Greater autonomy makes faithful reporting increasingly important for maintaining human control. In this vision paper, we propose honesty testing of LLMs and AI agents as a new research area in software engineering. Building on existing honesty evaluations, our goal is to make faithful reporting a software quality requirement that developers can specify, test, and maintain. We define honesty bugs as behavioural defects in which an LLM or AI agent, when required to communicate faithfully, makes an explicit claim that conflicts with its own judgment at the time of reporting. We present an initial framework covering LLM-level testing under controlled inputs and agent-level testing of complete systems. The framework connects these levels to testing workflows and oracle evidence. We identify research directions in benchmarks, model and harness analysis, oracle construction, and lifecycle testing to support effective oversight alongside task correctness.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Benchmarks: Can Small Open-Weight Models Get Real Coding Work Done?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34241)
*Vaibhav Jain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开源语言模型在文本生成与代码任务完成上的差异显著，模型后端兼容端点在工具调用格式、转控、上下文行为、缓存复用及可靠性上差异巨大，已验证的代码通过和连续会话依赖具体服务配置而非仅模型能力，研究提供大量实证数据但不对模型或运行时排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在量化开源语言模型在实际编码代理工作中服务、协议、会话连续性、artifact验证和验证的实际表现，揭示模型端点兼容性的局限性及服务配置对任务成功的关键影响。

Method: 包含2026年7月至10月工程工作回顾，涉及编码客户端、模型专属服务食谱、vLLM、SGLang、llama.cpp和MLX。通过4165个编码代理网关轮次、623个调试任务及三个隐藏行为检查的委托研究，结合固定Claude Opus 5.5父模型进行对比实验，观测性测量冷缓存与高复用请求的首Token时间，并进行45次试验的直接编码与委托对比。

Result: 直接编码在15/15和45/45任务中均通过。委托给量化Qwen3-Coder 30B-A3B工作者通过率分别为12/15和14/15。Nemotron Cascade 2工作者在45任务中仅12/45通过（11次工作者替换）。计入16个因订阅限制被替代的会话为失败后，直接编码成功率37/45和8/45，Qwen3-Coder工作者0/45。冷缓存请求首Token中位时间约69.4秒，高复用请求2.8秒。后验诊断显示36个仅因参数重命名被拒绝的Artifact中有19个在隐藏检查中未变并通过，不计为成功。直接编码的每验证成功成本低于前沿API等价价格。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个基于证据的实践账户，涵盖服务、代理协议、会话连续性、artifact验证和验证。研究未池总数据，也不对模型或运行时进行排序。实际模型部署需关注服务配置、协议兼容性及验证流程，而非单纯模型能力。

Abstract: Making an open-weight language model generate text is not the same as making it finish coding work. This retrospective empirical systems experience report follows my engineering work from July to October 2026 across coding clients, model-specific serving recipes, vLLM, SGLang, llama.cpp, and MLX. Models behind a nominally compatible endpoint still differed in tool-call formatting, turn control, context behavior, cache reuse, and reliability. A served model, coherent text, executed tool calls, and code that passes independent behavioral checks proved to be distinct levels of progress. The evidence includes 4,165 coding-agent gateway turns from my own sessions, 623 debug jobs, and three separate delegation studies with hidden behavioral checks and a fixed Claude Opus 5.5 parent. Saved cold-prefix requests had a median time to first token of about 69.4 s, versus 2.8 s for high-reuse requests (observational). In a 45-trial study, direct coding passed 15/15 and delegation to a quantized Qwen3-Coder 30B-A3B worker passed 12/15 and 14/15. On a second 15-task suite under a fixed artifact contract, the completed replacement cohort had 45/45 for direct coding and 12/45 for a Nemotron Cascade 2 worker (11 worker substitutions). Sixteen sessions stopped by a subscription limit before any model call were replaced; counting them as failures gives 37/45 and 8/45. A Qwen3-Coder worker passed 0/45. In both studies with a direct arm, direct coding had lower API list-price-equivalent frontier cost per verified success. A post-hoc diagnostic found that 19 of 36 artifacts rejected only for renamed parameters passed the hidden checks unchanged; they are not counted as successes.
  The contribution is an evidence-qualified account of serving, agent protocol, session continuity, artifact validation, and verification in practice. The studies are not pooled and do not rank models or runtimes.

</details>


### [41] [Developer Task Delegation to LLMs and the Trust Factor: A Mixed Methods Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34317)
*Samuel Ferino,Rashina Hoda,John Grundy,Christoph Treude,Hashini Gunatilake*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过22次访谈和160名软件从业者的调研，探讨开发者将哪些软件开发任务委托给大语言模型、委托程度如何以及背后原因，并提出四种委托模式与关键影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅涵盖部分任务和因素，尚不清楚开发者实际会将哪些任务委托给LLM、委托多少以及为何委托，缺乏系统性的整体认识。

Method: 采用定性与定量混合方法：先通过22名受访者的访谈归纳任务委托模式及其动机，再通过160人的调研进行统计分析，考察任务难度、任务风险、开发者动机和信任等因素与委托行为的关联。

Result: 开发者采用四种委托模式：全委托、部分委托、自我委托和任务回避；统计分析显示，信任是与向LLM委托软件开发任务显著相关的因素，任务难度、风险和动机也与委托选择相关。

Conclusion: 信任是开发者向LLM委托任务的核心驱动力；研究为人机协作在软件工程中的应用提供了可操作建议，帮助从业者更有效地利用LLM的委托能力。

Abstract: With the rise of Large Language Models (LLMs), software developers are presented with a powerful new approach: delegating software development tasks to LLMs. Existing studies have explored task delegation for a subset of tasks and factors. Critically, what tasks developers delegate to LLMs, to what extent, and why they delegate them, is not yet well understood. We explore these questions through 22 interviews and a survey with 160 software practitioners. Developers reported employing four modes of task delegation to LLMs: full delegation, partial delegation, self-delegation, and task avoidance. Drawing on our qualitative findings, we discuss the rationales behind these choices and explore how task difficulty, task risk, developer motivation, and trust appear to be associated with software development task delegation to LLMs. Our statistical analysis reveals that trust is a significant factor associated with task delegation to LLMs. Finally, we share actionable implications for human-AI collaboration in software research, and for software practitioners to make the most of their newly found powers of delegation.

</details>


### [42] [EAST: Embodied AI Safety Testing via Systematic Fault Injection in Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34358)
*Yuhao Tian,Tao Li,Ling Zhou,Dave Towey,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenjun Wu,Rubing Huang,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 EAST，一个基于数字孪生的具身 AI 安全测试框架，将文献安全证据转化为可执行故障注入测试，揭示任务成功不等于安全的隐藏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估侧重性能与鲁棒性，缺乏从文档化安全危害到可验证测试的系统路径；机器人可能在完成任务的同时违反安全要求，任务成功不足以衡量安全。

Method: EAST 包含专家审核的安全故障分类法与八个可执行算子，并附适用性和有效性约束；采用三阶段工作流：生成测试规范、在仿真执行中验证故障实现、独立评估安全挑战、违规、任务结果与行为特征，并保留可回放执行记录；在六项 RoboTwin 操作任务和 ACT、RDT 两种策略上进行评估。

Result: 覆盖全部 74 个适用任务-策略-算子组合，获得 63 个有效证据；安全挑战暴露率接近随机合法与基于规则策略；82 次有效执行中任务完成但存在安全违规，暴露了成功导向评估隐藏的风险。

Conclusion: EAST 支持超越任务完成度的系统化、有效性感知、可复现的具身 AI 安全测试。

Abstract: Task success is not a sufficient measure of safety for embodied artificial intelligence (AI), because robots may complete objectives while violating safety requirements. Existing evaluations often emphasize performance and robustness, but lack a systematic route from documented safety hazards to executable, verifiable tests. We present EAST (Embodied AI Safety Testing), a digital-twin framework that converts literature-grounded safety evidence into fault-injection tests across perception, instructions, environments, actions, and timing. EAST combines an expert-reviewed safety-fault taxonomy and eight executable operators with applicability and validity constraints. Its three-stage workflow generates test specifications, verifies fault realization during simulated execution, and independently assesses safety challenges, violations, task outcomes, and behavioral signatures using simulator-state evidence. The framework preserves execution traces and replayable records, supporting attribution and reproducibility across experiments. We evaluate EAST on six RoboTwin manipulation tasks and two policy architectures, ACT and RDT, against alternative testing strategies under matched budgets. Across five comparative campaigns, EAST targets all 74 admissible task-policy-operator combinations and obtains valid evidence for 63, while achieving safety-challenge exposure rates close to Random-Legal and Rule-Based. Moreover, 82 valid EAST executions complete their tasks despite safety violations, revealing risks concealed by success-oriented evaluation. These findings support systematic, validity-aware, reproducible safety testing beyond task-completion metrics.

</details>


### [43] [ComBeautify: Leveraging Code Change Summaries for Conventional Commit Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34374)
*Rishi Raj,Aashray Tandon,Nikolay Matyunin,Jibesh Patra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ComBeautify，一种利用大语言模型对 Git diff 进行上下文保留摘要，自动生成符合常规提交规范（CCS）提交信息的方法，并从历史提交中学习项目特定的类别定义，显著提升提交分类与信息生成的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提交信息对传达开发者意图至关重要，但不影响程序功能，导致开发者缺乏编写有意义提交信息的动力。虽然 CCS 提供结构化格式，但开发者常难以在遵循规范的同时准确表达意图，且现有方法依赖手动编制的类别定义，缺乏项目特定指导。

Method: ComBeautify 采用混合方法：结合 LLM 对 Git diff 进行上下文保留摘要以提升 CCS 下的提交分类准确率；从历史提交中自动学习类别定义，而非人工编制；并提出自动生成 CCS 描述组件的方法。

Result: 在 13 种语言、6500 个开源仓库和超 2000 万次提交上的评估显示 CCS 合规率随时间上升；在 20 个热门仓库上，基于项目特定数据训练的 ComBeautify 优于基线最高 3 倍；在 2000 条人工标注提交的数据集上，摘要方法准确率最高达 95.25%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自适应且具备上下文感知能力的 AI 方法对于提交分类和 CCS 合规提交信息生成至关重要，项目特定数据优于通用类别定义。

Abstract: Modern software development relies on version control systems such as Git to track changes and facilitate collaboration. While commit messages are crucial for conveying developer intent, they do not affect program functionality; as a result, developers often lack motivation to write meaningful ones. The Conventional Commits Specification (CCS) addresses this with a structured format and guidelines that help developers express the intent of a change, improving clarity and enabling automation. Yet, developers frequently struggle to express intent while adhering to the format. In particular, they may misclassify commits due to the large number of possible categories or the lack of project-specific guidance. We introduce ComBeautify, an approach that automatically generates CCS-compliant commit messages. Our hybrid approach leverages context-preserving summarization of Git diffs with large language models (LLMs) to improve commit classification accuracy under CCS. Rather than relying on manually curated category definitions, we learn them from historical commits. We also propose a method for automatically generating the CCS description component.
  Our empirical evaluation across 13 languages, 6,500 open-source repositories, and over 20 million commits provides long-term insights into CCS adoption, revealing increasing compliance. Our evaluation on twenty popular repositories reveals the limitations of approaches guided by generic category definitions, and shows that ComBeautify, when trained on project-specific data, outperforms baselines by up to 3x. Additionally, using a dataset of 2,000 manually curated commits, we validate our summarization approach, achieving up to 95.25% accuracy and surpassing baselines. These findings emphasize the need for adaptable, context-aware AI methods for commit classification and CCS-compliant commit message generation.

</details>


### [44] [From GPU to TPU with Coding Agents: What Works, What Fails, and What It Costs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34422)
*Hui Sun,Anderson Uchôa,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过编程代理将10个PyTorch项目从GPU迁移到TPU，发现多数可运行但存在静默计算变更、高失败率和成本波动，建议独立验证与制定工作负载特定标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算力成本上升、加速器产能有限及供应商依赖推动组织寻求GPU替代方案，关键问题是代理生成的TPU迁移是否可信及其成本。

Method: 构建148个PyTorch研究项目数据集，使用Claude Code将其中10个项目从NVIDIA GPU迁移至Google Cloud TPU，独立评估运行、数值保持、硬件意图、项目目标及性能，并考察失败、人力干预与成本。

Result: 8个项目满足项目目标标准，平均需2.4次人工干预；70次执行中53次失败（76%），其中49%无错误报告；主要失败原因为重复编译等执行低效，第三方库交互可能静默改变计算（如梯度裁剪变更致准确率从86.53%降至84.89%）；成本差异大，单一工作负载中TorchTPU训练速度快1.86倍且每次运行成本低25%。

Conclusion: 从业者应开展独立参考运行、在目标硬件配置下进行验证，并制定兼顾计算与人力成本的工作负载特定验收与停止标准。

Abstract: A coding agent can migrate a machine learning workload, report success, and still change how the model trains. Rising compute costs, limited accelerator capacity, and vendor dependence are pushing organizations toward alternatives to graphics processing units (GPUs); a key question is whether agent-generated migrations to tensor processing units (TPUs) can be trusted and at what cost. We build a dataset of 148 PyTorch ML research projects and use Claude Code to migrate ten of them from NVIDIA GPUs to Google Cloud TPUs. We independently assess whether each migration runs, preserves numerical behavior, executes on the intended hardware, maintains the project's goal, and delivers acceptable performance. We also examine failures, human effort, and costs. Eight projects meet our project-goal criterion, and migrations require 2.4 human interventions on average. Of 70 execution attempts, 53 fail (76%), and 26 of these failures (49%) raise no error. The most frequent cause is repeated compilation and other execution inefficiencies, while interactions with third-party libraries can silently change the computation: in one four-chip migration, the agent reports success despite a change in gradient clipping that reduces accuracy from 86.53% to 84.89%. Execution costs vary widely: in one workload, TorchTPU, a PyTorch execution backend for TPUs, trains 1.86 times faster and 25% cheaper per run than the GPU. For practitioners, these findings motivate independent reference runs, verification under the intended hardware configuration, and workload-specific acceptance and stopping criteria that account for both computational and human costs.

</details>


### [45] [MulFCoder: Framework-conditioned Multi-agent for MLLM-based Multi-framework Front-end Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34418)
*Jie Wu,Haoran Ma,Shisong Tang,Yulin Xu,Xiaoyu Kang,Jiechao Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现有MLLM方法多聚焦HTML/CSS，忽视React/Vue/Angular等多框架代码生成。提出MulFCoder，一种框架条件化的多智能体方法，显式编码框架约束将多框架差异映射至可决规则空间，通过Grounder、Planner、Writer、Judger四代理协作，从UI元素构建表格与布局、生成任务调度与框架合约，在受限编辑窗口内生成代码并通过轻量化约束验证修复，无需昂贵构建即防止漂移与死锁。实验显著提升编译成功率，特别是在Angular上效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前端代码生成主要局限于HTML/CSS，缺乏对主流JavaScript框架的支持。提示词的 naive 修改导致跨框架性能差距大，易产生框架特定错误。需要一种能显式编码框架约束、统一多框架差异的生成范式。

Method: MulFCoder采用四代理协作框架：Grounder从检测 UI 元素构建 ElementTable、ContentTable 与宏布局区域；Planner 构建类 DOM 的层级布局树、生成任务调度并推导框架专用文件合约；Writer 在受限编辑窗口内生成结构化文件写入或补丁；Judger 执行轻量化、框架条件化的约束验证，接受或拒绝更新并触发有界修复，防止漂移与死锁，无需昂贵构建。

Result: 实验表明，MulFCoder 显著提升代码编译成功率，减少框架特定错误，尤其在 Angular 框架上取得显著提升，生成代码的可运行性和一致性均有改善。

Conclusion: MulFCoder 通过显式编码框架约束与多智能体协作，有效解决了多前端框架代码生成中的性能差距与错误问题。该方法为 UI 截图自动化生成可运行前端代码提供了新的解决方案，并在复杂框架下表现优异。

Abstract: Generating runnable front-end code from UI screenshots is a long-standing goal in automated software engineering. Existing MLLM-based methods predominantly focused on HTML/CSS, leaving multi-framework generation for React/Vue/Angular underexplored. Naively modifying prompts leads to substantial performance gaps across multi-framework and highly framework-specific error modes. To address this, we propose MulFCoder, a framework-conditioned multi-agent method that explicitly encodes framework constraints to bring multi-framework differences into a decidable rule space. MulFCoder orchestrates four agents: Grounder constructs an ElementTable, ContentTable, and macro-layout regions from detected UI elements; Planner builds a DOM-like hierarchical layout tree, produces a task schedule, and derives a framework-specific file Contract; Writer generates structured file writes or patches within a restricted edit window; Judger enforces lightweight, framework-conditioned constraints to accept or reject updates and trigger bounded repairs, preventing drift and deadlocks without expensive builds. Experiments demonstrate that MulFCoder substantially improves compilation success rate and reduces framework-specific errors, with particularly pronounced gains on Angular.

</details>


### [46] [SymLift: Neural Semantic Lifting for Compile-Free Vulnerability Detection in Context-Incomplete Code Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34431)
*Xiarun Chen,Jiaxing Xu,Bozhi Wu,Chenhui Cui,Chunrong Fang,Weiping Wen,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SymLift框架针对上下文不完整代码漏洞检测，实现高精度与可审计推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失项目上下文阻碍构建分析，神经预测缺乏审计性。

Method: 编译-free框架，LLM语义提升生成LLVM IR，确定性解析与Datalog推导。

Result: 分析完成率仅降1.17%，FormAI F1达94.41%，MegaVul微均Precision 98.24%。

Conclusion: 上下文不完整场景下可实现准确可审计漏洞检测。

Abstract: Missing project context can prevent build-dependent analyzers from constructing program relations required for vulnerability detection, while neural predictions alone do not establish auditable derivations. We present SymLift, a compile-free framework centered on Vulnerability-Oriented Neural Semantic Lifting for context-incomplete code fragments. A fine-tuned large language model reconstructs vulnerability-relevant control-flow, data-flow, memory, and resource relations in a constrained LLVM-like intermediate representation (IR), without reconstructing the full project context or directly predicting vulnerability labels. A deterministic parser validates the IR and maps its supported operations and relations into normalized evidence facts. Explicit Datalog rules derive findings with supporting facts and derivation traces, ensuring deterministic reasoning for fixed facts and rules. We evaluate SymLift on FormAI, Juliet, and MegaVul. Reducing complete Juliet programs to function-level fragments lowers Clang compilation success from 100% to 12.00%, whereas SymLift's analysis completion rate decreases by only 1.17 percentage points. After task-specific fine-tuning, the lifter achieves a control-flow intersection over union (IoU) of 96.85% on FormAI. SymLift-Qwen-7B achieves a Target-F1 of 94.41% on FormAI and, across six vulnerability families on MegaVul, a micro-averaged precision of 98.24% and a micro-averaged Target-F1 of 91.66%. These results support accurate and auditable detection in the evaluated context-incomplete settings, even with partial program relations.

</details>


### [47] [A Multi-Domain Study on the Effectiveness of LLMs at Writing Property-Based Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34487)
*M M Abid Naziri,Feiran,Qin,Khush Patel,Marcelo d'Amorim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估了两个基于LLM的PBT工具在五大领域十个程序上的表现，Agentic-PBT在分支覆盖上优于Proptest-AI，并发现180个bug，65个被确认，23个已修复，指出低可观测性是主要挑战，建议成熟模糊测试器配合LLM编写属性以验证文档行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的PBT工具仅在Python库上评估，需研究其在深度学习库、数据库、系统软件、编译器、LLM服务引擎等多领域、多语言程序上的泛化能力。

Method: 在五大领域十个程序上对比Proptest-AI和Agentic-PBT，测量分支覆盖率，与领域专用模糊测试器对比，提交180个bug报告并追踪确认和修复情况。

Result: Agentic-PBT在7/10程序上覆盖更多分支（Opus 5），全10程序覆盖更多（GLM-5.3-Flash）；领域模糊测试器在编译器和PostgreSQL覆盖更多，在PyTorch覆盖较少；共提交180个bug报告，44个重复，65个被确认，23个已修复，覆盖所有五大领域。

Conclusion: 低可观测性阻碍LLM-based PBT工具；成熟模糊测试器结合LLM编写属性可验证功能是否符合文档。

Abstract: A property-based test (PBT) checks a property of a program on many generated inputs, but writing one takes a property, a generator of valid inputs, and an oracle. LLM-based PBT writers produce all three, yet they have been evaluated only on Python libraries. We study how two of them, Proptest-AI and Agentic-PBT, generalize to ten programs in five domains: deep learning libraries, databases, system software, compilers, and LLM serving engines. Most of these programs take text in their own language, such as queries, configurations, and programs. Agentic-PBT covers more branches than Proptest-AI on seven of the ten programs with Opus 5 and on all ten with GLM-5.3-Flash. Domain-specific fuzzers cover more of the compilers and of PostgreSQL and less of PyTorch than Agentic-PBT. We filed 180 bug reports, 44 of which duplicate reports that others filed after the models' knowledge cutoffs; maintainers have confirmed 65 of the others, in all five domains, and fixed 23. We draw lessons for building and evaluating such writers: low observability hurts them, and mature fuzzers paired with LLM-written properties could check that each feature behaves as documented.

</details>


### [48] [Multimodal Requirements Traceability Link Recovery in Industrial Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34503)
*Haoming Huang,Shinpei Hayashi,Kazuya Kohata,Myeongki Kim,Yuki Kurihara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对工业需求中的多模态可追踪性链接恢复，提出一种基于检索增强生成的方法，分别检索文本与图像候选并利用视觉语言模型进行分类，通过口语化置信度进行链接过滤。实验结果显示，在工业数据集上F1达0.310，优于文本基线0.114，但跨模态检索仍是主要瓶颈，置信度过滤则在精确率与召回率间提供权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业需求常包含图像，现有文本-only方法无法直接处理；固定分类器下二元决策无法平衡漏链与误链，因此需要一种兼容多模态输入并提供置信度调节的可追踪性链接恢复方法。

Method: 本文扩展RAG方法用于多模态需求可追踪性链接恢复。具体流程包括：分别检索文本与图像候选对，利用视觉语言模型对每对结果进行分类，并利用口语化置信度对预测链接进行过滤。

Result: 在三个非重叠的工业需求集上，该方法实现F1 0.310，显著高于Hey等人基线的0.114。方法在所有四种模态组合中均能恢复链接，在工业数据上提升了文本-only的F1表现，且在公开文本-only数据集上保持相似性能。但受限于图像查询未能检索到相关文本需求，恢复效果仍受限。置信度过滤能在未过滤链接中去除误正例并部分正确链接，提供精确率-召回率权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究利用工业数据提供了当前RAG基多模态需求可追踪性链接恢复方法的潜力与局限证据。主要局限在于跨模态检索环节，未来优化应优先聚焦于提升跨模态检索能力。

Abstract: Requirements traceability links support activities such as change-impact analysis, coverage checking, and validation. Industrial requirements can include traceable images, which text-only approaches cannot process directly. For a fixed classifier configuration, binary decisions alone offer no direct way to adjust the balance between missed and incorrect links. We extend a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approach for inter-requirements traceability link recovery (TLR). Our extension retrieves text and image candidates separately, classifies each retrieved pair with a vision-language model, and filters the predicted links by verbalized confidence. We evaluate our approach on three non-overlapping industrial requirement sets and six public text-only datasets. On the industrial test sets, our approach achieves an F1 score of 0.310, compared with 0.114 for our replication of the baseline approach by Hey et al. Our approach recovers links in all four modality combinations while improving text-only F1 on the industrial data and maintaining similar performance on the public datasets, but recovery remains limited by failures to retrieve related text requirements for image queries. Confidence filtering produces link sets with different precision and recall, removing false positives and some correctly recovered links from the unfiltered link set. This study provides industrial evidence on the potential and limitations of a current RAG-based TLR approach for multimodal requirements and identifies cross-modal retrieval as the component to prioritize in further optimization.

</details>


### [49] [Software Engineering for AI-Driven Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34573)
*Lukas Twist,Xinjian Wu,Jingzhi Gong,Shuyin Ouyang,Ying Xiao,Benedetta Donato,Yaoqi Guo,Zhenpeng Chen,Leonardo Mariani,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为SE4AI4Sci（面向AI驱动科学的软件工程）的新兴研究领域，旨在通过构建‘科学保障链’来解决AI自动化科学发现过程中因代码错误、实验问题或证据解释偏差导致的不可靠结论问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI自主代理能够分析复杂问题、设计实验、编写执行代码并得出科学结论，但其生成的代码错误、实验过程缺陷或证据解释偏差可能导致无效结论，威胁自动化科学发现的可靠性与可重复性，因此急需新的保障机制。

Method: 作者引入‘科学保障链’作为组织框架，串联意图、实验设计、实现、执行、证据和结论环节，并通过分析六个近期AI科学系统的典型失败案例，揭示保障链可能断裂的环节、现有保障机制的不足及由此引发的新研究挑战。

Result: 研究揭示了现有保障机制在意图传递、实验设计验证、代码鲁棒性、执行可控性、证据解释和结论验证等多个环节存在明显不足，并基于此提出了一系列亟待解决的研究课题。

Conclusion: 建立面向AI驱动科学的软件工程保障体系势具重要研究价值与应用前景，有必要通过跨链条的软件工程技术创新，提升AI自动化科学发现的可信度与可重复性。

Abstract: AI has made significant contributions to scientific discovery. Autonomous agents can now independently analyse complex problems, design experiments, write and execute code, analyse findings, and derive scientific conclusions. Nevertheless, errors in AI-generated code, experimental procedures, or evidence interpretation can lead to invalid research conclusions, undermining the reliability and reproducibility of automated scientific discovery. In this vision paper, we propose Software Engineering for AI-Driven Science (SE4AI4Sci) as an emerging research area for addressing this challenge. As an organising framework, we introduce the Scientific Assurance Chain, connecting intent, experimental design, implementation, execution, evidence, and claim. Through six documented failures in recent AI-for-science systems, we show where this chain can break, where existing assurance mechanisms fall short, and what new research challenges follow. We also outline a research agenda for adapting and extending software engineering techniques across the chain.

</details>


### [50] [Do AI Coding Agents Write Infrastructure as Code Like Humans? An Empirical Study of GitHub Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34613)
*Amine Barrak,Emna Ksontini,Charles Wehbe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI代理和自动化机器人越来越多地贡献Infrastructure-as-Code（IaC）拉取请求。本研究分析了49,241个GitHub仓库中的349,156个IaC PR，发现机器人占 ostensibly 人类PR的32.9%，沉默代理和披露代理占比不断增长。代理PR体积约是人类的4倍，更常修改核心基础设施模块，而机器人主要维护依赖项。静默代理的整体合并率高于人类（87.4% vs. 78.9%），但在同一仓库内合并速度慢4.0个百分点。披露代理更常创建新基础设施，但针对相同变更类型其PR合并可能性低8.4个百分点。可持续性担忧（如缺少远程状态和单体配置）在代理和人类间出现率相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理生成IaC的普及，关于其贡献与人类和机器人相比的差异、维护者的评价方式以及是否引入不同的可持续性顾虑知之甚少。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究者分析了49,241个GitHub仓库中的349,156个IaC拉取请求，将贡献者分类为人类、自动化机器人、沉默代理和披露代理。他们比较了不同组别的PR体积、修改目标、合并率以及可持续性味道（如缺少远程状态、单体配置）的频率，并在2,645个匹配的Terraform人-agent PR对上进行了统计分析。

Result: 机器人占 ostensibly 人类PR的32.9%，剩余PR中含代理痕迹的比例为12.3%，且随研究期间增长。代理PR体积约为人类的4倍，更常修改核心基础设施模块，而机器人主要维护依赖项。静默代理的整体合并率高于人类（87.4% vs. 78.9%），但同一仓库内合并可能性低4.0个百分点。披露代理更常创建新基础设施（34.0% vs. 21.4%），但针对相同变更类型其PR合并可能性低8.4个百分点。静默代理在大多数变更类型上的接受度与人类相似。在2,645个匹配的Terraform人-agent PR对中，代理和人类引入可持续性味道的率相似（10.4% vs. 10.3%；配对比值1.02，95% CI 0.83-1.25），最常见的包括缺少远程状态和单体配置。

Conclusion: AI代理和机器人在IaC的维护和扩展中扮演重要角色，但其贡献在规模、焦点和接受度上各有差异。虽然代理引入的可持续性顾虑与人类相似，但维护者不太可能合并披露代理的PR。这些发现凸显了在AI生成的基础设施代码周围需要提高意识并制定指南。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) configurations define cloud resources, networking, and access control, and AI coding agents increasingly write them. Little is known about how agent contributions differ from those of humans and automation bots, how maintainers evaluate them, and whether they introduce different sustainability concerns.
  We study 349,156 IaC pull requests (PRs) from 49,241 GitHub repositories, distinguishing humans, automation bots, silent agents, and disclosed agents. Bots account for 32.9% of ostensibly human PRs, and 12.3% of the remaining PRs contain agent traces, a share that grows over the study period. Agent PRs are about four times larger than human PRs and more often modify core infrastructure modules, whereas bots mainly maintain dependencies. Silent-agent PRs have higher overall merge rates than human PRs (87.4% vs. 78.9%) but are merged 4.0 percentage points less often within the same repositories. Disclosed agents create new infrastructure more often than humans (34.0% vs. 21.4%), and even for the same change type within the same repository, their PRs are 8.4 points less likely to be merged; silent-agent PRs show comparable acceptance for most change types. Finally, in 2,645 matched agent-human Terraform PR pairs, agent and human PRs introduce sustainability smells at similar rates (10.4% vs. 10.3%; paired odds ratio 1.02, 95% CI 0.83-1.25), most commonly missing remote state and monolithic configurations.

</details>


### [51] [Built to Remember, Shipped to Forget: Operational Contracts, Failures, and Repairs in Containerized LLM Agent Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34620)
*Meriem Mastouri,Emna Ksontini,Amine Barrak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了LLM代理系统部署平台（如Docker、Compose、Kubernetes）与代理框架（如LangGraph）之间的边界义务。平台视容器为可丢弃，而框架需持久化对话、暂停等待人工输入并从保存状态恢复，双方未明确合成后的行为义务。研究提出14条运行时合同，并通过多种证据验证部署中普遍存在的违约及其修复模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 框架与平台文档仅规定各自行为，未说明组合后应保证代理系统所需行为的义务，导致部署层面的状态丢失、服务中断等问题缺乏明确规范和检测依据。

Method: 从LangGraph及三个平台的首方文档中提取14条运行合同；利用三组独立证据进行评估：433个开源已部署代理系统、对12个系统注入的7类事件、以及从39,261个提交、拉取请求和问题中挖掘的122个开发者修复。

Result: 75.5%的系统至少适用一条合同，但保证常缺失：37.4%在重启后丢失状态，41.5%在容器替换后丢失，所有可评估的部署在排水期间停止生产服务器。故障注入中16个预测违例有14个转化为运行时故障，7个预测保持全部通过。开发者修复覆盖14条合同中的12条，78%修改应用代码，只有约三分之二能完全恢复被违背的保证。

Conclusion: 研究给出14条合同目录、检测规则、故障注入工具和35模式修复分类法，为检查和增强代理系统部署提供基础。

Abstract: LLM agent systems reach production as container images run by platforms such as Docker, Compose, and Kubernetes. Framework and platform document opposite expectations: to the platform a container is disposable, while the framework persists conversations, pauses runs for human input, and resumes interrupted work from saved state; each side specifies only its own behavior, so the obligations their composition creates are stated nowhere. We present the first empirical study of this boundary, stating these obligations as operational contracts: guarantees a deployment must provide to preserve an agent system's required behavior when a deployment-platform event occurs. From the first-party documentation of LangGraph and the three platforms we derive 14 contracts and evaluate them with three independent sources of evidence: 433 deployed open-source agent systems, seven events induced on 12 of them, and 122 developer repairs mined from 39,261 commits, pull requests, and issues. At least one contract applies to 75.5% of the systems, yet its guarantee is routinely missing: of systems that must keep state beyond the process, 37.4% lose it to a restart and 41.5% to a container replacement, and every assessable deployment stops the framework's production server mid-drain. Induced events turn 14 of 16 predicted violations into runtime failures and all seven predicted holds pass. The failures are born in deployment but repaired in code: developer repairs independently cover twelve of the fourteen contracts, 78% change application code, and only two in three fully restore the violated guarantee. The 14-contract catalogue, detection rules, fault-injection harness, and 35-pattern repair taxonomy provide a basis for checking and strengthening agent deployments.

</details>


### [52] [CodeMeepo: A Multi-Agent Workflow for Repository-Level Code Migration and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34633)
*Farzad Habibi,Changrong Wu,Jinzhou Jiang,Guohan Lu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeMeepo是一种多智能体管道，用于完整高效的大型仓库代码迁移，能够在保持行为一致的前提下实现更高的通过率和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码迁移面临行为跨模块依赖、上下文窗口限制以及迁移后性能下降等挑战，现有LLM代理难以全局正确处理这些问题。

Method: 采用八个专业智能体通过文件 artifacts 通信，围绕固定外部基准的有序里程碑进行评估，使用 parity 评估补全缺失，累计回归检查防止已迁移部分破坏，并加入性能优化与全套测试。

Result: 在九个主题的三种语言对上，CodeMeepo 取得最佳通过率，比最先进的多智能体基线多通过 18% 的测试；在生产守护进程上，优化阶段使 50% 基准提升，平均速度提升 25%。

Conclusion: CodeMeepo 有效解决了代码迁移的主要难点，提供了更完整、更可靠且更高效的迁移方案。

Abstract: Repository-level code migration rewrites an entire project in a new programming language while preserving its observable behavior. It is challenging because behavior spans interdependent modules, dependencies and build infrastructure must be adapted to the target ecosystem, and the source code is often the only specification.
  Large language model agents can now attempt these migrations end to end, but three challenges remain. First, an agent that writes both the translation and its acceptance tests may overlook the same requirements in both, leaving missing or incorrect behavior undetected. Second, large repositories can exceed a model's context window, and dividing the work into smaller tasks does not ensure that all required behavior is migrated. Third, even behaviorally correct translations may be slower than the original implementation.
  We present CodeMeepo, a multi-agent pipeline for complete and efficient migration of large repositories. Each milestone is evaluated against a fixed external oracle that the agents neither author nor modify. A separate parity assessment compares source and target components in scope and adds identified gaps back into the plan as required work. Ordered milestones limit the scope of each task, while cumulative regression checks guard against breaking already-migrated behavior. A benchmark-guided optimization phase addresses performance, followed by full-suite conformance testing and repair. Eight specialized agent roles communicate through file artifacts, and a deterministic state machine governs repair, deferral, scope changes, and termination.
  On nine subjects in three language pairs, CodeMeepo achieves the best pass rate on every subject and passes 18% more tests than a state-of-the-art multi-agent baseline using the same model. On a production daemon, its optimization phase improves half of the benchmarks, by 25% on average.

</details>


### [53] [Thinking-in-Bets at Test Time Reduces Sycophantic Revisions in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34654)
*Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Thinking-in-Bets 的训练-free 方法，代理在阅读用户反馈前写下主要声明、支持检查和置信度。受挑战时，代理查阅此声明清单以保留已验证的工作，同时调查不确定或新受挑战的部分。在八个基准测试中，记录声明可将错误撤回降至最低，保护正确的草稿，并提高各模型的准确性和置信度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理在用户挑战时倾向于放弃正确的工作。简单告知它们坚定会导致它们忽略真实错误。阅读投诉后，代理会贬低自身先前的检查以同意用户观点。

Method: 引入 Thinking-in-Bets，一种无需训练的方法，代理在阅读用户反馈前编写主要声明、支持检查和置信度的短私有记录。受挑战时，代理查阅 Claim Checklist 以保留已验证工作并调查不确定部分。记录在投诉后编写恢复收益极少。通过仅在代理不确定时编写记录来降低token开销，同时修复比基线更多真实Bug。即使在无用户反馈的非交互任务中，列出关键声明并在提交前重检查最弱者也能提升准确性和置信度估计。

Result: 声明在批评前的记录几乎消除了补丁审查中的错误撤回，并保护了在误推动下通过的报告草稿。在多轮编码中，预回声明记录显著减少了在误导投诉后的不必要文件编辑。仅在不确定时编写记录可降低token开销，同时修复比基线更多真实Bug。即使无用户反馈，列出关键声明并在提交前重检查最弱者也能提升所有测试模型的答案准确性和置信度估计。

Conclusion: Thinking-in-Bets 通过在用户挑战前记录声明，有效缓解了代理放弃正确工作的倾向。它在多个基准和模型中减少了错误撤回，保护了正确输出，降低了token开销，并提高了答案准确性和置信度估计。

Abstract: Language model agents often abandon correct work when a user challenges it, yet simply telling them to stand firm makes them ignore real mistakes. Once an agent reads a complaint, it tends to discount its own earlier checks in order to agree with the user. We introduce Thinking-in-Bets, a training-free method in which an agent writes a short private record of its main claims, the checks supporting them, and its confidence before reading user feedback. When challenged, the agent consults this Claim Checklist to keep verified work intact while investigating uncertain or newly challenged parts. We evaluate five Gemini models (3.5 Flash-Lite, 3.5 Flash, 3.6 Flash, 3.8 Flash, and 4.0 Argon) across eight benchmarks: SWE-Together, SWE-Interact, and DeepSWE for software engineering, Agents' Last Exam for professional reports, Humanity's Last Exam and SycEval for academic reasoning, Vals Finance for financial analysis, and Terminal-Bench for command-line tasks. Recording claims before criticism nearly eliminates false reversals in patch review and protects passing report drafts under mistaken pushback, whereas writing the same record after reading the complaint recovers very little of the gain. In multi-turn coding, pre-reply claim records substantially reduce unnecessary file edits after mistaken complaints, and gating the record so it is written only when the agent is uncertain lowers token overhead while fixing more real bugs than the baseline. Even in non-interactive tasks without user feedback, listing the key claims in a draft and re-checking the weakest one before submission improves both answer accuracy and confidence estimates across all tested models.

</details>


### [54] [The Physical Cost of Context: Quality-Constrained Resource Accounting for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34763)
*Zhongming Yu,Yitong Ma,Hengjia Yu,Boqin Yuan,Xiangye Lin,Hejia Zhang,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种质量门控协议来评估编码代理的上下文策略，通过测量KV缓存、时间和能耗等物理指标，发现预加载和压缩策略在满足非劣效性门控的同时能显著降低模型调用和GPU能耗，但删除历史可能增加额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文策略通常仅以提示词节省的token数作为评价标准，忽略了调用次数、前缀复用以及实际物理资源消耗。

Method: 将预填充分解为逻辑提示减去复用前缀，定义KV驻留、时间和能耗等度量；在SWE-rebench V2的450个案例上测试Eager（预加载10个块）和Compact（锚定历史压缩），并进行900个单元格的消融实验；使用mini-SWE-agent在100个SWE-bench Verified实例上评估Mask-5策略。

Result: Eager减少模型调用35.7%，GPU能耗代理指标降低33.2%；Compact未进一步节省；前缀缓存使Eager的未缓存预填充减少67.7%；Mask-5虽减少提示词体积33.3%，但导致未缓存预填充增加608.5%，能耗增加125.6%，无法通过质量门控。

Conclusion: 有效的上下文策略应通过缩短轨迹来节省物理工作，同时避免编辑可复用历史导致工作倍增；仅以token节省衡量可能掩盖实际开销。

Abstract: Coding agents resend a growing context on every model call, and context policies are usually judged by the prompt tokens they save. Executed work also depends on call count and prefix reuse: a caching runtime prefills only the tokens after the longest cached prefix. A shorter prompt can therefore cost more if it rewrites cached history. Our quality-gated protocol splits executed prefill into logical prompt minus reused prefix and measures KV residency, time, and energy. On 450 localization cases derived from SWE-rebench V2, preloading ten retrieved blocks (Eager) and adding anchored history compaction (Compact) both pass a five-point non-inferiority gate. In a separate 900-cell study, Eager cuts model calls by 35.7% and the GPU-energy proxy by 33.2% against on-demand search (Grep); Compact saves nothing further. Enabling prefix caching leaves Eager's prompt volume similar (+8.3%; its 95% CI spans zero) but cuts its uncached prefill by 67.7%. Deleting history can turn a token saving into extra work. With mini-SWE-agent on 100 SWE-bench Verified instances, keeping only the last five observations (Mask-5) cuts Eager's prompt volume by 33.3% but loses 135.9k reusable tokens per task. Uncached prefill rises by 608.5%, the energy proxy by 125.6%, and the policy misses the quality gate. Policies thus save physical work by shortening the trajectory and can multiply it by editing reusable history.

</details>


### [55] [Silent and Costly: Characterizing and Detecting Prompt-Caching Mis-Integration in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34817)
*Ying Zhang,Ruoqi Guo,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Yuekang Li,Yepang Liu,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对10个主流开源智能体系统中的120个提示缓存（prompt caching）相关拉取请求进行了实证分析，发现提示缓存错误集成主要源于请求侧配置错误和读取侧使用计数错误，且大多静默失败。为此，研究团队设计了CACHELINT工具，结合CodeQL静态分析与提供商能力预言机自动检测这些错误。实验表明，CACHELINT在真实项目中以0.86的精确率识别出32个错误集成，并推动了包括Anthropic官方SDK在内的18个上游修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）服务商通过提示缓存（prompt caching）来降低推理成本和延迟。然而，由于不同提供商的API异构且缓存语义持续变化，正确集成提示缓存极具挑战性。实证研究表明，错误集成通常会静默失败，不产生明显的运行时错误，从而隐藏了性能和成本问题。因此，迫切需要一种自动化工具来识别和修复这些隐蔽的缓存集成错误。

Method: CACHELINT将提示缓存错误集成建模为“调用站点”与“正确集成所需的前置条件”。它利用CodeQL静态分析工具定位缓存调用站点，然后针对每个前置条件，结合日期化的提供商能力预言机（provider capability oracle）或LLM防护检查（LLM guard check）进行条件验证，从而自动识别配置错误。

Result: 实验评估显示，CACHELINT在10个智能体系统的120个标注错误集成中达到了0.71的精确率和0.78的召回率。在更广泛的现实世界评估中，它在571个智能体项目中成功识别出32个错误集成，精确率高达0.86。该工具的有效性得到了21名开发者的确认，并促成了18个上游代码修复（包括Anthropic官方SDK中的一处修复）。

Conclusion: 提示缓存错误集成是大语言模型应用中常见但隐蔽的问题，常导致静默失败和成本浪费。CACHELINT的提出证明了结合静态分析（CodeQL）与动态预言机（provider oracle）能够有效、高精度地检测这类错误。该工具在实际应用中表现优异，成功推动了行业上游的修复，对提升LLM系统的可靠性和经济性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services process massive volumes of user requests, resulting in substantial inference costs. To reduce both cost and latency, providers have introduced prompt caching. However, correctly integrating prompt caching is non-trivial due to heterogeneous provider-specific APIs and continuously evolving caching semantics. To understand prompt-caching mis-integration in downstream applications, we conduct the first empirical study of 120 prompt-caching-related pull requests from 10 popular open-source agent systems. Our findings show that prompt-caching mis-integrations arise in both request-side misconfiguration and read-side usage miscount. More importantly, most mis-integrations fail silently without causing observable runtime failures.
  Motivated by these findings, we design CACHELINT, which models a prompt-caching mis-integration as an invocation site together with the preconditions required for correct cache integration. CACHELINT locates caching invocation sites with CodeQL and discharges each precondition either against a dated provider capability oracle or with an LLM guard check. CACHELINT achieves 0.71 precision and 0.78 recall in identifying the 120 labeled mis-integrations across the 10 studied agent systems. Our evaluation further demonstrates the effectiveness of CACHELINT in real-world settings, identifying 32 mis-integrations across 571 agent projects with 0.86 precision. These findings have received 21 developer confirmations and led to 18 upstream fixes, including one in Anthropic's official SDK.

</details>


### [56] [Large Language Models for Quantum Code Translation: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34841)
*Xiyuan Li,Yuechen Li,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型的量子程序跨SDK翻译方法，以解决量子软件开发中语义复杂和SDK碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件开发面临量子力学语义复杂以及分散的软件开发工具包（SDK）碎片化问题，导致跨SDK代码翻译和执行困难。

Method: 本文基于大语言模型（LLM）设计了量子感知的翻译工作流程，优化提示词设计并验证以反映量子语义，同时适配量子生态系统的版本演进。

Result: 在43个任务上对4种LLM配置进行初步评估，将Qiskit程序翻译为PennyLane和Q#，LLM零样本Pass@1最高可达72.33%，而一步式提示进一步提升了翻译效果。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明基于LLM的量子代码翻译具有潜力，为解决跨SDK量子程序迁移难题提供了可能方向。

Abstract: Quantum software development is challenged by quantum-mechanical semantics and fragmented software development kits (SDKs), which complicate cross-SDK code translation and execution. In this paper, we propose an LLM-based approach for translating executable programs between high-level quantum SDKs. We develop a quantum-aware translation workflow whose prompt design and validation reflect quantum semantics and accommodate the version evolution of quantum ecosystems. We conduct a preliminary evaluation of 4 LLM configurations on 43 tasks, translating Qiskit programs to PennyLane and Q#. LLMs achieve a zero-shot Pass@1 of up to 72.33%, while one-shot prompting further improves the translation effectiveness. These results suggest potential for quantum code translation by LLMs.

</details>


### [57] [Ethics by Configuration: AI Ethics Principles in Coding Agent Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34847)
*Haoyu Gao,Peerachai Banyongrakkul,Marc Cheong,Christoph Treude,Sebastian Baltes,Mansooreh Zahedi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究检视了 40,079 个 GitHub 仓库中的配置文件，分析了开发者如何在代码代理程序的配置文件（如上下文文件和技能）中体现 AI 道德原则。结果显示，虽然 39.4% 的文件和 61.5% 的仓库中包含道德相关陈述，但这些陈述主要关注责任、透明度、隐私保护和可靠性等领域，且更常见于任务特定文件（如技能）中。定性分析发现了 30 类道德考量，强调的是开发者、维护者和社区成员在开发流程中的关注点，而非公众或最终用户。道德内容主要存在于 prose 格式的文件中，但代理程序是否遵循这些说明尚未得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 代码代理程序成为软件开发中的关键组成部分，配置文件的应用日益广泛。然而，目前尚不清楚 AI 道德原则在开发者配置代码代理程序时是否以及如何得到体现。因此，研究旨在量化道德陈述的存在率，揭示编码配置文件中存在的道德考量，并确定这些考量所关注意味的对象。

Method: 收集了来自 8,893 个 GitHub 仓库的 40,079 个配置文件。利用一个经验证的大型语言模型分类器，自动识别出包含道德相关陈述的文件。进一步对 702 个文件进行了定性分析（分层抽样），共涉及 679 个文件，以揭示道德考量的具体类别和相关利益方。

Result: 道德相关陈述出现在 39.4% 的文件和 61.5% 的仓库中。定量分析表明，道德陈述集中在责任、透明度、隐私保护和可靠性四个方面，并且在任务特定文件（如技能）中比在项目范围内的上下文文件中更为常见。定性分析共识别出 30 类道德考量，它们通常表现为对代理程序的具体规则。这些考量显示出不同的关注重点：责任主要体现为对人工审批的要求；透明度则体现为解释和诚实地报告代理程序的工作；公平性则体现为可访问性和本地化。与此同时，道德考量主要关注开发者、维护者和社区成员在开发流程中的关注点，而非公众或最终用户。道德相关内容主要存在于 prose 格式的文件中，但代理程序是否遵循这些说明尚未得到验证。

Conclusion: 编码配置文件中的道德指导是有限的，且主要关注开发者和维护者，而非公众或最终用户。这表明当前的配置实践与公共道德框架之间存在差距。此外，由于缺乏对代理程序遵守这些指示情况的测试，道德规则的有效性也受到质疑。因此，建议从业者、研究人员和政策制定者共同努力，以增强配置文件的道德完整性，并开发验证代理程序遵守这些指示的方法。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become integral to software development, projects increasingly utilise configuration files, such as context files and skills, to govern agent behaviour. While AI ethics principles are widely used in research and policy, little is known about whether and how they are reflected when developers configure coding agents. We studied ethics principles in 40,079 configuration files across 8,893 GitHub repositories. Using a validated large language model (LLM) classification, we measured the prevalence of ethics-related statements, and qualitatively analysed 702 stratified samples across 679 files to uncover the ethical considerations reflected in these files and the parties they concern. Ethics-related statements appeared in 39.4% of files and 61.5% of repositories; concentrated on accountability, transparency, privacy protection, and reliability; and occurred more often in task-specific files such as skills than in project-wide context files. Our qualitative analysis identified 30 categories of ethical considerations, which take the form of concrete rules for the agent. Accountability appeared mainly as requirements for human approval, transparency as explain and honestly report the agent's work, and fairness as accessibility and localisation. These considerations reveal a difference in focus. Whereas public ethics frameworks aim to protect society and end users, configuration files mainly concern developers, maintainers, and community members in the development workflow. Ethics-related content appeared mainly in prose-based files, and whether agents follow these instructions remains untested. We discuss the implications for practitioners, researchers, and policymakers.

</details>


### [58] [AIPC: Evidence-Guided Agent Workflows for PyTorch Project Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34890)
*Zhanwei Wu,Jianhao Su,Brian Zhang,ShengTing Huang,Weidong Feng,Gang Liu,Zhiguo Li,Wei Huang,Li Tan,Wanjun Li,Zejiong Teng,Changgeng Zhong,Guanping Wu,Han-Hsing Tu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了AIPC方法，通过结合结构化技能和工具链/运行时/ profiling 证据，实现 PyTorch 推理项目在现场 NPU上的跨层诊断与修复，涵盖模型适配、工件构建、目标机执行、精度修复和性能优化。在 Qualcomm QAIRT 路径下的视觉、语言、 LLM 和扩散模型上评估，实验显示技能能显著提升成功率和速度，且在约定部署下可实现零人工介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专用加速器（特别是现场 NPU）上部署 PyTorch 推理项目超出了使用框架设备 API（如 CUDA）的范围，必须转换为硬件运行时工件并适配目标工具链和应用接口。模型格式、量化流程、算子支持及工具链接口的差异跨越了 PyTorch/ONNX、工具链、运行时和设备执行等多层，造成跨层问题。虽然 LLM 代理可调用工具解决这些问题，但因域知识不足、配置维修空间大或路径选择差而可能偏离。

Method: AIPC（AI Porting & Conversion）结构化代理方法，将结构化技能与工具链、运行时和 profiling 证据相结合，驱动跨层诊断与修复。它将模型适配、工件构建、目标机执行、精度修复和性能优化统一到一个部署流程中。在 Qualcomm QAIRT 的现场 NPU 路径下，针对视觉、语言、LLM 和扩散模型进行评估。

Result: 两个优化案例中：Whisper-Small 有 6/7 次运行符合预定义的精度和延迟标准，对照组为 0/6；YOLO-Worldv2 实现端到端 2.791 倍加速，对照组为 1.599 倍。在预先部署合同下，部分运行在技能、工具权限和检查条件约束下实现零人工介入（0-HITL）。此外，还检查了 Qwen3.8-27B 作为本地部署模型推动代理工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: AIPC 有效统一了 PyTorch 推理项目在现场 NPU上的跨层适配与优化流程。技能驱动的方法在成功率、加速比和自动化程度上均优于传统对照方法。Qwen3.8-27B 的本地部署展示了用于驱动 Agent 工作流的可行性，表明 AIPC 在实际工程场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Deploying and optimizing a PyTorch inference project on a dedicated accelerator, especially an on-device NPU, requires more than using a framework device API as with CUDA. The project must be converted into hardware-runtime artifacts and adapted to the target toolchain and application interface. Differences in model formats, quantization flows, operator support, and toolchain interfaces create cross-layer problems spanning PyTorch/ONNX models, toolchains, runtimes, and device execution. LLM Agents can invoke tools to address such problems, but may drift because of insufficient domain knowledge, large configuration and repair spaces, or poor path selection.
  We propose AIPC (AI Porting & Conversion), an Agent method for deploying PyTorch inference projects. AIPC combines structured Skills with toolchain, runtime, and profiling evidence to drive cross-layer diagnosis and repair, organizing model adaptation, artifact building, on-target execution, accuracy repair, and performance optimization into one deployment flow. We evaluate AIPC on the on-device NPU path of Qualcomm QAIRT across vision, speech, LLM, and diffusion models. In two optimization cases, 6/7 Skill-arm Whisper-Small runs meet predefined accuracy and latency criteria versus 0/6 control runs, while YOLO-Worldv2 achieves a 2.791x end-to-end speedup versus 1.599x for the control. Under a pre-fixed deployment contract, Skill, tool permissions, and check conditions, some runs achieve zero in-run human intervention (0-HITL). We also examine Qwen3.8-27B as a locally deployable model for driving an Agent workflow.

</details>


### [59] [SemWeaver: Refining LLM-Generated Static Vulnerability Checkers with Analyzer-Internal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34928)
*Chengzhi Yi,Shenao Wang,Yanjie Zhao,Xiao Cheng,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SemWeaver improves LLM-synthesized static-analysis checker refinement by using internal analyzer evidence (control-flow graphs, symbolic values, memory regions, checker callbacks) combined with source context and validation feedback, achieving higher warning-positive vulnerable revisions (23 vs 15) and improved version-level F1 (0.448 to 0.575) on 39 KNighter-derived CSA checkers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing refinement loops only use compilation errors and validation reports which describe symptoms, not root causes. Patch-based checker synthesis matches surface edits but misses deeper semantic conditions. Internal analyzer evidence already contains the necessary information to address this gap but is unused.

Method: SemWeaver is a plug-in refinement layer that extracts patch-relevant analyzer evidence (CFGs, symbolic values, memory regions, checker callbacks) and assembles it with source context and validation feedback into a structured, provenance-labelled bundle. An iterative editing loop refines checkers on vulnerable and fixed revisions, guarded by a retention gate that accepts warning recovery or false-positive reduction while preventing over-pruning.

Result: On 39 KNighter-derived CSA checkers from Linux kernel fixes, SemWeaver reached 23 warning-positive vulnerable revisions (vs 15 for KNighter's loop) and improved version-level F1 from 0.448 to 0.575. Ablation removing internal evidence recovered fewer cases and produced different edits, showing complementary gaps. Additional experiments validated robustness across models, CodeQL queries, and source variations.

Conclusion: SemWeaver effectively leverages internal analyzer evidence to refine LLM-synthesized checkers, significantly improving detection of true vulnerabilities and overall F1 scores. The approach demonstrates that combining multiple evidence sources addresses complementary recognition gaps in semantic condition understanding.

Abstract: Recent work shows that LLMs can synthesize static-analysis checkers from security patches, but a compilable checker is not necessarily a correct one. A patch reveals what changed, not why the change is safe, so generated checkers often match only the surface edit and miss the deeper semantic condition. Existing refinement loops feed back compilation errors and validation reports, yet these signals describe symptoms without explaining what the checker misunderstands about the code. We observe that the analysis framework itself already computes the information needed to close this gap. Control-flow graphs, symbolic values, memory regions and checker callbacks are available inside the analyzer but unused by current refinement strategies. SemWeaver is a plug-in refinement layer that extracts patch-relevant analyzer evidence and assembles it with source context and validation feedback into a structured, provenance-labelled bundle. An iterative editing loop refines the checker on both the vulnerable and fixed revisions, guarded by a retention gate that accepts warning recovery or false-positive reduction while preventing over-pruning. We evaluate SemWeaver on 39 KNighter-derived CSA checkers from Linux kernel fixes. With analyzer evidence, refinement reaches 23 warning-positive vulnerable revisions (vs. 15 for KNighter's loop) and raises version-level $F_1$ from 0.448 to 0.575. An ablation removing internal evidence recovers fewer silent cases and produces different checker edits, indicating that the two evidence configurations address complementary recognition gaps. Additional experiments cover three editor models on a 12-subject subset, a CodeQL query refinement, and controlled source-form variations. A qualitative follow-up on all 11 non-tied comparisons traces each warning-count change to a specific checker repair, such as recognizing a lowered callback or unwrapping an expression cast.

</details>


### [60] [What Will Replace Pull Requests? Rethinking Software Collaboration for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34933)
*Christoph Treude,Sebastian Baltes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI编程代理导致拉取请求激增，开源项目开始限制外部贡献，研究调查了替代方案及对实证软件工程研究的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编程代理大量生成拉取请求，传统拉取请求机制面临压力，部分开源项目限制或禁用外部贡献，需要了解从业者提出或采用的替代方案及其对实证研究的影响。

Method: 回顾了31个组织或个人在2025年10月至2026年10月期间发布的50份灰色文献，编码每个文献提到的问题、提出或采用的机制、接管的五种拉取请求角色（集成、审查、基本原理、问责、外部贡献）以及提供的证据。

Result: 大多数响应保留了某种形式的拉取请求（通过增加工具、附加新工件或限制访问），限制访问是最常报告采用的响应。部分提案将审查和基本原理转移到代理对话和会话记录中，但只有Zed报告实际使用了替代品。仅有两份文献提出外部贡献的替代方案，且均未报告采用。拉取请求的变化将无法被现有数据集捕捉：代理对话中的审查不留下PR评论，禁用PR的项目从新开PR样本中消失。

Conclusion: 拉取请求正在发生变化，现有数据集无法捕捉这些变化；提出了研究议程，包括新兴替代方案的有效性、对开源参与的影响以及实证方法的适应。

Abstract: The emergence of artificial intelligence (AI) coding agents has put pull requests under pressure: agents open millions of pull requests per month, while some open-source projects have begun to restrict or disable external contributions. We investigate which alternatives to pull requests practitioners are proposing or adopting and what these changes mean for empirical software engineering research. We reviewed 50 gray-literature documents from 31 organizations or individuals published between October 2025 and October 2026, coding the problems each named, the mechanisms it proposed or adopted, which of five roles of pull requests (integration, review, rationale, accountability, and external contribution) each mechanism takes over, and the evidence offered. Most responses retained pull requests in some form, whether by adding tooling, attaching a new artifact, or restricting access, and restriction was the response most often reported as adopted. Several proposed alternatives moved review and rationale into agent conversations and session records, but only Zed reported using a replacement in practice. Only two documents outlined a replacement for external contribution, and neither reported adoption. Pull requests are changing in ways that pull-request datasets will not capture: reviews conducted in agent conversations leave no pull-request comments, and projects that disable pull requests disappear from samples of newly opened ones. We outline a research agenda on the effectiveness of the emerging alternatives, their consequences for open-source participation, and the adaptation of empirical methods.

</details>


### [61] [How Do Coding-Agent Harnesses Self-Evolve? An Empirical Study of Retrospective Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34938)
*Christoph Treude,Sebastian Baltes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究 retro 技能让代理审查会话并提出 harness 改进，结果显示大多数提议关联具体会话事件，18/43 lint 相关提议普适性强。尽管实施并评估了多项改进，但大多数在后续任务中未能减少重复工作，部分甚至引入 unrelated issues，说明 harness 自我进化需建立验证与修正机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 coding agent 的 harness 自适应能力是否能在后续任务中改进行为，以及改进提议对减少重复工作的影响。

Method: 在 30 个 SWE-bench Verified 问题上运行 Claude Code，收集 125 条 retro 提议，质性编码后实施 24 条，并针对 12 条改进在有无改进的 fresh 会话中进行对比评估。

Result: 大多数提议引用会话事件，18/43 lint 提议推荐普适实践。七次针对性评估中，两项改进消除了目标动作，一项减少发生频率，四项无显著效果，部分改进增加了 unrelated issues 的工作量。

Conclusion:  harness 自我进化单靠生成和实施改进不足，必须建立机制来评估改进在后续任务中的有效性，并能修正或剔除无效改进。

Abstract: Coding agents can adapt their harnesses, the software, configuration, and instructions used to run them, based on experience from earlier tasks, but whether these adaptations improve their behavior on later tasks remains unclear. We study \retro, a skill that asks an agent to review a completed session and propose harness changes, and ask what changes it proposes and whether implemented changes reduce repeated work on later tasks. We ran Claude Code on 30 SWE-bench Verified issues, qualitatively coded the 125 proposals from \retro, implemented 24, and evaluated 12 harness changes on later issues from the same repositories in fresh sessions with and without each change. Most proposals referred to an event in the session, but 18 of 43 lint-related proposals recommended a practice regardless of what had happened. Among the seven evaluations in which sessions without the change encountered the targeted problem, two changes eliminated the targeted action, one reduced its occurrence, four showed no reduction, and some changes added work on unrelated issues. Harness self-evolution requires more than generating and implementing changes: it needs ways to establish whether a change helps on later tasks and to revise or remove changes that do not.

</details>


### [62] [Reasoning Patterns for Efficient AI Coding: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34943)
*Zhe Yin,Yuling Shi,Xiaodong Gu,Beijun Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型在编程任务中的推理模式，将推理痕迹转化为结构化动作序列，发现了四种通用的推理模式（设计‑‑实现、状态追踪、证据‑‑修复‑‑重核查和语义映射），并识别了支持和阻碍有效推理的积极和消极模式。通过基于模式的任务识别、模式引导提示和模式感知早期停止，验证了这些模式在分析和控制AI编码器推理行为方面的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型推理模型（LRM）能够生成详细的推理痕迹，但这些痕迹中潜在的模式及其对分析和控制编码推理的指导作用仍不清楚，这限制了系统化的表征和推理过程控制。因此，需要对编程任务中的推理模式进行系统性的实证研究。

Method: 对四个代表性编程任务进行了实证研究。将推理痕迹转化为结构化动作序列，并通过任务内支持、跨任务对比以及有效‑‑无效支持对比挖掘了重复片段。研究了通用推理模式，包括设计‑‑实现、状态追踪、证据‑‑修复‑‑重核查和语义映射，并区分了支持和阻碍有效推理的积极和消极模式。通过基于模式的任务识别、模式引导提示和模式感知早期停止来展示其应用。

Result: 所提出的模式特征实现了对推理痕迹的准确任务识别，无需访问任务描述或最终答案。受正模式指导的提示提高了下游任务的总体准确率，而受负模式指导的早期停止则显著减少了API报告的token使用量，同时仅略微降低了pass@1。所有三个应用均能推广到以前未见过的模型上。

Conclusion: 推理模式是分析和控制AI编码器行为的有力工具。通过将推理痕迹转化为可操作的洞察，我们展示了如何识别、指导和优化LRM在编程任务中的推理过程，为更透明和可控的AI推理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) generate long reasoning traces that expose intermediate inference behavior, offering opportunities to study and control reasoning in AI coding. However, the underlying patterns in these traces and their potential for analyzing and guiding coding models remain poorly understood, limiting systematic characterization and inference-time control of LRM reasoning. We conduct an empirical study of reasoning patterns across four representative coding tasks. We transform reasoning traces into structured action sequences and mine recurring fragments using within-task support, cross-task contrasts, and valid--invalid support contrasts. We characterize four general reasoning patterns by coding agents: design-to-implementation for code generation, state tracing for execution reasoning, evidence--fix--recheck for debugging, and semantic mapping for translation. We further identify positive and negative patterns associated with valid and invalid reasoning traces, respectively. Positive patterns reflect verification-driven convergence, whereas negative patterns reflect repeated design revision, reflection, and knowledge recall without closure. To demonstrate their practical utility, we evaluate three pattern-based applications: task identification, pattern-guided prompting, and pattern-aware early stopping. Features capturing general reasoning patterns enable accurate task identification from reasoning traces without accessing task descriptions or final answers. Prompting guided by positive and negative patterns improves the overall accuracy of downstream tasks, while negative pattern-aware early stopping substantially reduces API-reported token usage with only a modest decrease in pass@1. All three applications also generalize to previously unseen models, showing that reasoning patterns can support both the analysis and control of LRM behavior in AI coding.

</details>


### [63] [Rivet: Utility-Preserving Context Control for Repository-Level Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34967)
*Ruwei Pan,Linhao Wu,Yakun Zhang,Zixiong Yang,Lu Zhang,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rivet框架，旨在解决仓库级代码生成中的“上下文效用稀释”问题。在有限的上下文预算下，Rivet通过信号接地、效用链接和预算塑造来保留关键的仓库证据，从而提高代码生成的准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成需要仓库证据（如目标位置、辅助定义、调用者用法和配置约束），但受限于上下文预算，现有方法主要依据任务相似性、长度或结构邻近度选择上下文，无法确保选中的上下文包含这些核心证据，导致“上下文效用稀释”（看似相关却遗漏关键依赖）。
