Method: Rivet框架包含三个核心步骤：1) 信号接地（Signal Grounding）：融合词汇、语义和结构信号选择任务相关的代码锚点；2) 效用链接（Utility Linking）：通过依赖、使用和配置关系扩展这些锚点以收集仓库证据；3) 预算塑造（Budget Shaping）：按优先级打包证据，在预算紧张时将代码片段简化为签名或摘要后再丢弃。

Result: 在DevEval和RepoExec两个基准上，使用4个骨干模型、2种上下文预算（4K和8K token）以及8个基线进行评估。Rivet在所有16个设置中均达到最高Pass@1，比最强基线平均提高了3.73至5.15个百分点。此外，使用Rivet的GPT-5-mini在两个基准上均优于未使用Rivet的GPT-5，同时推理成本降低了73.3%至84.5%。

Conclusion: Rivet通过结构化、证据驱动的上下文选择和打包策略，有效解决了上下文效用稀释问题，是提升仓库级代码生成性能和成本效益的高效方案。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation asks an agent to implement a task requirement inside an existing repository. To produce code that works in the repository, the agent needs repository evidence, such as target locations, helper definitions, caller usages, and configuration constraints, which determine how the code should be located, integrated, and executed. Because the context budget cannot hold the entire repository, existing retrieval, compression, and structure-aware methods select context by task similarity, context length, or structural proximity, but these criteria do not directly measure whether the selected context contains this evidence. As a result, the selected context can appear relevant to the task yet miss the evidence that the implementation depends on. We call this failure mode context utility dilution. In this paper, we propose Rivet, a context control framework that preserves repository evidence for coding agents under a fixed context budget. Rivet, first uses Signal Grounding to select task-related code anchors by fusing lexical, semantic, and structural signals. Utility Linking then expands these anchors through dependency, usage, and configuration relations to collect repository evidence. Finally, Budget Shaping packs the evidence by priority and, when the budget is tight, reduces snippets to signatures or summaries before dropping them. We evaluate Rivet, on two executable repository-level benchmarks, DevEval and RepoExec, with four backbone models, two context budgets (4K and 8K tokens), and eight baselines. Rivet, achieves the highest Pass@1 in all 16 settings and improves over the strongest baseline by 3.73--5.15 percentage points on average. Moreover, GPT-5-mini with Rivet, outperforms GPT-5 without Rivet,on both benchmarks while reducing inference cost by 73.3%--84.5%.

</details>


### [64] [Toward Methods and Tools for Critical Examination of Disparate Systems for Discovery of Hidden Dependencies in Engineering of Autonomous and Semi-Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34974)
*Assaf Marron,David Harel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法、初始AI技能包及新兴结果，旨在增强软件工程师在新领域开发自主或半自主系统时的域知识获取与系统视角应用能力。核心是通过主动对抗性批判相关环境下异类系统的视频，帮助工程师和AI工具发现交互、依赖和环境假设，这些在项目文档中往往未被明确表达，从而提升目标系统质量、个人技能与组织实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决软件工程师在陌生领域开发自主系统时，难以获取和应用域知识与系统化工程视角的问题，传统项目文档往往无法显性化记录关键的交互、依赖与环境假设，导致系统质量与工程实践受限。

Method: 方法核心是主动对抗性批判：工程师和自动化AI工具观看相关环境下的异类系统视频，寻找其中的交互、依赖与环境假设，并将其映射到目标系统中，旨在挖掘项目文档中未被明确表达的隐性知识。

Result: 结果表明，该方法及其支持的工具可以提升目标系统的质量，增强个体工程师的技能水平，并优化组织的工程实践。新兴结果显示，该 approach 在实际场景中具有提升系统可靠性和工程效率的潜力。

Conclusion: 结论是，所提出的方法和工具有效解决了新领域系统开发中的知识获取瓶颈，能够显著提升目标系统质量、工程师个人技能及组织工程实践的整体水平。

Abstract: We present a methodology, an initial AI Skill package, and emerging results, for enhancing software engineers' acquisition and application of domain knowledge and system-wide engineering perspectives towards the development of an autonomous or semi-autonomous system in a domain that may be new to them. At the core of the approach is a process involving proactive adversarial critiquing of videos of disparate systems in related environments. Among other things, the engineers and automated AI tools look for interactions, dependencies, and environment assumptions manifested in the observed systems, which are applicable also to the target system, but that otherwise might remain unarticulated in project artifacts. The approach and its supporting tools can enhance the quality of the target system, the individual engineer's skills and the organization's engineering practices.

</details>


### [65] [V$^3$AST-Bench: Verifiable, Valid, and Versatile Safety Evaluation for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35188)
*Weitao Feng,Hengheng Xiong,Zihao Zeng,Jun Chen,Jiyang Guan,Xiaofeng Wang,Jialing Tao,Hui Xue,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: V³AST-Bench评估大语言模型作为代理人时的实际安全风险，发现即使具备高能力，也会在约21%任务中造成已验证的危害，说明风险认知不足以保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过代码执行等行为带来难以从文本判断的安全风险，需要能够验证真实后果的评估方法，以区分避免危害和任务无法完成的区别。

Method: 构建V³AST-Bench，包含212个涵盖18类的风险任务，使用可执行的运行环境验证伤害与结果，并设置配对的能力衍生任务检验工作流能力，对11款模型在5套框架下进行86,496次实验。

Result: 即使像Claude Opus 4.8配合Claude Code，也在20.9%的任务中导致已验证的危害，且不同模型在相似能力下表现出显著安全差异，说明单纯的任务能力不足以保证安全，且对风险的显性认知并未能可靠防止危害。

Conclusion: 当前安全评估仅依赖风险认知不足，必须在模型执行全过程实施安全机制，而非仅在前期识别风险。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly act through code, modifying repositories, executing programs, and interacting with services in agent form. Their actions introduce risks that cannot be assessed from generated responses alone. Safety evaluation must verify actual consequences, distinguish harm avoidance from task incapability, and cover realistic workflows. We introduce V$^3$AST-Bench, a benchmark for Verifiable results, Valid interpretation, and Versatile risk coverage of Agent Safety. It comprises 212 risk tasks spanning 18 categories. Dedicated runtime environments verify harm and task outcomes through executable checks over states, records, and artifact behavior, while paired capability twin tasks verify workflow competence. Our experiments span 51 combinations of 11 models and five harnesses, spanning 86,496 trials and over 9 billion tokens of model interaction. Results show that V$^3$AST-Bench remains challenging even for frontier coding agents, as Claude Opus 4.8 with Claude Code still causes verified harm on 20.9% of tasks. Across model-harness combinations, agents with comparable competence exhibit markedly different safety outcomes, showing that task capability alone does not guarantee safe execution. More importantly, even explicit recognition of risks does not reliably prevent harmful actions, revealing a critical gap between risk awareness and safe execution. These findings highlight the need to move beyond risk recognition toward enforcing safety throughout execution.

</details>


### [66] [Behavioral Regression Detection for Pull Requests via Structured Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35223)
*Yiwen Fu,Binhang Qi,Xiao Li,Runlin Liu,Hailong Sun,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ORION是一个分阶段的PR级回归检测框架，通过提取PR意图、AST影响分析、合成结构化行为预言机、生成可执行测试并差分运行，实现了比现有基线更高的F1分数，并在大规模真实PR上发现了多个已确认的回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件回归是在代码修改中引入的非预期行为变化，在PR级别难以检测。现有方法依赖不完整的测试或直接用LLM分析PR，缺乏结构化的行为推理和显式预言机。

Method: ORION分阶段工作：1)提取PR意图并通过AST影响分析识别受影响函数；2)从PR意图、静态风险信号和代码变更中合成函数级结构化行为预言机（编码保留/变更行为、设置约束、控制流提示）；3)在预言机指导下生成可执行测试，进行预言机覆盖率引导的增强和静态修复；4)在修改前后代码上差分运行测试；5)用确定性规则和受约束的LLM推理分类PR。

Result: 构建了首个Python PR回归基准RegBench-Py（154个验证的真实PR）。ORION在DeepSeek-Chat-V3.2上达F1 0.6125，Claude-4.5-haiku上达0.5578，优于Testora、Codex、Claude Code等基线。相比PatchGuru，ORION支持更广泛的多函数PR，在单函数情况下仍具竞争力。在1,459个已合并PR上发现9个可复现回归候选，8个经维护者确认。每PR约15分钟，适合CI/CD筛选。

Conclusion: ORION通过结构化预言机和分阶段推理，有效解决了PR级回归检测中的预言机缺失和推理不足问题，在真实世界基准和大规模部署中均表现出色，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: Software regressions are unintended behavioral changes introduced by code modifications and are hard to detect at pull-request (PR) level. Existing methods rely on incomplete tests or directly analyze PRs via LLMs, lacking structured behavioral reasoning and explicit oracles. We present ORION, a staged framework for PR-level regression detection. ORION extracts PR intent and identifies impacted functions via AST-based impact analysis, then synthesizes function-level structured behavioral oracles encoding preserved/changed behaviors, setup constraints and control-flow hints from PR intent, static risk signals and code changes. Guided by these oracles, ORION generates executable tests, conducts oracle-coverage-guided augmentation and static repair, and differentially runs tests on pre- and post-change code. It classifies PRs with deterministic rules and constrained LLM reasoning. We build RegBench-Py, the first Python PR regression benchmark with 154 verified real-world PRs. ORION achieves F1-scores of 0.6125 (DeepSeek-Chat-V3.2) and 0.5578 (Claude-4.5-haiku), outperforming baselines including Testora, Codex and Claude Code. Compared with PatchGuru, ORION supports broader multi-function PRs while remaining competitive on single-function cases. On 1,459 merged PRs, ORION finds 9 reproducible regression candidates, 8 confirmed by maintainers. It takes ~15 minutes per PR, showing practicality for CI/CD screening.

</details>


### [67] [Chart the Invisible: Interactive Perception for Hidden-Target Exploration in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35237)
*Yongxiang Hu,Ruiyang Liu,Qining He,Xuan Wang,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对 LLM 代理在 GUI 任务中难以通过滑动探索隐藏内容的问题，提出了滚动区域感知模型 Hagasu，并构建了包含 667 张 UI 截图的数据集，实验显示可显著提升依赖探索任务的完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 代理在受限视口（尤其是移动设备）下难以发现滑动后才显露的任务相关目标，导致在 47.8% 需要滑动的场景中错误选择点击，影响可靠执行。

Method: 手动采集 667 个 UI 屏幕构建 Hagasu 数据集，训练首个多模态滚动区域感知模型，在动作选择前识别可滚动区域以辅助隐藏区域探索。

Result: 模型可正确识别 78.5% 的可滚动区域；在九个 GUI 代理的实验中，依赖探索任务的端到端完成率从 64.8% 提升至 82.7%。

Conclusion: 增强 GUI 代理的滚动区域感知能力能有效解决隐藏内容探索问题，显著提升复杂 GUI 任务的成功率，并为后续研究开源了滑动数据集与模型代码。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in command-line interface (CLI) tasks, recent LLM-based agents still struggle with reliable task execution through graphical user interfaces (GUIs). A key challenge is the limited content visible within a bounded viewport, especially on mobile devices, where many task-relevant targets remain hidden and can only be revealed through swipe interactions. Therefore, although most GUI agents support swipe as an action primitive, the lack of target visibility makes it difficult for them to decide when, where, and how to swipe to reveal the hidden content. Our quantitative study further shows that GUI agents incorrectly choose a click action in 47.8% of cases that require swipe exploration. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes equipping GUI agents with scrollable-region perception to support hidden-area exploration before action selection. To this end, we trained the first multimodal model for scrollable-region perception on a manually collected dataset of 667 UI screens, called Hagasu. By correctly identifying 78.5% of scrollable regions, experiments across nine GUI agents show that integrating Hagasu improves the mean end-to-end completion rate from 64.8% to 82.7% on explore-dependent tasks. To facilitate follow-up research, we release the swipe dataset and the source code of Hagasu.

</details>


### [68] [Testing with Coding Agents: A Large-Scale Comparison of Agent-Supported and Human-Authored Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35297)
*Roberto Milanese,Francesco Salzano,Hovhannes Muradyan,Jingjing Wu,Remo Pareschi,Fausto Fasano,Mattia Fazzini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在仓库内部比较，agent-supported PR的改动测试代码的 odds 是 human-authored 的 1.65 倍。agent-supported PR更频繁添加测试（OR 1.50-1.58），较少修改（OR 0.70-0.76）或删除（OR 0.51-0.77）。测试结构、oracle 及覆盖率无显著差异，但在测试-only PR中，其测试对被覆盖代码的覆盖率较低。测虑味的差异边缘显著，但未在单个测试方法层面复现。汇总跨项目比较可能掩盖或反转 agent 的真实影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试工作在开发者和 coding agents 之间如何分配，影响长期可维护性。需要比较 agent-supported 与 human-authored PR 在测试变更、类型、结构、味道和覆盖率等五个维度上的差异。

Method: 分析 2025 年 1 月至 2026 年 4 月间 391 个 Java 仓库中合并的 114,134 个 PR，通过 PR 和 commit 信号识别 5 个 coding agent。利用混合效应模型在控制变化规模的情况下，在含有混合 PR 类型的仓库内比较 agent-supported 与 human-authored PR。

Result: pooled 跨仓库比较中，两组几乎同样频率改动测试代码；但在仓库内，agent-supported PR 改动测试的 odds 高 1.65 倍。agent-supported PR 更常添加测试，较少修改或删除。测试结构无显著差异，oracle 使用亦无差异。在 95 个可构建的仓库中，production 变更的覆盖率无显著差异；但 test-only PR 中，agent 的测试覆盖率较低。测试味道在边缘层面有差异，但未在单方法层面复现。

Conclusion: agent 介入关联测试工作模式的不同，跨项目汇总比较可能掩盖或反转其影响。agent 与测试质量的关系仍不确定。

Abstract: Context. Coding agents increasingly contribute tests through pull requests that developers then maintain, so how testing work is divided affects long-term maintainability.
  Objective. We compare pull requests in which developers and coding agents collaborate (agent-supported) with pull requests without detectable agent involvement (human-authored) along five dimensions: the frequency and extent of test changes, their type and context, test structure, test smells, and code coverage.
  Method. We analyze 114,134 pull requests merged between January 2025 and April 2026 in 391 Java repositories, identifying five coding agents through pull-request-level and commit-level signals. Since agent adoption varies across projects, we compare the two groups within repositories with mixed-effects models that control for change size.
  Results. Pooled across repositories, both groups change test code almost equally often; within repositories, the odds that an agent-supported pull request does so are 1.65 times higher. Agent-supported pull requests add tests more often (odds ratios 1.50-1.58) and modify (0.70-0.76) and delete (0.51-0.77) them less often. We detect no difference in most structural characteristics of the tests they add, which more often have an oracle, nor, in the 95 repositories we could build, in the coverage of their production changes; in test-only pull requests, however, their tests cover less of the code they exercise. Differences in test smells are borderline and not reproduced for individual test methods.
  Conclusions. Agent participation is associated with a different mix of testing work, which pooled comparisons across projects can hide or reverse; its relationship with test quality remains uncertain.

</details>


### [69] [RepoTrace: Improving Requirement Tracing in Multi-Round Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35333)
*Huanyu Liu,Yezheng Cheng,Jinliang You,Tianxiao Huang,Linyu Li,Zheng Fang,Peng Cao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Repotrace解决了代码智能体在多轮迭代中因需求与代码关联模糊而出现的性能退化问题。它通过仓库结构结合需求语义构建需求-代码对应关系，并将其注入智能体上下文，使智能体能遵循仓库架构、跨轮次追踪需求并识别核心代码元素。实验表明，Repottrace显著提升MPR（如GLM-5.2上+19.99），Terminus-2上将每轮奖励从0.163提升至0.371，平均回归错误降低56.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体在多轮交互后性能下降，主要因为无法区分需求的修改目标与无关代码，导致维护迭代修改时出错频发。

Method: 构建Reqtoretrace，核心是利用仓库结构和需求语义构建需求-代码对应关系，并通过注入对应关系的方式将仓库架构信息传递给智能体。具体包括：分析EvoCode-Bench上2,656条轨迹，识别出'需求聚焦错位'作为典型失败模式；从仓库结构+需求语义出发建立需求到代码的结构性映射；将映射结果作为上下文注入智能体提示；使智能体具备追踪需求变迁能力。

Result: Repotrace在不同模型和Agent harness上均显著提高MPR，以GLM-5.2为 例获+19.99的MPR提升，Claude Code获+10.22提升。在Terminus-2+DeepSeek-V4-Pro上，全轮次平均每轮奖励从0.163增至0.371，回归错误降低56.78%。具体数据支撑了效果：最多项级中至少四项达到+10以上，且最强模型报告最高提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作成功验证了需求驱动式仓库理解对代码智能体的关键作用。将需求语义融入智能体上下文能有效缓解多轮交互中的目标混淆问题，显著减少回归错误并提升每轮任务完成质量，为代码智能体在长期维护场景下的稳健性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Code agents have become increasingly capable of performing software engineering tasks through interaction with software environments. However, agents tend to degrade as the number of interaction rounds increases, making it difficult to maintain existing software during iterative modification. We analyze 2,656 agent trajectories in EvoCode-Bench and identify requirement focus misalignment as a recurring failure pattern, where agents struggle to distinguish the primary modification targets of a requirement from unrelated code.
  We propose Repotrace a requirement-driven repository understanding harness that combines repository structure with requirement semantics to construct a requirement-code correspondence and injects it into the agent context. This correspondence enables code agents to follow the repository architecture, trace requirements across multi-round interactions, and identify the core code elements corresponding to the current requirement. Experiments show that Repotrace improves the mean per-round reward (MPR) across different models (e.g., +19.99 on GLM-5.2) and agent harnesses (e.g., +10.22 on Claude Code). Across rounds, Repotrace increases the average per-round reward from 0.163 to 0.371 on Terminus-2 with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, while reducing average regression errors by 56.78%. We release the source code and agents' trajectories to support future research.

</details>


### [70] [Toward Evidence-Based Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35348)
*Sebastian Baltes,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析代理式编程工具实验中模型与框架（harness）混杂问题，指出现有研究难以分离两者效果，并呼吁采用固定版本的参考框架及可独立版本化的扩展来隔离特征效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式编程工具的任务成功率与执行成本同时取决于模型和框架，框架负责上下文管理、工具提供和智能体循环。现有实证研究常同时变更模型与框架，导致无法隔离框架的独立影响，威胁结论可靠性。

Method: 对2025—2026年发表的146篇全文同行评议论文中的325个实验进行定性标注，考察报告配置及比较是否在框架配置/版本、模型、任务和用户交互等维度上隔离框架影响。

Result: 仅15个实验报告了所用框架的精确版本，5个报告了运行时间，31个共享了所有会话的执行轨迹。在226个比较框架的实验中，35个未将框架单独变更，表明可复现性与隔离性普遍不足。

Conclusion: 研究提出参考框架的需求，呼吁社区报告实验的所有自由度，并建议会议围绕特定参考框架版本开设专辑或专题，固定框架版本并通过独立版本化的扩展变更特征，以隔离特征效果及其交互。

Abstract: Task success rates and execution costs of agentic coding tools depend on both model and harness. The harness manages context, provides tools, and runs the agent loop. In empirical software engineering (SE) studies, comparisons that change model and harness together cannot isolate their effects. We qualitatively annotated 325 experiments reported in 146 peer-reviewed full papers published between 2025 and 2026. We examined the reported configurations and whether comparisons isolated harness effects, including harness configuration and version, model, tasks, and user interaction. The exact version of every harness used was reported for only 15 experiments. For five, the paper reported when the experiments were run, and for 31, authors shared execution traces for every session. Of the 226 experiments comparing harnesses, 35 never varied the harness in isolation. Based on this analysis, we derive requirements for a reference harness that the SE community can use for experiments. We call on the community to report all degrees of freedom of experiments with agentic coding tools and on SE venues to pilot dedicated tracks or special issues around a specified reference harness release. In these tracks, papers would hold the harness version fixed and vary features through separately versioned extensions to isolate feature effects and test their interactions.

</details>


### [71] [Making Small Code LLMs More Efficient for Secure Code Review with Neuro-Symbolic Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35477)
*Mostafijur Rahman Akhond,Mahmuda Khatun,Gias Uddin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探讨小型开放权重代码模型是否能通过微调将静态分析器的安全证据转化为开发者可验证的解释。提出 secSemScan 与 OGSM 数据集，对 1,993 个 C/C++ 函数进行约束模式的安全独白生成，涵盖 22 种 taint/lifetime CWE。微调后，Qwen2.5-Coder-7B verdict 从 4.8% 提升至 96.6%，DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 达 78.9%，StarCoder2-7B 达 43.7%，表明微调有助于模型产出结构化、证据相关的安全推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析器只识别程序位置而缺乏解释，语言模型的解释又难以验证。小型开放权重模型可在本地部署，无需发送专有代码，但需微调以将证据转化为可检查的安全说明。

Method: 提出 secSemScan，将静态分析器与本地部署模型配对，生成约束模式的安全独白，命名程序站点、分配 CWE 并给出机器可解析 verdict。引入 OGSM 数据集，包含 1,993 个 C/C++ 函数，涵盖 22 种 taint/lifetime CWE，每条记录 pairing 函数与分析器导出或修复 diff 导出的证据及独白。分两阶段微调三种开放权重代码模型：首阶段输出格式，次阶段独白内容。评估提示供给 verdict 与 evidence，以衡量模型利用信息的能力而非独立检测。

Result: 在 355 条测试记录上，Qwen2.5-Coder-7B verdict 从 4.8% 提升至 96.6%；DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 达 78.9%；StarCoder2-7B 达 43.7%。Qwen2.5-Coder-7B 引用了 87.5% 的 Tier 1 oracle 站点，其引用的每一行均属于 oracle 证据。

Conclusion: 微调有助于小型模型产出更结构化、证据相关的安全推理，使其能生成可供开发者和工具检查的安全独白。

Abstract: As AI coding assistants generate more code, the need to review it for security flaws grows. Existing approaches leave a gap: static analyzers identify program sites but offer little explanation, while language-model explanations can be difficult to verify. Small open-weight code models could make security review available without sending proprietary code to a hosted service. We study whether fine-tuning helps these models turn security evidence into explanations that developers and tools can check. We present secSemScan, which pairs a static analyzer with a locally deployable model that writes a schema-constrained security monologue naming program sites, assigning a CWE, and giving a machine-parseable verdict. We also introduce OGSM (Oracle-Grounded Security Monologue), a dataset of 1,993 C/C\texttt{++} functions covering 22 CWEs in the taint and lifetime families. Each record pairs a function with analyzer-derived or fix-diff-derived evidence and a monologue. We fine-tune three open-weight code models in two stages, first on the output format and then on the monologue content. Because evaluation prompts supply the verdict and evidence, our results measure how well models use that information, not whether they detect vulnerabilities independently. On 355 test records, fine-tuning raised Qwen2.5-Coder-7B's verdict accuracy from 4.8\% to 96.6\%; DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B reached 78.9\%, and StarCoder2-7B reached 43.7\%. Qwen2.5-Coder-7B cited 87.5\% of Tier~1 oracle sites, and every line it cited belonged to the oracle evidence. The results suggest that fine-tuning helps small models produce more structured, evidence-linked security reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [Understanding Python Linters through a Comprehensive and Systematic Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35485)
*Qingyun Yang,Zejun Zhang,Dapeng Yan,Tian Zhang,Zhenchang Xing,Yi Li,Zhiming Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在本文介诺如下：三使用的 Python 仇漫器(Ruff, Pylint, Flake8, Mypy, Black) 对中的大规模大小度统考，分析了 1,551 个仇漫视图观，指定了三个类型，柿充flt,
50% 以上的视图是共用的，Ruff 和 Pylint 合带的视图覆打了 80% 以上，
测量还包括跑程速度，配置方式和越大的 GitHub 项目中使用情况，面弹出了元标用户参考和自动配置的提出，


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 其主要求是在小格游中小游给用户的三个关注：（1）共人用户关注，（2）跑程速度，（3）配置方式，（4）使用情况和使用列列阶升，为静式静化给用户以及为具有给用户参考为身辑注意提出。

Method: 从三使用的 Python 仇漫器(Ruff, Pylint, Flake8, Mypy, Black) 开始，安潮给个 1,551 个仇漫视图，分成三个类型，柿充flt；测量在三个类型中的库范合关注，安潮小金珠程度，（单视图默认，全视图），在三个类型中小游 1,000 个 GitHub 项目，统考跑程速度，小金珠输出，对个 443 个配置选项给用户的三个方式和三个类型，小游给个 3,094 个仇漫器用户的 GitHub 项目，对使用情况和使用列度，

Result: 仇漫视图：Ruff 和 Pylint 合带的仇漫视图覆打了 80% 以上，50%+ 个视图是共用的，Ruff 会在非类型选中小金珠最快，速度排序：Ruff 〉 Mypy 〉 Flake8 〉 Black 〉 Pylint，
配置：三个类型用户统考 4 个方式，443 个选项给用户 4 个类型，三个仇漫器得到提出。


Conclusion: 这个细究给用户统营三个仇漫器，得到常用户参考，为常用户，技术发程和使用列度（例如信值项路），提出常用户给用户参考和为常用户给用户参考提出，包括个仇漫视图小金珠和使用列度和使用列度的库范合细究，为开始细究给用户为给用户提出和为常用户平台靠提出來检稿，

Abstract: Code linters, tools that automatically detect code issues, help developers accelerate development and improve code quality by identifying errors early. Unlike previous studies that focused mainly on single linters or informal comparisons, this paper presents the first large-scale empirical study of five widely used Python linters: Ruff, Pylint, Flake8, Mypy and Black, through four key user concerns: linting rule checks, speed, configuration methods, and linter preferences. We analyze 1,551 linting rules, categorize them into five types, and construct rule mappings, finding that more than 40% are shared between multiple linters, with Ruff and Pylint together covering over 80% of all rules. Pylint has the most rules in the first two categories, and Ruff leads in the last three. Next, we measure their running times on 1,000 GitHub projects under three setups (single-rule, default, all-rules), finding a consistent speed ranking from fastest to slowest: Ruff, Mypy, Flake8, Black, and Pylint. Ruff is extremely fast, with runtimes typically within one second. Then, we summarize four configuration methods and four types of configuration options from 443 available options across the five linters. While all linters support the four configuration methods, they differ in project configuration formats and flexibility in their configuration options. Finally, an analysis of 3,094 GitHub projects using the five linters reveals that over 50% use multiple linters, share strong consensus on option selections and rule suppressions, but exhibit project-specific variations in enabled-rule configurations. Our findings provide actionable insights for linter users and establish a benchmark dataset and taxonomy to support future research, such as cross-linter rule mapping, performance modeling, and automated configuration.

</details>


### [73] [FeatureLoop: Benchmarking Coding Agents under Evolving Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35488)
*Jia Feng,Alyssa Wu,Cuiyun Gao,Yunlong Wang,Songyuanyi Lu,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FeatureLoop 是一个新基准，包含 61 个来自 34 个存储库和 5 种主要编程语言的任务，用于评估软件开发代理；它跟踪多轮需求和失败反馈下的任务完成情况。评估了 12 个前沿模型，发现最佳模型可完成 72.1% 的任务，但仅有 15.4% 的失败阶段能在两次修改内恢复。分析揭示了不完整修复、回归和模型特定的反馈利用/令牌效率问题，这些问题限制了低干预的可靠开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估代码生成代理在典型开发工作流程中的表现，包括需求演变和故障反馈，这与现有的仅限于最终正确性的指标不同。

Method: 创建 FeatureLoop 基准，包含 61 个人工审阅的任务，涵盖五个编程语言和七个软件形式。每个任务在持续的工作区中通过添加/澄清需求（演变反馈）和报告失败（完成反馈）来推动。执行检查持续跟踪新行为、修复和已保存的功能。使用 12 个前沿模型进行评估，并分析其成功率、失败阶段恢复、修复不完整性、回归、反馈利用和令牌效率。

Result: - 最强模型完成 72.1% 的任务（两种反馈类型均存在）。- 仅 15.4% 的失败阶段能在两次修改内恢复。- 观察到不完整的修复和回归。- 不同的模型在反馈利用和令牌使用方面存在差异。

Conclusion: FeatureLoop 揭示了开发代理面临的挑战：尽管在最佳情境下表现出色，但代理在有限修改内修复失败的能力有限，存在不完整的修复和回归问题。此外，模型特定的反馈处理效率不高，限制了低干预、可靠软件生成的实现。

Abstract: Software development with coding agents often begins with a rough request that users refine after trying the implementation. Final correctness alone does not capture the repeated guidance this process requires. We introduce FeatureLoop, a benchmark of 61 human-reviewed tasks from 34 repositories across five primary programming languages and seven software forms. Each task follows one development goal in a persistent workspace through evolution feedback, which adds or clarifies requirements, and completion feedback, which reports failures of stated requirements. Cumulative executable checks track new behavior, repair, and preservation of earlier functionality. We evaluate 12 frontier models and find that the strongest completes 72.1% of tasks with both feedback types. Across models, however, only 15.4% of observed failed stages recover within two corrective revisions. Further analysis reveals incomplete repairs, regressions, and model-specific differences in feedback use and token efficiency, identifying obstacles to reliable, low-intervention development.

</details>


### [74] [The Hidden Costs of Forgetting: A Study of Trajectory Deletion in Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35496)
*Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz,Islem Bouzenia,Michael Pradel,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件工程智能体每轮重发整条轨迹，促使在线轨迹压缩。实证研究显示，删除大量轨迹内容（如推理文本100%）可保持有效性，但必须有延迟；即时删除会显著降低效果。代理会通过重新获取补偿缺失内容，增加轮次和token。成本降低效果取决于定价模型：扁平定价下26/42配置成本下降，但缓存感知定价下则因破坏低费率缓存前缀而未见降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体每轮重发整条轨迹，从而促使在线轨迹压缩。既往研究表明随机删除旧轮75%的token可能保持代理有效性，但仍不清楚为何以及代理如何补偿缺失内容，以及是否实际降低成本。

Method: 本文实证研究了42种轨迹压缩配置，聚焦有效性、代理行为和成本。系统探索通过 varying scope, granularity, amount 和 timing 从旧轮删除内容，在9,400次运行中使用两个 state-of-the-art agents 在 SWE-bench Verified。

Result: 发现删除大量轨迹内容（如推理文本100%）可保持整体有效性。重要的是，必须有延迟应用删除，即时删除显著降低有效性。代理常通过重新获取缺失内容来补偿，增加轮次和input/output token。无论是否降低成本取决于定价模型：在扁平定价模型下，26/42评估中估计成本下降。令人惊讶的是，在缓存感知定价模型下，零评估产生更低成本，因为减少轨迹会破坏以较低费率计费的缓存前缀。

Conclusion: 本文发现三个启示：首，token计数可能具有误导性；轨迹压缩技术需考虑现实定价模型下的总成本。二，分析轨迹对于理解代理行为至关重要，因为端到端有效性掩盖了如过度重获取等差异。三，这些结果突显了未来在内存管理工作的需求，以在不牺牲有效性的情况下降低成本。

Abstract: Software engineering agents resend their entire trajectory to an LLM at every turn, motivating online trajectory reduction. Prior work shows that randomly deleting 75% of tokens in old turns may preserve agent effectiveness, but it remains unclear why, how agents compensate for missing content, and whether it actually reduces costs. This paper presents an empirical study of 42 trajectory-reduction configurations, focusing on effectiveness, agent behavior, and cost. We systematically explore deleting content from old turns by varying the scope, granularity, amount, and timing of deletion across 9,400 runs with two state-of-the-art agents on SWE-bench Verified. We find deleting large amounts of trajectory content (e.g., up to 100% of reasoning text) can preserve overall effectiveness. Importantly, such deletion must be applied with a delay, as immediate deletion tends to significantly degrade effectiveness. Agents often compensate for deleted content by re-fetching it, increasing both the number of turns taken and the input/output tokens. Whether trajectory reduction reduces costs depends on the pricing model: Under a flat pricing model, estimated costs fall in 26 of 42 evaluations. Surprisingly, under a caching-aware pricing model, zero evaluations yield lower costs, since reducing the trajectory breaks the cached prefix billed at a lower rate. Our findings have three implications: First, token counts alone are misleading; trajectory reduction techniques must consider overall costs under a realistic pricing model. Second, analyzing trajectories is crucial to understand agent behavior, as end-to-end effectiveness hides differences such as excessive re-fetching. Finally, these results highlight the need for future work on memory management to reduce costs without sacrificing effectiveness.

</details>


### [75] [Ranking Function Generation with LLMs for Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35523)
*Rongkang Li,Jiaxin Hu,Taolue Chen,Yong Li,Fu Song,Zhilin Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RAINFALL是一种神经符号框架，用于自动合成循环终止性排名函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动构造排名函数具有挑战性，且需要较强专业知识；将LLM与符号验证结合，可探索更自动的终止证明方法。

Method: RAINFALL采用生成-验证-精炼范式，将排名函数合成建模为代码补全任务，在输入程序循环中插入占位符，约束LLM填充四个预定义槽位；随后由符号验证器检查，若失败则把验证器诊断转换为自然语言反馈，引导LLM迭代改进。

Result: 在两个公开基准上包含358个终止C程序中，RAINFALL使用MiniMax V2.7合成可验证排名函数，成功率为90.22%，优于多数基线；尤其单独相比PROTON 2.1多解决12个程序；组合RAINFALL与PROTON 2.1后成功率提高到96.65%。消融实验也确认了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: RAINFALL展示了一种通用且可靠的自动排名函数合成路径；其思路或可扩展到更广泛的形式验证辅助生成，如LLM辅助合成可形式检查的活性证书等方向。

Abstract: Ranking functions are a fundamental technique for proving loop termination, but constructing them manually is challenging and requires substantial expertise. We propose RAINFALL, a neuro-symbolic framework which follows the generate-verify-refine paradigm and tightly integrates LLMs with symbolic verification. RAINFALL formulates ranking function synthesis as a code-completion task by inserting placeholders into the input program and constraining the LLM to fill four predefined slots for each loop. The completed program is then checked by a symbolic verifier. If verification succeeds, a valid ranking function is obtained; otherwise, the verifier's diagnostics are translated into natural-language feedback that guides iterative refinement of the LLM's output. We evaluate RAINFALL on two public benchmark suites comprising 358 terminating C programs. Using MiniMax V2.7, RAINFALL synthesizes verifiable ranking functions for 323 programs (90.22%), outperforming all promising baselines. In particular, it solves 12 more programs than PROTON 2.1, the best-performing baseline and winner of the SV-COMP 2026 Termination category. Furthermore, combining RAINFALL with PROTON 2.1 increases the success rate to 96.65% (346/358 programs). We also conduct ablation study confirming individual contributions of placeholder contracts, few-shot examples, symbolic verification, and diagnostic information feedback. Experiments across LLMs further confirm the effectiveness of RAINFALL. Our results suggest a generic, sound framework for ranking-function synthesis and, beyond termination, LLM-assisted synthesis of formally checkable liveness certificates, both of which represent new directions of software engineering research.

</details>


### [76] [FLOWSTEP: A Framework for Learning to Predict GitHub Actions Workflow STEPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35650)
*Jiangnan Huang,Bin Lin,Yutaro Kashiwa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出FLOWSTEP框架，用于GitHub Actions工作流步骤补全，并区分Action与Script生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD配置复杂，现有Transformer方法将工作流步骤视为同质生成任务，忽略了复用Action与项目私有Script的不同知识类型。

Method: FLOWSTEP结合大语言模型与对比学习，显式建模Action和Script生成，并探索LLM-as-a-judge进行语义评估。

Result: 在基准测试上，端到端工作流步骤预测准确率从38.23%提升到51.09%；最佳配置与人工标注的Krippendorff's alpha达0.701。

Conclusion: FLOWSTEP通过区分Action和Script提升流程补全，LLM-as-a-judge可作为可扩展评估补充，但后续仍需验证实际部署效果。

Abstract: Continuous Integration and Delivery (CI/CD) are fundamental to modern software development, yet configuring pipelines such as GitHub Actions (GHA) workflows remains complex and error-prone. Recent work has explored Transformer-based models for GHA workflow recommendation, but existing approaches typically model workflow steps as a single homogeneous generation task. This formulation overlooks a key distinction: reusable Actions are structured, ecosystem-level components, whereas project-specific Scripts encode local build, test, and deployment semantics. Consequently, current models struggle to capture the different forms of knowledge required for accurate workflow completion. In this work, we present FLOWSTEP, a framework for GHA workflow step completion that explicitly distinguishes between Action and Script generation. FLOWSTEP combines large language models (LLMs) with contrastive learning to better capture semantic relationships among workflow contexts, Actions, and Scripts. Evaluated on established benchmarks, FLOWSTEP improves end-to-end workflow step prediction accuracy from 38.23% to 51.09%. Beyond prediction accuracy, we further study whether LLM-as-a-judge can provide a more semantic evaluation of generated workflow steps. By comparing LLM-based assessments with human annotations, we find that the best-performing model configuration reaches substantial agreement with human ground truth (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.701), suggesting that LLM-as-a-judge can serve as a scalable complement to manual evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Queuing for Inference: Dynamic Memory Footprints and Costly Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33923)
*Jiahui Ni,Vishrant Tripathi*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新的排队模型，分析Transformer推理工作负载中动态内存足迹、头阻塞及内存依赖抢占开销的影响。研究表明，Gittins-index策略在考虑抢占开销时可能导致性能下降，因此提出Multi-Level-Priority-Threshold (MLPT)策略，在限制抢占的同时保持短作业优先。通过表征MLPT下的平均响应时间并给出阈值优化的经验经则，揭示了在缩短短作业响应时间与限制抢占开销之间的平衡是最优调度的关键，为内存受限排队系统中的推理任务调度提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based推理工作负载因动态内存足迹、重尾请求分布引起的头阻塞以及内存依赖的抢占开销而面临独特挑战。现有的Gittins-index策略在标准M/G/1中最小化平均响应时间，但在考虑抢占开销的模型中性能可能下降，这 motivates the need for new scheduling policies that balance priority and overhead control。

Method: 提出Multi-Level-Priority-Threshold (MLPT)调度策略，通过设定优先级阈值来限制抢占行为，同时保持对短作业的优先权。研究对MLPT下的平均响应时间进行了数学表征，并提供了解决阈值优化问题的经验经则。

Result: 实证和理论分析表明，最优调度是短作业响应时间与抢占开销之间的权衡。MLPT策略在有效抑制预emptions开销的同时，保留了优先级机制，展现出优于传统Gittins-index策略的系统稳定性和调度效果。

Conclusion: 研究建立了内存受限排队系统中推理任务调度的理论框架。关键观察表明，有效的调度需在提升短作业响应时间和控制抢占开销之间取得平衡，这为实际系统的调度设计提供了经验指导。

Abstract: Modeling inference workloads for transformer-based neural networks poses unique challenges due to their dynamic memory footprints, head-of-line blocking caused by heavy-tailed request distributions, and memory-dependent preemption overhead arising from the need to load/unload cache state. In this paper, we introduce a new queuing model that captures the impacts of these features on system stability and scheduling design. We show that the Gittins-index policy, which minimizes mean response time in the standard M/G/1 setting, can incur substantial preemption overhead in our model. Motivated by this limitation, we propose the Multi-Level-Priority-Threshold (MLPT) policy, that limits preemption while preserving priority for short jobs. We characterize the mean response time under MLPT and provide empirical rules-of-thumb to solve the resulting threshold-optimization problem. Our key observation is that optimal scheduling is a balance between reducing the response times of shorter jobs and limiting the overhead incurred when preemption occurs. These results provide a theoretical framework for understanding scheduling in memory constrained queuing systems for inference tasks.

</details>


### [78] [FastSAR: High-Throughput, High-Fidelity Open-Source SAR Image Formation on CPUs, GPUs and TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33932)
*Paul Singerman,Tom Braun*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: FastSAR 是一个用于 SAR 图像形成的开源库，支持 CPU、NVIDIA GPU 和 Google TPU，提供因式分解、精确反投影和极坐标格式，以及厂商相位历史文件读取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确反投影太空 SAR 数据需要每个像素对每个 pulse 进行插值读取，例如 Panama 水坝 Umbra 图像达到 1.6 万亿次读取。

Method: FastSAR 采用因式分解反投影、精确反投影和极坐标格式，以统一 Python 接口和相位历史文件读取器实现 SAR 成像。

Result: Panama 图像因式分解成像时间分别为 16-vCPU 实例 11.2 秒、NVIDIA L4 3.0 秒、H100 0.6 秒、TPU v6e 2.9 秒；与 float64 精确反投影相比误差为 -56 到 -63 dB。L4 成本最低，为每千幅图像 0.59 美元。精确反投影速度与 MatX 相当，因式分解算法比 MatX 快 3.8 倍；极坐标格式在 L4 上 1.4 秒，误差 -32 dB。五个 Capella 采集在三种模式下，最差区域误差低于 -59 dB。

Conclusion: FastSAR 展示了面向 CPU、GPU 和 TPU 的高效 SAR 图像形成能力，在速度、精度和成本上具有竞争优势。

Abstract: Exact backprojection of a spaceborne SAR collection needs one interpolated read per pixel for every pulse; for the 12,207 by 8,808 pixel Umbra image of the Panama Canal this balloons to 1.6 trillion reads. FastSAR is an open-source library for SAR image formation on CPUs, NVIDIA GPUs and Google TPUs, with factorized backprojection, exact backprojection and polar format behind one Python interface and readers for the vendors' phase-history files. Its factorized kernels form the Panama image in 11.2 s on a 16-vCPU instance, 3.0 s on an NVIDIA L4, 0.6 s on an H100 and 2.9 s on a TPU v6e, with errors of -56 to -63 dB relative to a float64 exact backprojection in three regions over six cloud devices; the L4, a modest GPU, gives the lowest cost of the six at 0.59 US dollars per thousand images. At exact backprojection FastSAR is as fast as the MatX backprojection example, by far the fastest open-source implementation found (10.9 against 11.6 s at the same accuracy, with MatX's pixel coordinates in float64); its factorized algorithm is 3.8 times faster than MatX, and its polar format forms the image in 1.4 s on the L4 at -32 dB. On five Capella collections in three modes the worst-region error is below -59 dB.

</details>


### [79] [One Megakernel is Not Enough: Predicting Where to Cut for Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35336)
*Aditya Chatterjee,Anand Joseph X M,Sanjay Singh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文探讨了流式推理中核融合（Megakernel）的最佳配置方案，提出了一种无需端到端运行即可通过模拟图结构来预测和选择最佳多Megakernel组合（或全 conventional 图）的框架。通过对NH-TTS等模型的测试，证明了保留多个专用Megakernel能显著提升H100 GPU上的延迟和吞吐量，并提出了四种模型分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的做法通常是在低批量大小下使用单一的巨型核（monolith megakernel），或者仅将部分组件融合为巨型核而其余部分保留为传统图结构。然而，核融合的边界应该延伸到何处、哪些边界值得保留，尚缺乏系统性的研究。本文旨在寻找全融合与传统图执行之间的最佳平衡点，以优化流式推理的延迟和吞吐量。

Method: 提出了一种自动框架，该框架利用传统图的性能剖析和独立内核基准测试结果，无需端到端运行候选设计即可模拟候选方案的 tile 和事件图，从而预测首次输出时间（TTFA）并自动选择最佳的多 Megakernel 组合配置。此外，根据模型随批量大小变化的最佳组合特征，将模型分为四类（稳定型、递减型、递增型、规避型）。

Result: 在13种模型和批量大小的组合中进行测试，最佳配置为2至4个巨型核组合，或完全传统的图执行，无一为单一巨型核。在H100 NVL GPU上，以NH-TTS模型在批量大小为32时为例，保留三个专用巨型核的方案相比单一巨型核，平均批量P95首音频输出时间（TTFA）降低了21.4%，吞吐量提高了28%；相比传统的TensorRT图执行，引擎级TTFA p95降低了4.05倍，吞吐量提升了19.7%。

Conclusion: 最优的核融合配置并非单一的巨型核，而是多个专用巨型核的组合或完全传统的图结构。在流式推理中，合理保留特定的核边界（即拆分为多个专用巨型核）能够同时实现更低的延迟和更高的吞吐量。

Abstract: We address a fundamental question: How far should kernel fusion extend and which boundaries are worth preserving? Unlike the general convention of using one monolith megakernel at lower batch sizes or supporting only a selective model component as megakernel and rest of the model as conventional graph, we show that the best configuration is a composition of multiple megakernels with no overlapping conventional graph component, or a fully conventional graph. We present a framework that selects megakernel regions in a streaming inference model from conventional-graph profiles and standalone kernel benchmarks, without running any candidate design end to end. It simulates each candidate's tile and event graphs to predict time to first output and selects the measured best design in all 13 combinations of model and batch size we tested. Across NH-TTS, Qwen3-TTS and Llama-1B, the best composition ranges from two to four megakernels to fully conventional execution and none is a single megakernel. We introduce four model categories (megakernel-stable/ receding/ advancing/ averse) that capture how a model's best megakernel composition changes with batch size. Using NH-TTS model on H100 NVL GPU, we demonstrate that preserving three specialized megakernels can improve both latency and throughput despite introducing additional kernel boundaries. At batch size 32, this design delivers 21.4% lower mean batch-p95 time to first audio (TTFA) and 28% higher throughput than the evaluated monolith megakernel. Against conventional TensorRT graph execution, it reduces engine-level mean batch TTFA p95 by 4.05x and increases throughput by 19.7%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Double KV Cache Optimization on Apple Silicon: Block-FP4, Radial Quantization, and Fused Metal Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33076)
*Ibrokhimsho Abduchaborov*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 摘要在苹果M5 Max上评估五种KV缓存路径，发现融合Metal极化量化（PolarQuant）在短上下文中吞吐最高且压缩率高，但32K以上所有压缩方案因反复重建完整缓存和累积O(N²)解码开销而性能崩溃，64K甚至因超出Metal缓冲区限制而OOM；长文本可行性需要直接在压缩KV上进行融合SDPA并配合分块预分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存容量和内存带宽是长上下文自回归推理的主要瓶颈，需要在统一内存硬件上验证低比特KV缓存（FP4、径向/极化量化）的实际收益，并将表示层压缩与核心/分配器影响区分开来。

Method: 在M5 Max上使用MLX实现并对比五条缓存路径：bf16原生基线、分块缩放E2M1 FP4、Python极化量化参考（含QJL风格残差）、FP4到极化的顺序组合、融合Metal极化内核；测量吞吐（tok/s）、每Token字节数和压缩比，并观察32K与64K长上下文下的表现。

Result: 融合Metal PolarQuant达46.8 tok/s，3456 B/token，较bf16基线压缩3.64倍，比Python极化参考（25.4 tok/s）快1.84倍，略优于块FP4（41.5 tok/s，3.62倍压缩）。顺序FP4+极化组合无收益（4241 B/token，15.1 tok/s）。32K以上所有压缩变体跌至1.5–14 tok/s；64K时Metal因申请33.5 GB超出MLX 22.6 GB缓冲区限制而OOM。

Conclusion: 当打包、旋转与重建置于单设备核心时，径向编码可具有竞争力，但现有注意力路径反复重建完整缓存导致累积O(N²)解码工作，使低比特KV缓存优势不延伸至长上下文；长文本可行性要求直接对压缩KV进行融合缩放点积注意力并配合分块预分配。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache capacity and memory traffic are primary bottlenecks in long-context autoregressive inference. We evaluate five cache paths on an Apple Silicon M5 Max using MLX: a native bf16 baseline, block-scaled E2M1 FP4, a Python reference implementing radial quantization with a QJL-style residual, sequential FP4-to-Polar composition, and a fused Metal Polar kernel. On Apple Silicon M5 Max, fused Metal PolarQuant achieves 46.8 tok/s at 3,456 B/token - 3.64x compression versus the bf16 baseline and 1.84x faster than the Python Polar reference (25.4 tok/s), while slightly outperforming block-FP4 (41.5 tok/s at 3.62x compression). Sequential composition of FP4 and Polar (Double Optimization) yields no additive benefit: 4,241 B/token at 15.1 tok/s, worse than FP4 alone on both axes. The fused path establishes that radial coding can be competitive when packing, rotation, and reconstruction are placed in one device kernel, but this result does not extend to longer contexts. At 32K and above, all compressed variants collapse to 1.5-14 tok/s because the current attention path repeatedly reconstructs the full cache, retaining cumulative O(N^2) decode work. At 64K, the Metal variant runs out of memory when a 33.5 GB allocation exceeds MLX's 22.6 GB Metal buffer limit. Fused scaled dot-product attention operating directly on compressed KV, together with chunked pre-allocation, is required for long-context viability. These results separate representation-level compression from kernel and allocator effects and identify the conditions under which low-bit KV caching helps on unified memory hardware.

</details>


### [81] [AnalogPortBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Analog Circuit Porting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33088)
*Saeid Rajabi,Aly Moussa,Vishal Saxena,Chengmo Yang,Satwik Patnaik*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AnalogPortBench提供54个跨工艺节点的模拟电路端口任务，评估六个大语言模型在端口、供电降低和规格硬化等情境下的表现，发现模型擅长简单端口但难以重新设计受供电裕度影响的放大器，且更依赖仿真反馈而非更多样本或推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电路设计仍依赖专家知识，且在新工艺节点迁移时存在瓶颈；随着供电降低，原设计可能失效，现有LLM基准仅针对单一工艺且假设规格可行，缺乏真实的端口挑战。

Method: 构建包含10个电路、54项任务的AnalogPortBench，涵盖从直接端口到降低供电、更严格规格乃至不可行规格的全过程，并提供五个工艺角的参考设计；对六个前沿LLM在两种推理层级以及有无仿真反馈的条件下进行评估。

Result: 模型能够端口简单电路，但在供电裕度敏感的放大器重新设计、识别不可行规格方面表现不佳，且通过仿真反馈显著提升性能，优于仅增加样本或推理层级。

Conclusion: 需发展更具备电路重新设计能力的LLM，并结合仿真反馈来弥补当前基准的局限，推动自动化模拟电路设计的进展。

Abstract: Analog circuit design still relies heavily on expert knowledge, and porting proven designs to new process nodes is a recurring bottleneck. As a lower supply reduces available voltage headroom, a working topology may require redesign or fail to meet specifications. While large language models (LLMs) have begun to generate and optimize analog circuits, existing benchmarks focus on one process node and assume feasible specifications. To bridge this gap, we introduce AnalogPortBench, a benchmark of 54 tasks for porting ten circuits, from current mirrors to telescopic amplifiers, from a 0.18 $μ$m process to the open-source SKY130 PDK. Tasks range from a direct port through a reduced supply and harder specifications to infeasible ones, and every feasible task has a reference design verified at five process corners. Evaluating six frontier LLMs at two reasoning levels, with and without simulation feedback, we find that models port simple circuits but rarely re-design headroom-sensitive amplifiers, seldom recognize infeasible specifications, and gain more from simulation feedback than from more samples or reasoning. Code and data are available at https://github.com/saeidrajabii/AnalogPortBench.

</details>


### [82] [Operating System Support for Accelerators as First-Class Compute Elements in Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33380)
*Mohammad Shahidzadeh,Lesley Shannon*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究针对异构SoC中操作系统将硬件加速器（HA）视为外设而非专用计算单元（CE）而产生的高软件开销（即“操作系统外设税”）问题，提出了基于FPGA的MEERKAT框架。通过将操作系统支持部分迁移至硬件，并引入虚拟内存感知通信、轻量级调用、中断策略自适应（IPA）及HA感知调度等技术，实现了高达260倍的通信加速和2至6倍的整体性能提升，强调了将加速器作为一等计算资源、构建以加速器为中心的操作系统的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构系统芯片（SoC）越来越多地利用特定领域的硬件加速器（HA）来获得超越通用处理器的性能和能效。然而，现有操作系统（如Linux）将HA视为外设，导致在通信、调用、中断和调度等方面产生严重的软件开销（即“操作系统外设税”），从而大幅降低了硬件加速的实际效益。

Method: 研究团队构建了MEERKAT，一个基于FPGA的框架，用于将Linux操作系统的部分支持功能迁移至硬件中，以识别、研究并量化操作系统税的影响。为此，提出了四项关键技术：虚拟内存感知（VMA）通信方法以实现高效的应用数据访问；轻量级HA调用接口；支持动态轮询和中断选择的中断策略自适应（IPA）技术；以及能够动态在CPU和多个HA实例间分发工作负载的HA感知调度器。

Result: 实验结果显示，通信速度提升了高达260倍，整体运行时间（wall-clock time）相比传统调度器实现了2至6倍的性能提升。此外，研究揭示了系统整体性能不仅取决于HA的计算速度，还高度依赖于操作系统开销、资源可用性以及调度决策。

Conclusion: 研究表明，为了充分发挥异构系统的性能潜力，必须将硬件加速器（HA）视为一等计算资源（first-class computational resources），并推动以加速器为中心（accelerator-centric）的未来操作系统设计。

Abstract: Heterogeneous systems-on-chip (SoCs) increasingly rely on domain-specific hardware accelerators (HAs) to deliver performance and energy efficiency gains beyond what general-purpose processors can provide. However, operating systems (O/S), such as Linux, assume that processors are the Compute Elements (CEs) and HAs are peripheral devices rather than specialized CEs. As a result, HA execution incurs substantial software (SW) overheads that reduce the effective benefits of hardware (HW) acceleration.
  This work demonstrates that significant inefficiencies arise from ``O/S peripheral device taxes'' (O/S taxes), including SW stack-accelerator communication, invocation, interrupt handling, and scheduling. We built MEERKAT, a Field-Programmable Gate Array (FPGA)-based framework for configurable multicore systems that include HAs; it allows the user to migrate Linux O/S support from SW to HW and identify, study, and quantify the impact. Here we study four major sources of O/S tax and introduce techniques to mitigate them. These include a virtual-memory-aware (VMA) communication method that enables efficient HA access to application data, a lightweight HA invocation interface, Interrupt Policy Adaptation (IPA) for dynamic polling and interrupt selection, and an HA-aware scheduler that dynamically distributes work across Central Processing Units (CPUs) and multiple HA instances.
  Experimental results demonstrate up to $260\times$ faster communication and $2$--$6\times$ performance improvements in wall-clock time over conventional schedulers. Furthermore, our study reveals that overall system performance depends not only on HA speed, but also on O/S overheads, resource availability, and scheduling decisions. These findings highlight the importance of treating HAs as first-class computational resources and motivate a more accelerator-centric O/S design for future heterogeneous systems.

</details>


### [83] [EGGPlacer: Energy-Guided Generative Chip Placement with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33099)
*Qi Zhou,Guojun Liu,Juanhong Zhao,Guangzhi Qi,Zehao Chen,Ming Lu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: EGGPlacer是一种能量引导的扩散框架，用于宏单元 placement，通过整合分析放置反馈和热反馽提升放置质量，同时优化线长和温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 芯片 placement显著影响布线线长和热行为，但在宏单元放置阶段难以评估这些下游指标。现有方法要么忽略标准单元连接和空间需求（如基于学习的宏单元 placers优化 mHPWL），要么忽略热效应（如分析混合规模 placers优化可微导线长度和密度）。因此需要一种新方法在放置阶段同时考虑线长、密度和热效应。

Method: EGGPlacer引入一个分析性 placer作为在线物理响应模型，用于暴露由每个中间宏单元放置引发的标准单元分布和全网表线长代理；同时引入一个可微热模型提供温度反馽。这些信号被构建为可组合的能量函数，并在逆扩扩扩过程通过，无需重新训练底层生成模型。

Result: 在公开的 placement基准测试上，EGGPlacer与强大的分析和学习方法相比，获得了有竞争力的混合规模 HPWL。端到端评估中，EGGPlacer实现了最低的平均 routed 线长和峰值温度。消融研究表明，分析和热引导在线长代理、布线线长和热目标上提供了不同且互补的益处。

Conclusion: EGGPlacer通过引入分析放置反馽和热反馽作为可组合能量，有效提升了宏单元 placement的质量，实现了线长和温度的显著优化，不仅保持了与先进方法的竞争力，还在最终布线线长和热性能上取得了最优，验证了该方法在实际物理设计中的有效性。

Abstract: Chip placement strongly affects routed wirelength and thermal behavior, yet these downstream metrics are difficult to evaluate during macro placement. Learning-based macro placers commonly optimize the macro-only half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL), denoted mHPWL, which ignores standard cell connectivity and spatial demand. In contrast, analytical mixed-size placers optimize differentiable full netlist wirelength and density objectives but are typically unaware of thermal effects. We propose EGGPlacer, an energy-guided diffusion framework that incorporates analytical placement feedback and thermal feedback into macro placement generation. An analytical placer acts as an online physical response model, exposing the standard cell distribution and a full netlist wirelength proxy induced by each intermediate macro placement, while a differentiable thermal model provides temperature feedback. These signals are formulated as composable energies and injected throughout reverse diffusion without retraining the underlying generative model. Experiments on public placement benchmarks show that EGGPlacer achieves competitive mixed-size HPWL relative to strong analytical and learning-based macro placers. In the end-to-end evaluation, EGGPlacer also obtains the lowest average routed wirelength and peak temperature among the evaluated methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate that analytical and thermal guidance provide distinct and complementary benefits across wirelength proxies, routed wirelength, and thermal objectives.

</details>


### [84] [Fast Path, Exact Path: One Library of Hardwired Operators for Streaming and Exact Canny Edge Detection on a Low-Cost FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33107)
*Raj Mali*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FPGA的Canny边缘检测滞后处理硬件实现，利用统一3×3算子库同时支持快速近似与精确滞后，显著降低延迟并提高吞吐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滞后边缘跟踪需多遍扫描，难以单遍流式处理，现有FPGA方案或近似或需额外硬件，限制实时性

Method: 设计一组具有相同两周期延迟的硬连线3×3算子，通过K级流水线实现逐像素流式处理；在片上BRAM中以128×128块为单位，用原地前向与反向扫描替代双缓冲迭代

Result: 在Artix-7 XC7A35T上，快速路径延迟262–720 µs，比30 fps帧处理器快46–127倍；精确路径保留96.1%的精确边缘，边界F-measure保持98.6%以上；滞后步骤加速6.6–21.7倍，整体加速4.5–9.9倍；资源仅1455 LUTs，输出与软件模型逐位一致

Conclusion: 该方案在低延迟、高吞吐实时视频场景中优势明显，适用于嵌入式视觉，但多核CPU在处理已存储图像时仍更快

Abstract: Hysteresis edge tracking, the final stage of Canny edge detection, follows edge chains of unbounded length and therefore resists single-pass streaming; FPGA designs either approximate it or dedicate separate labelling hardware to it. We show that a single library of hardwired 3x3 operators, each with an identical two-clock latency, delivers both a camera-rate approximation and exact hysteresis on a low-cost Artix-7 (XC7A35T). The fast path streams camera pixels through the operators and K local hysteresis stages and commits each edge decision 262.15-720.63 microseconds after the pixel arrives (K = 1-8; system simulation with a datasheet camera model), that is, 3 + K camera lines later and 46-127x sooner than a frame-based processor can even begin at 30 frames per second. It provably never reports an edge that exact hysteresis rejects, recovers 76.6-96.1% of exact edges at thresholds 20/40, and on the 200 BSDS500 test images retains at least 98.6% of the exact path's boundary F-measure (ODS 0.470-0.477 versus 0.476). The exact path holds 128x128 tiles in block RAM, is exact within each tile, and replaces two-buffer iteration with in-place forward and reverse sweeps built into the same operator. This reduces hysteresis from 38-82 passes to 3-7 on pattern and photo tiles, accelerating the hysteresis step 6.6-21.7x (40x on a worst-case tile) and a complete tile 4.5-9.9x, with identical output. Both paths are bit-exact with software golden models at every pipeline stage and close timing at 50 MHz in at most 1455 LUTs. An optimised multi-core CPU remains faster on stored images, but it cannot begin until the frame is complete.

</details>


### [85] [How AI Load Swings Reduce Datacenter Power Conversion Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33300)
*Heetarth Joshi,Ali Alenezi,Fang Chen Lin,Prasad Enjeti,Xiaoyang Wang,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GPU 训练和推理负载在计算与通信阶段之间快速摆动，使机架级级联电源转换器在不同效率的工作点间移动。基于商用 GaN PFC 与 LLC 的测量效率曲线，循环平均效率低于等效恒定平均负载效率，并随片上电源电压级联进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 负载功率在 5 kW 与 510 W 之间快速切换，传统按平均负载额定效率的评估无法反映真实转换损耗，需要量化电网到 GPU 的多级变换在动态负载下的效率惩罚。

Method: 采用基于能量的循环平均方法，沿 240 V AC→400 V DC→51 V DC 机架电源路径，使用商用 GaN  PFC 和 LLC 转换器的测量效率曲线；负载以 1 Hz、50% 占空比在 5 kW 和 510 W 之间摆动，并进一步加入 51/48 V 到 1 V 的片上负载点转换进行分析。

Result: 仅机架路径效率从等效恒定负载的 96.82% 下降到 95.97%，损失 0.88%；加入 51/48 V→1 V 片上转换后，效率从 90.96% 下降到 89.45%，损失扩大到 1.66%。

Conclusion: 在平均负载下评级的效率会高估真实性能，且每增加一个负载相关的变换级，真实效率惩罚就会进一步增大，多级电源设计与评估应考虑动态负载效应。

Abstract: AI training and inference workloads make GPU power swing repeatedly between compute and communication phases. As a result, the cascaded converters in a datacenter rack keep moving between operating points with different efficiencies. This paper uses an energy based, cycle averaged method to quantify that effect along a 240 V AC to 400 V DC to 51 V DC rack power path, using measured efficiency curves of commercially available GaN based PFC and LLC converters. For a 1 Hz, 50% duty-cycle load swinging between 5 kW and 510 W, conversion efficiency falls to 95.97%, compared with 96.82% at the equivalent constant average load, a loss of 0.88%. When 51/48V to 1V point of load conversion to the chip rail is included, it is shown that the loss grows to 1.66% (89.45% vs. 90.96%). These results show that efficiency rated at average load overstates real world performance, and that the penalty grows with each load dependent conversion stage between the grid and the GPU.

</details>


### [86] [The Analytical Investigation of Inductor-Coupled Mixers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33424)
*Arash Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文推导了电感和变压器耦合平衡混频器的负载准静态方程，通过解析方法（如Lambert函数、Cardano立方、Ferrari四次方程等）获得了不同半导体器件（二极管、真空管、JFET、MOSFET、BJT）下的闭式解。经37次LTspice仿真验证，波形误差低于0.017%，并展示了在无参数扫描电路设计中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平衡混频器分析多采用开关近似、转换矩阵、谐波平衡或纯数值仿真，难以在保留变压器比、反射负载和偏置等关键物理量的同时提供精确的解析解。为解决这一局限，本文旨在从器件物理定律出发，推导负载准静态方程，提供精确的闭式解析解。

Method: 从器件定律出发，推导包含变压器比、反射负载、偏置及IF共模连接的负载准静态方程。针对不同器件（如二极管、真空管、JFET、MOSFET、BJT）和拓扑结构，采用特定的数学工具求解（如主Lambert函数 $W_0$、Cardano立方、Ferrari四次方程、$r$-Lambert 函数等），并通过傅里叶投影引入Beta/Gamma、修正贝塞尔和椭圆函数。通过37个LTspice仿真和7个独立Python程序验证解析解的精度。

Result: 成功推导出针对不同器件和拓扑的精确闭式解析解（如 $W_0$、Cardano立方、Ferrari四次方程、$r$-Lambert 函数等）。在37个LTspice仿真验证中，12个主要对比的RMS波形误差均低于解析RMS值的0.017%。设计实例表明，这些闭式公式可直接用于 sizing 热离子IF负载和Gilbert单元尾电流，无需参数扫描。

Conclusion: 本研究通过从器件定律推导负载准静态方程，为电感和变压器耦合平衡混频器提供了一套高精度、无需参数扫描的解析设计方法。这突破了传统开关近似和纯数值仿真的局限，展示了利用特殊数学函数对复杂非线性混频电路进行精确闭式建模和设计的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Inductor- and transformer-coupled balanced mixers are usually analyzed with switching approximations, conversion matrices, harmonic balance, or circuit simulation. This paper instead derives their loaded quasistatic equations from the device laws, keeping the transformer ratios, reflected load, bias, and the connections that set the IF common mode. The functions needed to solve these equations are surprisingly varied. A Shockley single-balanced pair is solved by the principal Lambert function $W_0$ under a quantified one-diode-dominance condition. The double-balanced ring has a unique loaded solution that reduces to an exact inverse-hyperbolic-tangent form only for an open IF port. Vacuum diodes and triodes lead to Cardano cubics and structured sextics, whereas depletion JFET/MESFET crossbars need only square roots. Bilateral MOS channels give piecewise quadratics in strong inversion, or a Ferrari quartic when the IF winding floats. In weak inversion they give an exact $W_0$, the real $r$-Lambert function, or the inverse of $\sinh x+ρx$, depending on topology and IF connection. In matched BJT and HBT Gilbert cells, equal emitter resistors turn the hyperbolic-tangent current steering into an exact $r$-Lambert function. Fourier projection brings in Beta/Gamma, modified Bessel, and complete elliptic functions. We checked the equations against 37 LTspice simulations using seven independent Python programs. In the 12 primary comparisons, which implement the assumed device laws exactly, every RMS waveform error was below 0.017% of the analytical RMS value; built-in and vendor device models were examined separately. Two design examples show how the closed forms size a thermionic IF load and a Gilbert-cell tail current without parameter sweeps.

</details>


### [87] [DiffRouter: A Differentiable Routing Framework for UltraScale FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33510)
*Xiaohan Gao,Zhili Xiong,Yusu Wang,David Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DiffRouter是一种基于可微分、梯度下降的FPGA布线框架，利用GPU并行加速，将布线提升至连续优化空间，实现高并行性和全局最优性，随后离散化至UltraScale硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统布线器依赖逐网串行处理和剥离重试启发法，串行性限制了并行性，导致资源利用不优和运行时间显著增加。

Method: 框架包含三个关键阶段：(1) 基于资源修剪的预处理，每网提取紧凑的布线变量以降低内存和问题复杂度；(2) 通过梯度下降的可微分全局布线，将布线形式化为拉格朗日松弛优化，最小化线长并迭代 enforces 连接和拥塞约束；(3) 后处理，将连续的线分布映射到有效的UltraScale布线解并通过详细布线完成。

Result: 在FPGA 2024布线大赛基准测试中，DiffRouter优于现有并行布线器，相对于RWRoute实现5倍以上速度提升，相对于Vivado实现2倍提升，比学术最先进的布线器Potter快18%，且具有竞争力的关键路径线长。

Conclusion: DiffRouter通过可微分优化和GPU并行突破了传统布线器的串行瓶颈，展现出更高的效率和优化质量，成为FPGA布线的有力候选方案。

Abstract: Routing has become a major bottleneck in large-scale FPGA design flows, where traditional routers rely on sequential net-by-net processing and rip-up-reroute heuristics. This inherent sequentialism limits parallelism, leads to suboptimal utilization of routing resources, and significantly increases runtime on modern FPGAs. This paper introduces DiffRouter, the first differentiable, gradient-based FPGA routing framework with GPU acceleration. By lifting routing into a continuous optimization space, DiffRouter enables highly concurrent routing and improved global optimality before projecting solutions back onto the discrete fabric. The framework consists of three key stages: (1) Resource-pruned preprocessing, which extracts compact routing variables per net to reduce memory footprint and problem complexity; (2) Differentiable global routing via gradient descent, which formulates routing as a Lagrangian-relaxed optimization that minimizes wirelength while iteratively enforcing connectivity and congestion constraints as relaxed terms; and (3) Postprocessing, which maps the continuous wire distributions to a valid UltraScale routing solution and completes solutions by detailed routing. Experiments on the FPGA 2024 Routing Contest benchmarks show that DiffRouter outperforms state-of-the-art parallel routers, achieving over 5x speedup over RWRoute and 2x over Vivado, and running 18% faster than the state-of-the-art academic FPGA router Potter while delivering competitive critical path wirelength.

</details>


### [88] [ZipFusion: Fusing Lossless KV Decompression with Attention for Efficient LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33719)
*Yuetao Chen,Peng Wang,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ZipFusion通过联合设计压缩格式与注意力核，直接在寄存器中解压KV缓存，降低GPU内存带宽压力，提升大型语言模型服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM服务上下文变长且并发请求增加，KV缓存对GPU内存容量和带宽的需求持续增长，限制了解码吞吐量和并发性能。

Method: ZipFusion修改注意力核以直接消费压缩后的KV缓存，使用简单位操作在寄存器中重构原始值，避免额外解压步骤，并重新分配压缩释放内存以扩展不同层的KV存储容量。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100上，ZipFusion相比FlashInfer实现约1.25倍的注意力解码速度提升；在8K上下文下，较之未压缩SGLang提升最多26.3%的解码吞吐量；在最大支持批次下，比最优无损压缩基线提升2.76倍吞吐量。

Conclusion: ZipFusion通过压缩感知注意力核有效缓解KV缓存带来的内存带宽瓶颈，显著提升LLM服务效率，为实际部署提供更高性能与更大并发支持。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache places growing demands on GPU memory capacity and bandwidth as large language models serve longer contexts and more concurrent requests. Its footprint limits concurrency, while repeated KV reads during attention can make decoding memory-bandwidth bound. Prior lossless KV cache compression methods reduce transfers between devices but restore the uncompressed KV cache in GPU memory before attention. They therefore leave these repeated KV reads unchanged and require additional work and memory for decompression. We present ZipFusion, which enables attention to consume the losslessly compressed KV cache directly. ZipFusion co-designs the compression format and attention kernel to reduce the bytes read from GPU memory during attention. The kernel reconstructs the original values directly in registers using simple bit operations, avoiding a separate decompression pass and easing the memory-bandwidth bound. ZipFusion also reassigns memory freed by compression across layers to store additional KV cache. Its memory manager reserves space for ongoing generation and makes the remaining capacity available to new requests. We implement ZipFusion on top of FlashInfer and integrate it with SGLang. On an NVIDIA H100, ZipFusion achieves about 1.25x speedup over FlashInfer in decode attention. With 8K context, it improves model decode throughput by up to 26.3% over uncompressed SGLang under the same GPU memory budget. At each method's largest supported batch, it also delivers up to 2.76x the throughput of the best-performing lossless KV cache compression baseline.

</details>


### [89] [Adapting DNNs to Embrace Voltage Scaling Induced Errors via Accumulation-Aware Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33824)
*Alexis Maras,Panagiotis Chaidos,Georgios Alexandris,Dimitrios Soudris,Sotirios Xydis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 基本计划了一种可靠的 DNN 计算纠化，则出现了中心 在不同省準点下的 周量调节中 的高级计数的 引起过程，并做到求结果在田粗豆标 2nm 设模点：低省準点下的 能盏一致失尝 涉响，灵成 能量 和 能量-倒扗价 的 莲叉率 由于关键同汽 固位 和合能 特性 的 未来 纠建方式，整合 深度化输准和组新织，附与選强 MSB 附带和精织方式，并做到 国幂期测试，交诉于 受海精确率不不助1%，


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用 DNN 的 基本 文件的 平维应用，用户继承上变度越清的 电流 和 回起 纠建器站的 能量、可靠方式；使用 压度调节 中心 的 小家元 越 纠建方式，使近一轴 设模点的 涕源使用 不涉哯e 和 调节中心 高级计数的 交互性，并做到 使用 中心 固位 和维成 机构的方式 完分端 的 能量 和性能，

Method: 建议一个 服什转合能的 方式：
1) 个卷器在计算并输准组中 的个卷器、计数的小寶元 越 关键 固位 和级别 的转切 对应结果；
2) 混合 深度化输准 和空组织式，绖空詢记成团度用于近一轴计算路径，
3) 在 服件端 持续 电流和频率的 基本 服件并转切 的 方式，
4) 在 服件端 技术中 允宵且密封 MSB 和精织方式，
5) 应用 小家元 越 访取 的 个卷器，基于 每个 平维的 数字 深度，
使用个卷器已得跟时上的转切的 引起 使用方式，尤具后，使用 红套 2nm 设模点访取使用 浂范下的 能量 和能量-倒扗价的相对测试，

Result: 在浮上申射的低省準点下：能量 和 能量-倒扗价 分布 由 于 近一轴 实现方式 的 莲叉率 基于 整合 计算路径 和服件端纠建器 的 能量 和性能，
访取 基本 服件并 红套 设模点的 低省準点，能量 红套 对应结果：
- 能量 的 莲叉率：相对应结果中 的 能量 应结果 符红套 对应结果 的 能量 层洴 的 
39%
- 能量-倒扗价 的 莲叉率：23%
保持 性能不不助1%



Conclusion: 使用合能共帼的 转切小家元，深度化输准和空组织，附与選强 MSB 附带和精织方式，调节中心已得跟的方式，基于 深度化输准 和 能量 过程的 邀合，匹配 低省準点下的能量 咊能量-倒扗价 方案， 

使用红套 2nm 设模点＊保持性能 咊機构的永捗性＊

.

Abstract: The pervasive deployment of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) across the computing spectrum has brought growing attention to energy efficiency, alongside raw performance. While voltage scaling is a potent knob for power reduction, its application in next-generation nodes is hampered by severe throughput degradation and susceptibility to process variation. This work aims to address these energy-reliability trade-offs by introducing a hardware-software co-design methodology, based on the observation that timing-robust bit ranges within the accumulator of a systolic array vary significantly across different voltage-frequency operating points. At the software level, we propose an Accumulation-Aware Training approach that incorporates this information as a first-order optimization constraint during training. By integrating mixed-precision quantization with unstructured pruning, we effectively constrain the model's weight distribution to avoid activating the hardware's sensitized high-rank carry chains. At the architectural level, we complement this with selective MSB-masking and fine-grained clock-gating to minimize unnecessary dynamic switching activity. Furthermore, we employ a layer-wise Dynamic Frequency Scaling strategy to further adapt to the accumulator's reduced dynamic range, based on the numerical sensitivity of each layer. We experimentally evaluate the proposed solution on a deep scaled 2nm technology node and demonstrate that, when operating at a low-voltage frontier, our co-optimized post-layout architecture delivers substantial energy and energy-delay product gains of 39% and 23% respectively, relative to the nominal voltage implementation, while maintaining accuracy degradation within 1%.

</details>


### [90] [Real-Time Recurrent Qubit-State Discrimination on a Heterogeneous AI-Engine-FPGA Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33843)
*Vu Le,Yilun Xu,Neel Vora,Gang Huang,Deepak Ganesan,Phuc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向AMD Versal的硬件感知LSTM加速器，通过在依赖边界进行分割——独立输入投影在AIE阵列上、循环携带更新在可编程逻辑中实现，在64 ns输入节奏下在1,000 ns预算内运行。该设计在保持全分辨率分类器精度（96.59% vs 96.37%）的同时，将参数减少403倍、DSP减少103倍，相对误差较GMM、QubiCML、MF-NN分别降低68.2%、43.6%、16.82%，并实现8个量比特的96.78%平均精度，是最强的硬件部署 operating point。同时证明了在低延迟量子读取加速器设计中，时间分辨率与循环状态放置需 jointly 考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中的 qubit readout 是最慢、最易出错的操作之一，传统判别器丢弃量子比特留下的 relaxation 证据，全分辨率前馈分类器成本太高，循环分类器虽然保留了全分辨率但因隐状态寄存器溢出至数据内存导致延迟随序列长度增长。

Method: 在AMD Versal设备上实现硬件感知LSTM加速器，采用依赖边界分割策略：独立的输入投影并行在AIE阵列上进行，循环携带的更新在可编程逻辑（PL）中完成，避免了单个AIEタイル寄存器容量不足的问题，并维持64 ns的输入节奏与1,000 ns的预算。

Result: 该设计在中读取状态转移的量子比特上，精度匹配全分辨率前馈网络（96.59% vs 96.37%），参数减少403倍，DSP减少约103倍。相对误差较GMM、QubiCML、MF-NN分别降低68.2%、43.6%和16.82%。仅使用4个AIEタイル和17个DSP58，DSP58使用量比QubiCML和MF-NN分别少2.2-8.2倍。AIE-PL分割相比纯AIE映射将尾随延迟削减60.3%，相比全PL实现将DSP58使用减少31.1倍。 across eight qubits，小型变体达到96.78%平均精度，代表最强的硬件部署 operating point。

Conclusion: 在量子读取加速器的低延迟设计中，必须将时间分辨率与循环状态放置 jointly 考量。避免在序列长度增长时引入额外延迟，实现硬件高效、高精度的量子状态判别。

Abstract: Qubit readout, or qubit state discrimination, remains one of the slowest and most error-prone operations in superconducting quantum processors, making fast, accurate, and hardware-efficient classification essential for scalable quantum computing. Conventional discriminators integrate the I/Q record into a single point, discarding evidence a relaxing qubit leaves behind; full-resolution feed-forward classifiers recover it but are too costly to deploy under this constraint. Recurrent classifiers retain full resolution far more cheaply, but their loop-carried hidden state cannot fit a single AI Engine (AIE) tile's register capacity and spills to data memory, adding latency that grows with sequence length.
  We present a hardware-aware LSTM accelerator for an AMD Versal device that resolves this by partitioning at the dependency boundary - independent input projections on the AIE array, the loop-carried update in programmable logic (PL) - keeping pace with a 64 ns input cadence under a 1,000 ns budget. On the qubit affected by mid-readout state transitions, our design matches a full-resolution feed-forward network's accuracy (96.59% vs. 96.37%) using 403x fewer parameters and roughly 103x fewer DSPs while cutting relative error by 68.2% over GMM, 43.6% over QubiCML, and 16.82% over MF-NN. Using 4 out of 400 AIE tiles and 17 DSP58, it needs 2.2-8.2x fewer DSP58 blocks than QubiCML and MF-NN, and the AIE-PL partition cuts exposed tail latency by 60.3% versus a pure-AIE mapping and DSP58 usage 31.1x versus an all-PL implementation. Across eight qubits, a compact variant reaches 96.78% mean accuracy, the strongest hardware-deployed operating point. Temporal resolution and recurrent-state placement must then be treated jointly when designing low-latency quantum readout accelerators.

</details>


### [91] [Toward Near-Optimal Floorplanning with Complex Design Constraints for Modern SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34046)
*Fuxing Huang,Hao Ai,Qiyuan Chen,Hao Wu,Wenxing Zhu,Xinning Liu,Ziran Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种三阶段分析框架，整合了半定规划（SDP）、增广拉格朗日方法（ALM）和二次锥规划（SOCP），解决SoC floorplanning中的约束问题，显著减少了线路长度和面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代SoC floorplanning面临高度耦合的复杂设计约束，现有方法将约束松弛为目标函数，通过多目标优化导致不可行解或质量下降。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：第一阶段采用两步SDP公式获得非凸搜索空间中的鲁棒初始化；第二阶段将可微目标和约束纳入增广拉格朗日方法处理全局floorplanning；第三阶段使用SOCP严格强制可行性并进一步减小面积。

Result: 在FloorSet基准测试上，方法满足所有约束且运行速度最快，平均减少线路长度90%和面积16%，接近最优解，线路长度和面积仅比基准参考大2%和1%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过严格约束处理和高效优化显著提升了SoC floorplanning的质量和效率，相比现有方法有显著优势。

Abstract: Modern system-on-chip (SoC) floorplanning is increasingly challenged by highly coupled complex design constraints. Existing methods often relax constraints into objectives via multi-objective optimization, leading to infeasible solutions or degraded quality. To address this, we formulate the problem as a constrained optimization task and propose a novel three-stage analytical framework integrating semidefinite programming (SDP), augmented Lagrangian method (ALM), and second-order cone programming (SOCP), which enforces constraints more rigorously and achieves higher-quality solutions. First, we adopt a two-step SDP formulation to obtain a robust initialization in the non-convex search space. Next, we incorporate differentiable objectives and constraints into an incremental ALM to jointly handle coupled design constraints during global floorplanning. Finally, an SOCP-based legalization strictly enforces feasibility while further reducing area. Experiments on the FloorSet benchmark show that our method satisfies all constraints with the fastest runtime. Compared with state-of-the-art methods, it reduces wirelength and area by 90\% and 16\% on average, and achieves near-optimal results, with wirelength and area being merely 2\% and 1\% larger than the ground-truth references, respectively.

</details>


### [92] [ChroNoC: A Tableless TDM Circuit-Switched NoC With Delay-Aware Deployment for Time-Sensitive Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34083)
*Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Mehdi Modarressi,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于硬件-软件协同设计的神经形态计算架构 ChroNoC，用于解决时间敏感神经形态网络对可预测互连的需求。核心贡献包括：1）无表 TDM NoC 架构，将预计算的满网格广播树嵌入组合逻辑作为固定路由基底；2）DARSA（Delay-Aware Reservations and Spatial Annealing）部署算法，将轴突延迟转化为周期调度空闲以错开网络注入并降低通信开销；3）在 130nm CMOS 工艺中，每跳能耗降低超过 75%；4）在 UCI-HAR 和 N-MNIST 工作负载上，每步通信周期减少约 60% 和 45%，同时降低了分配的通信存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态架构需要可预测的互连来精确保持事件时序。现有静态调度 TDM 电路交换 NoC 虽然提供确定性延迟，但代价是配置内存随调度深度线性扩展，且联合优化任务放置、路由和调度的组合搜索空间难以处理。此外，现有部署框架忽略算法轴突延迟，强制即时脉冲注入会增加网络竞争。论文旨在通过硬件-软件协同设计同时解决这些问题，提升硬件效率和系统级通信性能，同时保持神经事件时序的准确性。

Method: 核心方法是硬件-软件协同设计结合算法创新：(1) ChroNoC 无表 TDM NoC 架构，预计算满网格广播树嵌入组合逻辑作为固定路由基底，将部署简化为时空搜索；(2) DARSA 部署算法，通过延迟感知预留和空间退火，将轴突延迟转化为周期性调度空闲，从而错开网络注入，降低通信开销；(3) 在 DE_SCHED 和 DE_BUS 等电路交换和包交换基线方法上比较评估；(4) 使用算法模拟评估通信效率，使用 130nm CMOS 工艺评估能耗。

Result: 关键结果：(1) 在 130nm CMOS 工艺中，ChroNoC 每跳能耗降低超过 75%，相比评估的电路交换和包交换基线。(2) 在 UCI-HAR 工作负载上，DARSA 每步通信周期比即时传输减少约 60%。(3) 在 N-MNIST 工作负载上，DARSA 每步通信周期比即时传输减少约 45%。(4) DARSA 同时降低了分配的通信存储。这些结果表明，轻量级 TDM 交换机设计与延迟感知部署的结合可以同时提升硬件效率和系统级通信性能，同时保留神经事件时序。

Conclusion: 论文成功展示了在神经形态计算系统中采用 ChroNoC 无表 TDM NoC 与 DARSA 部署算法的双重优势：相比基线方法大幅降低每跳能耗和每步通信周期，同时高效管理通信存储。这为时间敏感神经形态系统的设计提供了具有实际价值的硬件-软件协同路径。

Abstract: Time-sensitive neuromorphic architectures require predictable interconnects to preserve precise event timing. Statically scheduled time-division-multiplexed (TDM) circuit-switched networks-on-chip (NoCs) provide deterministic latency at the cost of linear configuration-memory scaling with schedule depth. Furthermore, jointly optimizing task placement, routing, and scheduling creates an intractable combinatorial search space. Existing deployment frameworks also ignore algorithmic axonal delays by forcing immediate spike injections that increase network contention. This paper proposes a hardware-software co-design to address these challenges. ChroNoC is a tableless TDM NoC that embeds precomputed full-grid broadcast trees into combinational logic as a fixed routing substrate. This fixed substrate reduces deployment to a spatial search via Delay-Aware Reservations and Spatial Annealing (DARSA). DARSA converts axonal delays into periodic scheduling slack to stagger network injections and reduce communication overhead. In a 130nm CMOS process, ChroNoC reduces per-hop energy consumption by more than 75% relative to the evaluated circuit- and packet-switched baselines. On UCI-HAR and N-MNIST workloads, DARSA reduces per-timestep communication cycles by approximately 60% and 45%, respectively, compared with immediate transmission, while also reducing provisioned communication memory. These results demonstrate how combining lightweight TDM switch design with delay-aware deployment improves both hardware efficiency and system-level communication performance while preserving neural event timing.

</details>


### [93] [MCF: A Memory- and Communication-Aware Co-Designed Framework for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34731)
*Huaizhi Zhang,Jiacheng Zhu,Xuqi Zhu,Junkyu Lee,Klaus McDonald-Maier,Xiaojun Zhai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MCF 是一个面向长上下文 LLM 推理的内存与通信感知框架，通过动态 KV 缓存布局转换和查询中心执行模型，显著提升首 token 延迟、吞吐与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式推理系统在长上下文场景下面临内存压力大、KV 缓存通信开销随上下文增长而线性增加、预填充与解码阶段布局转换昂贵等问题，亟需一种自适应的内存调度与通信优化方案。

Method: 核心是 MARS 机制：在分块预填充早期利用内存余量复制高复用 KV 块以提升局部性并隐藏传播延迟；随内存压力增加逐步回收副本，收敛为分片的解码就绪布局，避免阶段边界转换开销。解码阶段采用查询中心模型，KV 分片固定，仅路由轻量查询张量，将通信从依赖 KV 缓存转为依赖查询，降低随上下文增长的通信量。并结合 FPGA 算力网卡的硬软协同执行层，实现元数据驱动的 KV 路由与集合操作。

Result: 实验表明 MCF 在首 token 延迟、吞吐率和扩展性上均优于现有最先进分布式推理系统，且随着上下文长度和集群规模增大优势更明显。

Conclusion: MCF 通过内存感知的动态复制调度与查询中心通信模型，配合硬件加速网络路径，有效解决了长上下文 LLM 推理的内存与通信瓶颈，为大规模长序列部署提供了可扩展的高性能方案。

Abstract: This paper presents Memory- and Communication-Aware Framework for Long-Context LLM Inference (MCF) that optimizes long-context inference through communication-aware and stage-adaptive KV-cache layout transitions. At the core of MCF is {Memory-Aware Replication and Scheduling} (MARS), a dynamic orchestration mechanism for chunked prefill. MARS exploits early-stage memory headroom by selectively replicating highly reused KV blocks, improving locality and hiding KV propagation latency behind attention computation. As prefill progresses and memory pressure increases, MARS progressively drains redundant replicas and converges the KV cache into a sharded decode-ready layout, avoiding expensive layout conversion at the prefill--decode boundary. For decoding, MCF adopts a query-centric execution model in which KV shards remain stationary while lightweight query tensors are routed across workers. This changes communication from KV-cache-dependent transfer to query-dependent transfer, reducing communication growth with context length. MCF further integrates a hardware-software co-designed execution layer based on an FPGA compute-NIC datapath, enabling metadata-driven KV routing and collective operations directly in the network path. Experiments show that MCF improves TTFT, throughput, and scalability over state-of-the-art distributed inference systems, with larger benefits as context length and cluster size increase.

</details>


### [94] [SPARTA: A Sparse Ternary LLM Accelerator with Adaptive Attention and Unified Datapath for Long-Context Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34824)
*Yanzhen Zhou,Zhengyu Han,Qunyou Liu,Zheyu Yan,Weihong Xu,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPARTA是一款面向边缘设备的稀疏三元LLM加速器，针对长上下文推理中的KV缓存和注意力瓶颈进行优化，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在边缘设备上受限于内存和带宽，1.58-bit三元权重虽然减少了模型存储，但长上下文推理仍受KV缓存流量和注意力机制的瓶颈限制。现有加速器对三元线性层和注意力使用独立的数据路径，导致硬件和执行开销。

Method: SPARTA整合了三个关键组件：(1) 考虑相位的稀疏数据流，利用预填充和解码阶段的异构稀疏性；(2) 统一计算 fabric 将三元SpMM/SpMV和位串注意力映射到共享处理单元，利用零权重和零位稀疏性；(3) 自适应两阶段注意力引擎，结合页级Top-k选择减少K缓存访问和Token级Top-p剪枝减少V缓存流量。

Result: SPARTA将KV流量减少了4.1-4.8倍（相较于全注意力）和1.3-1.5倍（相较于SOTA稀疏注意力方案），在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现3.9倍的生成吞吐量和8.3倍的端到端能效比，在AMD Ryzen 7 9700X上分别提升5.0倍和81.7倍；同时在FIGLUT、TeLLMe和MCBP上实现1.6-12.0倍的吞吐量提升、2.0-4.2倍的能效比提升和1.3-51.4倍的面积效率提升。

Conclusion: SPARTA通过相位感知的稀疏数据流、统一计算 fabric 和自适应两阶段注意力引擎，有效解决了边缘设备上的KV缓存和注意力瓶颈，显著提升了长上下文LLM在资源受限环境下的推理性能和能效。

Abstract: LLMs on edge devices are constrained by limited memory and bandwidth. Although 1.58-bit ternary weights greatly reduce model storage, long-context inference is increasingly bottlenecked by KV-cache traffic and attention. Existing accelerators also use separate datapaths for ternary linear layers and attention, incurring hardware and execution overhead. We present SPARTA, a sparse ternary LLM accelerator for efficient long-context edge inference. SPARTA integrates: (1) a phase-aware sparse dataflow exploiting heterogeneous sparsity across compute-bound prefill and memory-bound decode; (2) a unified compute fabric mapping ternary SpMM/SpMV and bit-serial attention onto shared processing elements to exploit zero-weight and zero-bit sparsity; and (3) an adaptive two-stage attention engine combining page-level Top-k selection to reduce K-cache accesses with token-level Top-p pruning to reduce V-cache traffic. SPARTA reduces KV traffic by 4.1-4.8x over full attention and 1.3-1.5x over a state-of-the-art sparse-attention scheme, with task-dependent quality loss. Compared with NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, SPARTA achieves 3.9x higher generation throughput and 8.3x higher end-to-end energy efficiency on average; versus AMD Ryzen 7 9700X, the gains are 5.0x and 81.7x. It also delivers 1.6-12.0x higher throughput, 2.0-4.2x higher energy efficiency, and 1.3-51.4x higher area efficiency than FIGLUT, TeLLMe, and MCBP.

</details>


### [95] [PaFeDSE: Pareto-Feedback-Guided Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration for Efficient Pareto Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34838)
*Joonyong Park,Hyojeong Lee,Sejeong Yang,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PaFeDSE 是一种基于帕累托反馈的微架构设计空间探索框架，通过利用帕累托命中和帕累托遗漏反馈来优先化有潜力的候选者并抑制无望区域的探索，同时过滤低优先级的冗余候选者，显著减少模拟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代乱序处理器中，微架构设计空间探索（DSE）对于优化性能、功耗和面积的权衡至关重要。虽然机器学习-based DSE 显著降低了对大规模设计空间的穷举评估成本，但现有方法仍会浪费昂贵的模拟资源在帕累托遗漏候选者上，并在迭代探索过程中重复处理冗余候选者。

Method: PaFeDSE 提出了一种基于帕累托反馈的微架构 DSE 框架。它利用帕累托命中反馈来优先化有可能改善帕累托前沿的候选者，利用帕累托遗漏反馈来抑制探索无望局部区域，并在迭代过程中过滤掉在多个迭代中保持低优先级的冗余候选者。

Result: 在 SPEC CPU 2017 工作负载上测试，PaFeDSE 相比最强基线在达到目标超体积水平时需要最多 49.0% 更少的模拟，并通过自适应候选者过滤实现了 7.1 倍的探索开销减少。

Conclusion: PaFeDSE 有效解决了 DSE 中冗余模拟和无效探索的问题，通过帕累托反馈引导实现了显著的效率提升，为微架构设计空间探索提供了一种更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient microarchitecture design space exploration (DSE) is critical for optimizing performance, power, and area trade-offs in modern out-of-order processors. While machine-learning-based DSE significantly reduces the cost of exhaustively evaluating large design spaces, existing methods still waste expensive simulations on Pareto-miss candidates that do not improve the Pareto frontier and repeatedly process redundant candidates during iterative exploration. In this paper, we propose PaFeDSE, a Pareto-feedback-guided microarchitecture DSE framework for efficient Pareto search. PaFeDSE leverages Pareto-hit feedback to prioritize candidates likely to improve the frontier and Pareto-miss feedback to suppress exploration of unpromising local regions, while filtering redundant candidates that remain low priority across iterations.
