Evaluated on SPEC CPU 2017 workloads, PaFeDSE requires up to 49.0% fewer simulations than the strongest prior baseline to reach target hypervolume levels, while achieving a 7.1x reduction in exploration overhead through adaptive candidate filtering.

</details>


### [96] [Detection of Single Stuck-at and Bridging Faults in Cluster-based FPGA Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35026)
*Nachiketa Das,Sudip Ghosh,H. Rahaman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于集群基FPGA架构的单点卡死-故障和搥接故障检测技术，通过配置逻辑元件的测试结构，利用相同的测试向量集合（由TPG电路生成）在特定序列下检测所有故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA可靠性 Testing 存在效率问题：传统方法可能需要不同测试向量处理不同故障类型，增加资源消耗。该技术旨在统一检测策略，降低测试开销。

Method: 通过配置逻辑元素为测试结构，在FPGA内部生成待测模块(BU T)、TPG和输出响应分析电路。比较两个独立逻辑元素的输出差异以判断故障。

Result: 实验结果表明，相同测试向量在特定顺序下可有效检测卡死-故障和搥接故障，验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该技术通过优化测试向量序列和硬件电路配置，实现了高效故障检测，为FPGA可靠性测试提供了更具资源效率的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a new technique for detecting single stuck-at and bridging faults in cluster-based FPGA architectures, using same set of test vectors generated by a test pattern generator circuitry. This technique is based on configuration of logic elements using test logic structure and the outputs of two separated logic elements are compared to detect a fault. To do this, block under test, TPG and output response analysis circuitry are generated in FPGAs. The configurations of test pattern generation, block under test and output response analysis are also discussed in this paper. The novelty lies in the fact that the same set of test vectors can detect all the stuck-at as well as bridging faults if the test vectors are applied in a particular sequence and analyzing the output results. We presented a detail analysis of results to detect faults as well.

</details>


### [97] [Whole-Route Planning of a Memory Bus Between Two BGAs: An Exact Model of Crossings and Layer Changes, Negotiated to DRC-Clean Copper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35601)
*Andrew Haas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种自动化路由器，用于规划印刷电路板上处理器和DRAM之间的多-lane BGA‑to‑BGA 总线。该路由器的核心是一个精确的约束编程模型，该模型同时为所有线路选择交叉点、交叉顺序和改层位置（包含通孔空间），并生成符合设计规则的铜箔布局。模型具有物理般的自然解释。通过线性规划进行毫米级坐标定位，并利用违规约束作为裁剪和协商后的拥塞价格进行审计，直到布局通过生产路由器的规则检查或得到清理。测试结果表明，该路由器在 Allwinner H3（51 条网络）和 Zynq‑7020（43 条网络）上运行时，与人类设计师相比，通孔数量更少（80 vs 88/62 vs 100），铜箔用量更少；在 Zynq 板本身上，路由器完成了 45 条网络（70 个通孔），而设计师完成了 105 个通孔。在 3、4 和 6 层总线上，路由器每次运行都能成功完成布局，并且能够达到已知的最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印刷电路板设计中，处理器和 DRAM 之间的总线布线仍然 largely 是手动进行的，已发表的算法主要关注交叉或层计数等代理指标，而没有全面优化整条总线的拓扑结构。需要一种能够自动规划每条线路的路径、处理交叉顺序、层更改和通孔布线，并确保符合设计规则的路由器，以减少人工工作量，降低通孔密度和铜箔用量，同时保持最优性能。

Method: 采用精确的约束编程模型，模型同时为所有线路选择必要的交叉点、交叉顺序（基于每三条线路的规则）以及每条线路的改层位置（包含通孔空间）。模型具有物理般的自然解释，可映射为世界线、 frustrated Ising spins 和接触力。通过线性规划进行毫米级坐标定位。违规约束被用作裁剪，并通过协商后的拥塞价格进行审计，以确保布局通过生产路由器的规则检查或得到清理。差分对线路保持极性，并在端点极性不一致时自动进行通孔交叉。路由器在 Allwinner H3 和 Zynq‑7020 平台上运行，从 DDR3 设备开始构建完整的总线。

Result: 在 Allwinner H3 上，路由器成功路由了多达 51 条网络（完整的总线），在 Zynq‑7020 上路由了 43 条网络。与人类设计师相比，通孔数量更少（80 vs 88/62 vs 100），铜箔用量更少。生产路由器在 10 个级联中留有 4 个未连接的网络，而 Freerouting 则完全失败。在 Zynq 板本身上，路由器完成了 45 条网络（70 个通孔），而设计师则需要 105 个通孔。在 3、4 和 6 层总线的生成布局中，每一次运行都能成功完成，并且能够达到已知的最优解。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种自动化且精确的 BGA‑to‑BGA 总线路由器，它能够生成符合设计规则、最佳通孔布线和最优铜箔使用的完整布局。实验证明，该路由器在实际芯片上优于人类设计，并且在各种层数配置中都能达到最优解，为印刷电路板总线设计提供了一种强大的新方法。

Abstract: Routing the bus between a processor and its DRAM remains largely manual in printed circuit board (PCB) design, and published algorithms optimize proxies such as crossing or layer counts. We present a router that plans every lane of a two-layer BGA-to-BGA bus before any copper is laid, and carries the plan to design-rule-clean copper. Its core is an exact constraint-programming model that chooses, for all lanes at once, where each necessary crossing occurs, in what order -- by a rule over every triple of lanes -- and where each lane changes layer, with via room; for the ends it is given, it proves its plan optimal within the model. The model reads naturally as physics: worldlines, frustrated Ising spins and contact forces. A linear program places the lanes in millimeters; the constraints it must violate return to the model as cuts, and audit findings as negotiated congestion prices, until the plan passes an audit against the production router's rules or lays clean. The escapes are chosen by an estimate of the same objective, and differential pairs carry their polarity, with an automatic via crossover where the ends disagree. On an Allwinner H3 and a Zynq-7020, each to a DDR3 device, the router completes every rung of two ladders, of up to 51 nets on the H3 (its whole bus) and 43 on the Zynq, connected and DRC-clean, never with more vias than the human designer (80 against 88 and 62 against 100 at the top rungs) and always with less copper, where the toolkit's production router leaves nets open on four of the ten rungs and Freerouting completes none. On the Zynq board itself it completes 45 of the bus's nets with 70 vias to the designer's 105. On generated buses of three, four and six routing layers every run completes, and every one with a known optimum attains it.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [LooseLeaf: Enabling Serverless GPUs for Computational Notebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33275)
*Yusen Wu,Tingfeng Lan,Benjamin Carver,Haoliang Wang,Geoffrey Fox,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LooseLeaf is a serverless GPU platform for interactive notebooks that extracts minimal cell closures and prepares a GPU worker between cells, achieving near‑zero latency and reducing GPU‑hour usage by 32–55% compared to existing solutions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional computational notebooks keep a GPU pinned for the entire session, leading to idle GPU time. Serverless execution could release the GPU after each cell, but reconstructing notebook state on a fresh worker incurs high overhead. The need to efficiently reuse GPU resources between cells while preserving interactivity and reducing cost drove this work.

Method: The authors analyzed 5,000 real‑world notebooks to determine that cell‑specific closures (required variables and packages) are small. They designed LooseLeaf to compute these closures per cell, prepare a GPU worker with the necessary state while the user edits, execute the cell, and quickly persist the updated state, making the GPU reusable after each cell.

Result: LooseLeaf starts 95–98% of GPU cells with no visible delay, matches the interactivity of a pinned GPU session, and provisions 32–55% fewer GPU‑hours than state‑of‑the‑art notebook platforms.

Conclusion: LooseLeaf demonstrates that serverless GPU execution is viable for interactive notebooks by efficiently managing per‑cell state, delivering low latency and significant resource savings.

Abstract: Computational notebooks reserve a GPU for the whole session, yet the GPU computes only while a cell executes and sits idle otherwise. Serverless execution would release the GPU between cells, but reconstructing the accumulated notebook state on a fresh worker imposes substantial cost on every GPU cell. This paper presents LooseLeaf, a serverless GPU platform for interactive notebooks. LooseLeaf derives the state and import closures of each cell, the minimal variables and packages that the cell needs. Our analysis on 5,000 real-world notebooks shows that these closures are small, and user-cell editing leaves seconds to prepare them. LooseLeaf prepares a GPU worker with these closures while the user edits the cell. After execution, the worker saves the updated state within seconds, so the GPU is reclaimable after every cell. LooseLeaf starts 95-98% of GPU cells with no visible delay, matches the interactivity of a pinned session, and provisions 32-55% fewer GPU-hours than state-of-the-art notebook platforms.

</details>


### [99] [LLMs in Cloud: A Discussion on Architecture, Orchestration, and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33475)
*Jannatun Noor,Athar Noor Mohammad Rafee,Md. Sadman Haque,Sreezon Das Gupta,Atiar Osman,Md. Abu Obaida Zishan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从硬件虚拟化、集群调度和运行时推理三个层面，对云端大规模基础模型服务开展跨层系统综述，提出统一分类法，评估弹性、容错与多租户隔离架构，并总结吞吐量、延迟、成本与保密性的帕累托权衡及未来研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多停留在单层，将加速器虚拟化与集群编排、运行时推理割裂，且忽略2024-2026年的关键进展；同时，LLM服务面临HBM带宽墙、预填与解码非对称性、KV缓存爆炸、冷启动及硬件可靠性等瓶颈，亟需全面梳理。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020框架开展系统性文献综述，综合硬件虚拟化、集群级调度、运行时推理优化（如连续批处理、预填-解码解耦、分层KV缓存卸载），以及云原生弹性服务架构（缩零到零、静默数据损坏的容错检查点、TEE多租户隔离）进行分析。

Result: 构建了跨层统一分类法，梳理了突破性优化技术，评估了多租户隔离与容错方案，并形式化了吞吐量、延迟、货币成本、保密性之间的多维帕累托权衡。

Conclusion: 未来应围绕CXL内存池、内存内计算以及自主多智能体控制平面展开研究，以支撑更高效、可靠、保密的云端LLM基础设施。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of foundational Large Language Models (LLM) across cloud datacenters marks a fundamental paradigm shift from stateless deep learning toward distributed, memory-intensive generative workloads. However, serving massive foundation models under stringent Service Level Objectives (SLO) exposes critical physical and system bottlenecks. These challenges include the persistent High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) wall, operational asymmetry between compute-bound prefilling and memory-bandwidth-bound decoding, explosive Key-Value (KV) cache expansion, prohibitive serverless cold starts, and hardware reliability vulnerabilities. Existing literature reviews remain layer-siloed, decoupling low-level accelerator virtualization from cluster orchestration and runtime inference, while largely overlooking defining advancements from 2024 to 2026. To address these limitations, this study presents a comprehensive, cross-layer systematic review guided by the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) 2020 framework. We synthesize a holistic taxonomy unifying hardware virtualization, cluster-level scheduling, and runtime inference optimizations, including iteration-level continuous batching, prefill-decoding disaggregation, and hierarchical KV cache offloading. Furthermore, we evaluate resilient cloud-native service architectures, examining scale-to-zero serverless paradigms, fault-tolerant checkpointing against silent data corruption, and hardware-enforced Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) for multi-tenant isolation. Finally, we formalize the multi-dimensional Pareto trade-offs governing throughput, latency, monetary cost, and confidentiality, outlining a forward-looking research roadmap spanning Compute Express Link (CXL) memory pooling, in-memory computing, and autonomous multi-agent control planes to guide future cloud LLM infrastructure.

</details>


### [100] [DualPace: Decoupled Scheduling and Cooperative GPU--CPU Decoding for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33501)
*YuXuan Lin,KeQiang Wu,Kingsum Chow*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DualPace is a new LLM serving runtime that decouples GPU and CPU scheduling to independently advance CPU-resident decodes, improving throughput and tail latency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing CPU-offloading LLM serving systems remain GPU-gated, causing bursty CPU progress and high tail inter-token latency; an independent CPU serving engine is needed.

Method: DualPace separates scheduling into a GPU engine for prefills and GPU-resident decodes, and an AMX-enabled CPU engine for independent CPU-resident decode batching, with cooperative execution and offline profile-guided optimization.

Result: DualPace improves throughput by 6.9-43.2% over NEO and 11.5-90.6% over vLLM in decode scenarios, while preserving NEO's P99 TTFT advantage and sustaining 7.3-50.5% higher request rates than NEO at the same P99 ITL.

Conclusion: Treating the host as an independently scheduled decode engine significantly enhances LLM serving throughput and tail latency without sacrificing responsiveness.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving relies on batching to improve inference throughput, but the key-value (KV) cache grows with sequence length and limits GPU-resident concurrency. CPU offloading can expand KV capacity using host memory, yet existing systems remain largely GPU-gated: CPU-resident requests make progress only when the GPU scheduler exposes suitable overlap opportunities. As a result, host memory and CPU compute cannot act as an independent serving resource, leading to bursty CPU-side progress and inflated tail inter-token latency (ITL) under load.
  We present DualPace, an LLM serving runtime that turns the host into an independently scheduled decode engine. DualPace separates scheduling authority across two engines: the GPU engine executes prefills and GPU-resident decodes, while an AMX-enabled CPU engine independently batches and advances CPU-resident decodes. The engines are decoupled in scheduling but cooperative in execution: the CPU can execute a complete decode iteration locally or selectively invoke GPU Dense Assist for dense stages, while offline profiles guide hardware-specific batching and routing.
  Our evaluations show that, in decode-oriented scenarios, DualPace improves serving throughput by 6.9--43.2\% over NEO, a state-of-the-art open-source CPU-offloading system, and by 11.5--90.6\% over vLLM, a widely used GPU serving system, while maintaining the same level of latency. DualPace also preserves NEO's P99 time-to-first-token (TTFT) advantage over vLLM while sustaining 7.3--50.5\% higher request rates than NEO at the same level of P99 ITL.

</details>


### [101] [Replicate What Varies: Resource Sharing for Domain-Randomized Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33582)
*Zixu Hao,Weijun Wang,Xiangyu Li,Liang Mi,Hao Wu,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Patchwork通过共享模板和补丁机制，显著降低并行仿真环境的内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行运行多个领域随机化仿真环境会消耗大量CPU/GPU内存，因为独立构建的场景重复了不变的资源。

Method: 提出Patchwork系统，采用E=T+Δ表示法，共享模板包含通用资源，补丁记录变异；分层运行时共享编译模型结构和不可变资产，每个实例私有可变参数和状态；在robosuite和MuJoCo上实现。

Result: 在32个副本下，共享模板执行相比进程级环境执行将CPU RSS降低88%-92%；内存增长转向模板多样性和独特资产内容，而非实例数量。

Conclusion: Patchwork通过资源共享有效解决了大规模并行仿真的内存瓶颈，使内存随模板多样性而非实例数增长。

Abstract: Simulation provides a low-cost sandbox where embodied agents can attempt tasks, observe outcomes, and learn from experience. Running many diverse simulation environments concurrently can consume substantial CPU and GPU memory because independently constructed scenes duplicate resources that remain unchanged across instances. We present Patchwork, a resource-sharing system for domain-randomized simulation, built around the representation $E=T+Δ$: a shared template contains common resources, and a patch records the mutations needed to construct a target environment. Patchwork separates randomization sampling from replay and selects reusable templates together with patches that preserve each target environment's intended configuration. Its layered runtime shares compiled model structure and immutable assets while keeping mutable parameters and simulation state private to each instance. We implement Patchwork on robosuite and MuJoCo and evaluate it on LIBERO-plus and RoboCasa365 workloads. At 32 replicas, shared-template execution reduces CPU RSS by 88-92% relative to process-per-environment execution. Controlled experiments show how sharing shifts memory growth toward template diversity and unique asset content rather than instance count, within the supported sharing boundaries.

</details>


### [102] [Speeding up Log-Sum-Exp: Kernel Fusion at the Memory Wall, Integer Arithmetic at the Compute Wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33647)
*Lingyun Yao,Martin Andraud,Niki Loppi,Andrea Pilzer,Anji Liu,Guy Van den Broeck,Martin Trapp*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: The paper introduces FuseLSE (GPU・SFU・fused kernel) and IntLSE (CPU・integer‑approx kernel) to accelerate Log‑Sum‑Exp computation. Both achieve 10‑11× speed‑up over PyTorch’s torch.logsumexp on GPUs and 3‑6× on CPUs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current torch.logsumexp uses many sub‑kernels, causing high HBM traffic and launch overhead on GPUs, while CPU implementations suffer from expensive exp/log arithmetic, limiting scalability.

Method: Designed FuseLSE: a single‑pass fused kernel that leverages GPU Special Function Units for exp/log. Designed IntLSE: replaces LSE with approximated integer arithmetic for CPU execution. Both are drop‑in replacements.

Result: Benchmarks across workload sizes show ~10‑11× faster execution on GPUs (larger workloads ~6×) and ~3‑6× faster on CPUs compared with the original PyTorch implementation.

Conclusion: The proposed kernels effectively reduce kernel launch overhead, memory traffic, and computational cost, delivering substantial performance gains for LSE in modern ML pipelines.

Abstract: While the Log-Sum-Exp (LSE) function is a foundational numerical primitive for stable log-domain computation in modern machine learning, its fast and efficient computation on generic platforms (\ie, CPUs and GPUs) has been underexplored, potentially creating execution bottlenecks for large workloads. On GPU, the de facto implementation torch.logsumexp dispatches a chain of nine sub-kernels per call, incurring redundant High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) traffic and launch overhead. On CPU, exp and log operations are hardware-hungry, limiting the computing efficiency. To address these bottlenecks, we propose two drop-in kernels to significantly accelerate LSE computation on both GPUs and CPUs: FuseLSE, a single-pass fused kernel that evaluates LSE's exp and log functions on the GPU's Special Function Unit (SFU); and IntLSE, which further replaces the LSE computation with approximated integer arithmetic for CPU computation. Our experiments, validated by benchmarks across workload sizes and hardware platforms, show that both kernels are ${\sim}10-11\times$ faster than PyTorch at small workloads and ${\sim}6\times$ faster on larger ones on GPUs, where the speed-up is attributed to reduced launch overhead and HBM traffic. On CPU, where no SFU is available, LSE computation dominates the cost, allowing IntLSE to gain ${\sim}3-6\times$ execution speed over FuseLSE.

</details>


### [103] [SGL: Towards a Bridging Model for Heterogeneous Hierarchical Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33995)
*Chong Li,Gaétan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Introduces Scatter-Gather Language (SGL), a simple imperative model for parallel programming built on BSP principles, designed for multi-level heterogeneous architectures.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Driven by the shift to multi-core, GPU and hierarchical network systems, needing a simpler, load‑balanced parallel programming approach.

Method: Derives SGL from BSP experience, provides an imperative language with straightforward primitives, explicit data distribution, and automatic or programmable load balancing.

Result: SGL covers many BSP algorithms, avoids complex message passing, and delivers predictable, portable, scalable performance.

Conclusion: SGL offers a concise, efficient framework for modern parallel computing across diverse architectures.

Abstract: This paper introduces the Scatter-Gather parallel-programming and parallel-execution model in the form of a simple imperative Scatter-Gather Language (SGL). Its design is based on past experience with bulk-synchronous parallel (BSP) programming and BSP language design. SGL's novel features are motivated by the last decade's move towards multi-level and heterogeneous parallel architectures involving multi-core processors, graphics accelerators and hierarchical routing networks in the largest multiprocessing systems. The design of SGL is coherent with L. Valiant's Multi-BSP while offering a programming interface that is even simpler than the primitives of Bulk-Synchronous Parallel ML (BSML). SGL appears to cover a large subset of all BSP algorithms while avoiding complex message-passing programming. Like all BSP-inspired systems, it supports predictable, portable and scalable performance. Moreover, SGL's explicit data distribution will allow automatic or programmable load-balancing.

</details>


### [104] [InferScale: Kubernetes-Native Predictive Autoscaling for SLO-Aware AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34006)
*Wen Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: InferScale is a Kubernetes-native autoscaler designed for inference services that suffer from slow reactive scaling and multi‑tenant resource contention. It uses inference‑aware signals, short‑horizon demand prediction, SLO‑state control, fast‑path spike handling, and tenant‑priority headroom to improve scaling speed and fairness without replacing the default scheduler. Evaluations on Azure Kubernetes Service show significant gains in SLO compliance (47.8% → 65.3%) and reduced SLO variance under contention compared to a stronger external‑metric HPA.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional Kubernetes HPA reacts too slowly for inference workloads with long cold‑starts, and in shared clusters independent HPAs compete, harming tenant SLOs. The need is for an autoscaler that anticipates demand, handles spikes quickly, and respects tenant priorities while preserving integration with the existing scheduler.

Method: InferScale integrates inference‑specific metrics, short‑term demand prediction, SLO‑state feedback control, rapid spike handling, and priority‑based headroom allocation, all implemented as a native Kubernetes controller that complements (rather than replaces) the default scheduler. The authors evaluate it via ResNet‑50 ONNX workloads with 21‑second cold‑start ramps and controlled multi‑tenant contention experiments, including ablation studies to isolate component contributions.

Result: On real inference workloads, InferScale raises SLO compliance from 47.8% ± 1.5% (strong external‑metric HPA) to 65.3% ± 9.0%. In multi‑tenant contention, it reduces the Gold‑tier SLO standard deviation from 16.1 to 1.2 percentage points and aligns contention outcomes more closely with priority policy. Ablations show 6‑8 point drops per component, and a priority ablation yields a 27‑point drop under contention.

Conclusion: For startup‑sensitive inference serving, the key benefit of prediction is not precise forecasting but moving the controller early enough to offset cold‑start latency, leading to higher SLO compliance and more predictable, priority‑aware resource sharing in shared clusters.

Abstract: Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaler (HPA) is widely used for inference services, but reactive scaling is often too slow when pods take tens of seconds to become ready. The problem gets worse in shared clusters, where independent HPAs compete for the same resource budget and can hurt different tenants from run to run. We present InferScale, a Kubernetes-native autoscaler that combines inference-aware signals, short-horizon demand prediction, SLO-state control, fast-path spike handling, and tenant-priority headroom without replacing the default scheduler. We evaluate InferScale on Azure Kubernetes Service in two settings. On a real ResNet-50 ONNX workload with 21-second cold start, repeated ramp-burst runs improve SLO compliance from 47.8% +/- 1.5% under a stronger external-metric HPA to 65.3% +/- 9.0% under InferScale. In repeated controlled multi-tenant contention experiments, InferScale cuts Gold-tier SLO standard deviation from 16.1 to 1.2 percentage points and makes contention outcomes follow the priority policy more closely. Repeated single-service ablations show 6-8 percentage point degradation, with an exploratory priority ablation showing a 27-point drop under contention. The main takeaway is that, in startup-sensitive inference serving, the practical value of prediction lies less in precise forecasting than in moving the controller early enough to offset cold-start delay.

</details>


### [105] [Do Bio-Inspired Algorithms Save Energy in Data Centres? Separating Server Packing from Migration Search in Virtual Machine Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34102)
*Yichiet Aun,Yu Hang Woo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入匹配控制组重现了云模拟中的虚拟机整合实现，发现初始放置和主机停用占据了大部分能耗节省，元启发式搜索往往不降反增或无显著改善，仅蚁群算法在轻负载异构场景下有微小优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心电力需求上升，虚拟机整合的能效至关重要。现有研究常将蚁群优化、粒子群优化、遗传算法等元启发式算法与传统分配策略对比，但这些基线在初始放置和迁移优化上均不同，导致难以分离元启发式搜索的真实贡献。

Method: 在 CloudSim Plus 中复现现有整合实现，设计匹配控制组：保持相同的初始打包和空主机停用机制，但移除迁移搜索。对比 ACO、PSO、改进遗传算法（MGA）在两种机群配置和四种负载模式下的表现，每种条件进行十次随机重复实验。

Result: 匹配控制组相对扩散基线实现 0.8%-81.4% 的节能，随负载上升可用节省量急剧下降，且无迁移或任务未完成。24 次对比中，元启发式搜索在 11 种情况增加能耗，7 种无差异，6 种降低能耗；经 Holm 校正后，10 种增加和 5 种减少仍显著。6 种表观减少中有 5 种伴随任务未完成，按交付计算工作量归一化后消失。仅 ACO 在轻异构负载下保留 3.22 个百分点的改进（95% CI 2.96-3.47）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，初始打包和主机停用解释了所考察实现中大部分测得的节能效果，强调了匹配控制条件和工作负载完成度度量对可靠评估虚拟机整合算法的重要性。

Abstract: Energy-efficient virtual machine (VM) consolidation matters as data centre electricity demand rises. Bio-inspired metaheuristics, including ant colony optimisation (ACO), particle swarm optimisation (PSO), and genetic algorithms, have reported substantial energy savings. However, these improvements are commonly evaluated against conventional allocation policies that differ in both initial VM placement and subsequent migration optimisation, making it difficult to determine the contribution of metaheuristic search. This study reproduces an existing consolidation implementation in CloudSim Plus and introduces a matched control that retains the same initial packing and empty-host deactivation mechanisms but excludes migration search. ACO, PSO, and a modified genetic algorithm (MGA) are evaluated across two fleet configurations and four workload regimes, with ten stochastic replications per condition. The matched control achieves energy savings of 0.8-81.4% relative to the spreading baseline, the amount available falling sharply as load rises, without migrations or incomplete workload execution. Across 24 comparisons, metaheuristic search increases energy consumption in 11 cases, produces identical outcomes in seven, and reduces consumption in six. Of these, ten increases and five reductions remain statistically significant after Holm correction. Five of the six apparent reductions coincide with unfinished tasks and disappear after normalisation by delivered computational work. Only ACO under light heterogeneous load retains a 3.22-percentage-point improvement (95% CI 2.96-3.47). These findings demonstrate that initial packing and host deactivation account for most of the measured energy savings in the examined implementations, highlighting the importance of matched control conditions and workload-completion measures for reliable evaluation of VM consolidation algorithms.

</details>


### [106] [MANTA: Unlocking DAG Flexibility in Asynchronous BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34419)
*Mohan Yu,Chenlin Wu,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Chenyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一種靈活的 DAG 成長框架，用於非同步 BFT 共識，以解決異質 WAN 中傳統 DAG BFT 協定因輪次同步僵化導致的延遲與效能問題。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 現有 DAG-based BFT 協定在異質廣域網路環境下，因為複本需在固定輪次中等待足夠的引用來源才會推進，導致輪次拉長與效能下降；而引用法人的必要性僅在於安全性與活性，不應阻礙每輪的推進。

Method: 提出將連通性需求分散到多個輪次累積的靈活 DAG 成長機制，並實現 MANTA 協定，結合放鬆輪次同步與彈性幣投機以提前觸發領導者選舉與最終確定。

Result: 在異質 WAN 部署實驗中，MANTA 將延遲降低 approximately 30% 至 70%。

Conclusion: 透過打破輪次同步僵化，MANTA 提供更具彈性的 DAG 成長策略，有效提升非同步 BFT 共識在異質網路環境下的效能表現。

Abstract: DAG-based BFT protocols marked a shift in consensus design by replacing single-leader proposal paths with concurrent block proposals linked through a shared graph, where references encode consensus evidence. However, under heterogeneous wide-area networks (WANs), existing designs suffer from prolonged rounds and degraded performance due to straggler effects. The root cause is rigid round advancement: replicas move in synchronized rounds, waiting for enough references from the previous round before advancing.
  We propose a flexible DAG growth framework for asynchronous BFT consensus. Our key observation is that while reference quorums are necessary for safety and liveness, they need not block every round advancement. Instead, the connectivity required for commitment can be accumulated across rounds. Within the framework, we instantiate MANTA, which combines flexible DAG growth to advance by relaxing round synchronization and flexible coin to trigger leader election and finality earlier. Experiments on heterogeneous WAN deployments show that MANTA reduces latency by approximately 30%-70% in our evaluation.

</details>


### [107] [Prefix Keys and Values Matter Late: RIPE Caching for Self-Speculative Decoding in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34436)
*Dezhen Lu,Runguo Li,Zixiang Ni*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RIPE缓存压缩方法,只保存最深层KV平面,大幅降低内存占用,同时保持自推测解码速度提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环路语言模型(R次循环)带来巨大的KV缓存内存开销,现有方法需保存全部平面,亟需高效压缩方案

Method: 分析表明晚期循环(25-32)比早期(2-9)更敏感,最深平面主导性能;提出RIPE:保存最深平面+近期窗口全深度,其余位置仅最深平面

Result: 在Huginn模型上,64位位置窗口下仅用11.9%内存(1024token上下文)移除89%尾部位置,自推测解码速度提升2.58倍;固定内存吞吞量提升2.24倍

Conclusion: RIPE通过选择性保存最深平面实现内存-速度权衡优化,验证了简单模型对接受率、解码成本和内存的预测,适用于批量验证场景

Abstract: A looped language model runs one block of layers $R$ times per token, so its key-value (KV) cache holds $R$ planes per recurrent layer, and self-speculative decoding (draft with a few loops, verify with all $R$) reads them all. We find that prefix keys and values matter late: at equal perturbation size, loops 25-32 are $e^{15.0}$ (Huginn-0125) and $e^{12.8}$ (Raven-Llama) times more sensitive than loops 2-9, and the planes approach the last one geometrically, at 0.88 and 0.81 per loop. Whether the deepest plane alone suffices then depends on how ripe the late planes are: on Raven, storing only it is indistinguishable from a bf16-to-fp32 change; on Huginn, 1% of positions carry 74% of its error. We turn this into RIPE, which stores the deepest plane everywhere except a window of recent positions kept at full depth. On Huginn, a 64-position window removes 89% of the tail positions at 11.9% of the full cache's memory (1024-token context), and any window at least as long as the draft block makes batched verification block-independent by construction. Nine pre-registered GPU batches back these results. Simple models of acceptance, decoding cost and memory, two of them checked on held-out measurements, account for the measured self-speculative speed-ups ($2.09\times$ with the full cache, $2.58\times$ with the deepest plane) and decompose the $2.24\times$ fixed-memory throughput gain of one shared plane.

</details>


### [108] [Supervision Without Blocking: An Evidence-Driven Redesign of OTP-Style Fault Tolerance for Cooperative Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34521)
*Sam Uwejan,Erdogan Dogdu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: The paper audits supervision in actor frameworks, uncovering 24 defect classes (rooted in missing explicit atomicity). It introduces a new supervision runtime that fixes all defects while preserving OTP's guarantees and adding supervisor responsiveness during group restarts and uncancellable message handlers. The solution is validated with 241 tests and defines unattainable forcing guarantees for cooperative runtimes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing actor frameworks inherit OTP's synchronous supervisor model, but the consistency contracts and atomicity assumptions are unexamined, posing risks. The authors wanted a systematic audit to identify and rectify supervision defects across frameworks.

Method: They performed a verbatim comparison of OTP documentation and source code with three Rust actor frameworks at fixed revisions, conducted blind adversarial audits, recorded evidence for each design decision, catalogued defects, and then designed a new supervision runtime featuring supervisor-owned completion values, per‑incarnation identity, and a single lifecycle state machine. The runtime was validated with 241 tests, including reproductions of reverted fixes.

Result: 24 defect classes were discovered; eight collapse to one root cause – lack of explicit atomicity for supervisors. The new runtime eliminates all defects, passes 241 tests, upholds OTP's verbatim‑verified contracts, adds supervisor responsiveness during group restarts, and guarantees that message handlers are never cancelled mid‑execution. The study also delineates forcing guarantees that no cooperative runtime can honor.

Conclusion: This work provides a comprehensive defect catalog, a concrete supervisor design that strengthens safety and liveness, and clarifies the theoretical limits of cooperative runtime supervision, advancing both the practical reliability and the formal understanding of actor‑model supervision.

Abstract: Actor frameworks on mainstream runtimes advertise Erlang/OTP-style supervision, but OTP's consistency contracts assume a synchronous, single-threaded supervisor, and ports inherit that assumption's absence as unexamined risk. We evaluated every supervision guarantee against pinned OTP documentation and source read verbatim, against the source of three further Rust actor frameworks at fixed revisions, and against the actor-model literature, recording the evidence behind each design decision. Blind adversarial audits and the cross-framework study surfaced 24 defect classes, of which eight reduce to one root cause: reconstructing, by explicit mechanism, the atomicity a synchronous supervisor gets implicitly. We introduce a supervision runtime that closes all 24 cataloged classes through supervisor-owned completion values, per-incarnation identity, and a single lifecycle state machine, validated by 241 tests including reverted-fix reproductions. The result holds OTP's verbatim-verified contracts while adding two properties OTP does not offer: a supervisor that remains responsive throughout group restarts, and message handlers that are never cancelled mid-execution. We delimit the forcing guarantees no cooperative runtime can honor.

</details>


### [109] [NovaFabric: A Black-Box Recorder for AI Agents with Sealed, Replayable, Diffable and Auditable Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34594)
*Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NovaFabric is an open-source (Apache-2.0) black-box recorder for AI agents that converts complex agent runs into sealed, replayable, auditable evidence capsules using flat-file directories and signed exports based on existing standards.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As AI agent runs become increasingly complex cascades of prompts, model calls, tool invocations, and sub-agents, traditional mutable and partial logs fail to provide reliable audit trails or meaningful post-failure analysis.

Method: The system uses two core formats: Run Capsules (flat-file directories as source of truth) and Evidence Bundles (signed auditor exports). It supports distributed execution through capsule hierarchies and implements features like fail-closed replay, diff gates, DSSE signatures, trust-level validation, and deny-by-default authorization. The implementation is validated via probes on 150 commands, 84 subpackages, and 16,700+ test functions.

Result: NovaFabric successfully provides a replayable, diffable, and auditable framework for AI agent runs, supporting standard protocols like OpenTelemetry GenAI, OpenLineage, W3C PROV, and in-toto/DSSE, with explicit maturity labels and documented limitations.

Conclusion: The report demonstrates that NovaFabric addresses critical auditing and accountability gaps in AI agent operations through a standards-compliant, transparent architecture, establishing a foundation for trustworthy agent governance without introducing experimental components.

Abstract: An AI agent run is no longer a function call. It is a cascade of prompts, model calls, tool invocations, file and network effects and sub-agents. When such a run misbehaves, or an auditor asks what happened, logs and traces answer poorly: they are mutable, partial and cannot be re-executed. This technical report describes NovaFabric, an open-source (Apache-2.0) black-box recorder for AI agents that turns a run into a sealed, replayable, diffable and auditable evidence capsule. The design rests on two formats only. The Run Capsule is a directory of flat files and the source of truth; every database is a rebuildable index over capsules. The Evidence Bundle is the signed export for an auditor. Everything else follows existing standards: OpenTelemetry GenAI, OpenLineage, W3C PROV, in-toto/DSSE, RFC 3161 and CycloneDX. A single-node run is the smallest case of a distributed one; the schema supports a capsule hierarchy for Slurm arrays and Kubernetes jobs, though automatic parent finalization is library-only today. The report covers the architecture and the engines behind the verb chain capture, seal, replay, diff and audit: fail-closed mocked replay; a diff gate with a stable exit-code contract; DSSE signatures that bind their intent and a capsule root that commits to file names; a capsule treated as untrusted input; signer identity-trust levels with offline TSA and CRL checking; maker-checker promotion against an operator-held trust list; and a deny-by-default authorization table for every dashboard route. It presents no new experiments; the evaluation is in arXiv:2609.12582. Every count except the private ADR tally is generated by a probe script run on the public source at the v0.107.0 tag (150 top-level commands, 84 Python subpackages, 16,721 test functions), every capability carries an explicit maturity label, and honest-limits boxes state where the system stops.

</details>


### [110] [Cross-Modal Data Co-Locality for Heterogeneous Storage Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34595)
*Abdullah Al-Mamun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Coloc 是一个面向多模态向量存储的存储管理层，通过跨模态访问图和迁移管理器优化多模态向量的物理布局，在不修改现有 ANN 索引的前提下降低跨模态查询的数据移动和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式向量存储中，多模态表示虽被联合访问，却独立分区与放置，导致跨模态物理局部性未被优化。即便各模态的近邻搜索高效，多模态查询仍会产生远程访问和跨层数据搬移。

Method: Coloc 构建融合语义亲和性、共访问与时间行为的跨模态访问图；采用受容量与拓扑约束的移动感知优化器进行向量放置；通过迁移管理器在局部性收益与迁移开销之间权衡以适应负载漂移；并使用置信度门控预取器掩盖慢层延迟。它运行在现有 ANN 索引之上，无需修改嵌入、搜索或检索语义。

Result: 在涵盖 GPU、NUMA-DRAM、NVMe 与 S3 的多层 AWS 测试台上，Coloc 显著降低了数据搬移和查询延迟，且不改变 ANN 检索效果。在计划负载漂移下，其迁移管理器与定期全局重组达到相当局部性，但搬移数据量减少 5.9 倍、完成速度提升 3.9 倍。

Conclusion: 将跨模态访问局部性作为一等公民的数据布局信号，可在无需改动底层 ANN 索引的前提下，有效优化多模态向量存储的放置与迁移，从而降低异构分层存储上的数据移动开销与查询延迟。

Abstract: Distributed vector stores increasingly serve multimodal workloads whose representations are jointly accessed but independently partitioned and placed. As a result, cross-modal physical locality remains largely unoptimized. Multimodal queries can incur remote accesses and cross-tier data movement even when each modality's ANN search remains efficient. We present Coloc, a storage-management layer that makes cross-modal access locality a first-class data-layout signal. A Cross-Modal Access Graph combines semantic affinity, co-access, and temporal behavior. A movement-aware optimizer places frequently co-accessed vectors under capacity and topology constraints. A migration manager adapts placement as workloads shift by weighing locality gains against migration cost. A confidence-gated prefetcher hides remaining slow-tier latency. Coloc operates above existing ANN indexes without modifying embeddings, search, or retrieval semantics. We deploy Coloc on a multi-tier AWS testbed spanning GPU, NUMA-DRAM, NVMe, and S3. Coloc substantially reduces data movement and query latency while leaving ANN retrieval unchanged. Under a scheduled workload shift, its migration manager matches the locality of periodic global reorganization while moving 5.9x less data and completing 3.9x faster.

</details>


### [111] [Failure-Resilient and Verifiable Distributed Approximate Nearest-Neighbor Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34608)
*Abdullah Al-Mamun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TrustANN 是一种中层组件，为分布式近似最近邻（ANN）检索提供查询级别的信任保障，解决了现有系统在保护索引完整性或副本可用性方面的不足，特别是针对被篡改的副本返回伪造但有效的检索结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式向量数据库在安全敏感应用中越来越受支持，但现有系统主要保护索引完整性或副本可用性，而非检索结果的可信度。被篡改的副本可以返回看似合理但被操纵的结果，这些结果在密码学上仍然有效，传统的复制机制和拜占庭防御无法直接确认检索结果的可信度。

Method: TrustANN 引入了三个互补协议：TrustBind 验证从认证、一致且跨故障域独立的副本证据中获取检索信任；CoordShift 异步构建可重用的协调纪元，位于查询关键路径之外；AvailGuard 在副本不可用、陈旧或冲突时执行有界恢复，同时保持信任标准。

Result: 在 AWS 部署的分布式 FAISS HNSW 上，TrustANN 将拜占庭检索攻击的检索成功率从 1.000 降低到 0.000；验证层增加了 95 分位数的干净延迟仅 9.36%；在副本崩溃情况下，TrustANN 能够在 33% 的故障率下保持可信检索，并在证据不足时正确返回 "Unverified"。

Conclusion: TrustANN 通过查询级别的信任验证机制，有效地解决了分布式向量数据库检索结果可信度问题，在保持低延迟的同时提供了强大的安全保障。

Abstract: Distributed vector databases increasingly support security-sensitive applications, yet existing systems primarily protect index integrity or replica availability rather than the trustworthiness of retrieved results. Consequently, a compromised replica can return a plausible but manipulated result that remains cryptographically valid, while conventional replication and Byzantine defenses do not directly establish whether the retrieved results are trustworthy. We present TrustANN, a middleware layer that provides query-level trust for distributed approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) retrieval without modifying the underlying ANN algorithm. TrustANN introduces three complementary protocols: TrustBind, which verifies retrieval trust from authenticated, consistent, and failure-domain-independent replica evidence; CoordShift, which asynchronously constructs reusable coordination epochs outside the query critical path; and AvailGuard, which performs bounded recovery when replicas are unavailable, stale, or conflicting while preserving the trust criterion. Queries are marked Trusted only when sufficient independent evidence exists for every relevant shard; otherwise, TrustANN returns Unverified. On an AWS deployment using distributed FAISS HNSW, TrustANN reduces retrieval attack success rate from 1.000 to 0.000 under the tested Byzantine-retrieval attack with two-of-three replica verification. The verification layer increases clean p95 latency by only 9.36%. Under replica crashes, TrustANN maintains trusted retrieval through 33% failures and correctly returns Unverified when sufficient evidence is unavailable.

</details>


### [112] [Live Adaptation of Dataflow Stream Processing for Hybrid Edge-Cloud Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34658)
*Yuanli Wang,Prateek Jain,Lei Huang,Le Xu,Vasiliki Kalavri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Sluice 是一种适用于混合云（边缘-云）环境的自适应数据流处理系统，它兼容现有的数据流引擎（如 Apache Flink），通过动态查询重写和实时路由实现计算在边缘与云之间的无缝迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据流处理系统（如 Flink 和 Spark Streaming）主要针对数据中心设计。在混合边缘-云分析管道中，纯云端处理或静态边缘卸载难以在动态 WAN 条件和有限边缘资源下维持目标处理速率。现有引擎假设资源同构、采用静态任务放置，且缺乏对动态网络和边缘条件的适应能力。

Method: 引入 Sluice 系统，其核心贡献包括：(i) 自动查询重写器，将应用分解为独立、容错的片段；(ii) 实时路由机制，在不暂停执行的情况下在边缘和云之间转移计算，并保留事件时间语义。该系统在 Apache Flink 上实现，并通过物理测试床（树莓派）和大型模拟测试床（100个虚拟机）进行评估。

Result: 在五个真实世界应用上的实验结果表明，Sluice 能够无缝地在边缘和云之间转移计算，并且相比基线系统持续实现了更高的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Sluice 成功将数据中心导向的数据流处理平台扩展至混合边缘-云管道，在保持用户应用透明性、容错性和事件时间语义的同时，提升了系统在动态环境下的吞吐量和适应性。

Abstract: Dataflow streaming systems, like Apache Flink and Spark Streaming, are integral to modern business analytics, providing real-time insights across industries. However, for emerging hybrid edge-cloud analytics pipelines, neither processing everything in the cloud nor static edge offloading can reliably sustain the target rate under dynamic WAN conditions and limited edge resources. In this paper, we explore how these datacenter-centric platforms can be extended to support such hybrid analytics pipelines. Our goal is to maintain full transparency for user applications, while retaining built-in fault tolerance, out-of-order processing, and existing ecosystem integrations. Achieving this is challenging because existing dataflow engines assume homogeneous resources, rely on static task placement, and do not support adaptation under dynamic network and edge conditions. We introduce Sluice, an adaptive data stream processing system for hybrid clouds, compatible with dataflow streaming engines. Sluice's key contributions are: (i) an automatic query rewriter that decomposes applications into independent, fault-tolerant segments, and (ii) a live routing mechanism that shifts computations between edge and cloud without pausing execution while preserving event-time semantics. We implement Sluice on Apache Flink and evaluate it on a physical testbed with Raspberry Pi devices, as well as on a large simulated testbed of 100 VMs. Across five real-world applications, our results show that Sluice seamlessly shifts computations between edge and cloud, while consistently achieving higher throughput than baseline systems.

</details>


### [113] [HiLO: Towards Product-Level Graceful Degradation with a Quality-Aware, Load-Aware Control Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34844)
*Knut Magne Risvik,Zhuowei Li,Nirupam Purushothama,Hunter Su,Greg Dunham,Yan Reznikov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HiLO is an automated, product-level graceful degradation system that maps traffic load to a lattice of pre‑validated product states, enabling predictable, testable quality under sustained overload. It has been deployed at Microsoft Bing, handling regular traffic spikes and keeping relevance score drops under 0.5 points even when load exceeds twice the baseline peak.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional degradation mechanisms (load shedding, circuit breakers, manual feature switches) protect services but create untestable product states and unpredictable quality. The need for a scalable, deterministic, product‑centric degradation approach grew as GPU‑intensive LLM inference introduces persistent capacity constraints that provisioning alone cannot solve.

Method: HiLO constructs a small lattice of pre‑validated product states ordered by degradation severity. Load observations are mapped to states, and dependencies are resolved at design time, eliminating runtime discovery. The same lattice abstraction works across heterogeneous services (deep models, LLMs, serving layers), providing automated, continuous control of degradation.

Result: Deployed in Bing (over 1 billion monthly active users), HiLO manages about two traffic surges per week across all datacenters, bounding OneDCG (relevance) drops to <0.5 points (operational target) even under sustained loads >2× baseline peak. It complements existing service‑level protections with product‑level coordination, making quality measurable, testable, and predictable.

Conclusion: HiLO demonstrates that a product‑level degradation lattice can deliver deterministic, testable, and predictable system behavior at scale, overcoming the limitations of service‑level mechanisms and supporting reliable operation of large‑scale, GPU‑intensive services.

Abstract: Graceful degradation is a key mechanism employed by large-scale systems to handle overload conditions. Its importance has grown further as GPU-intensive LLM inference introduces persistent capacity constraints that cannot be addressed through provisioning alone. Common degradation approaches rely on service-level mechanisms such as load shedding and circuit breakers, or on feature-level switches (some manually controlled) to preserve availability and quality. While effective at protecting services, such approaches lead to untestable product states and thereby unpredictable product quality.
  We present HiLO (High Load Optimizer), an automated product-level degradation system designed to handle graceful degradation at scale. HiLO maps observed load to a product state lattice: a small set of pre-validated product states, partially ordered by degradation severity. Dependencies are resolved at design time enabling deterministic and continuous control without requiring runtime dependency discovery. The same abstraction applies unchanged across heterogeneous services, including GPU-intensive deep models, LLMs and their serving layers.
  At Microsoft, HiLO is deployed in Bing (serving over a billion monthly active users), where it manages on average two traffic surges per week across all datacenters, bounding OneDCG (relevance) drop to under 0.5 points (the operational target) even under sustained load exceeding 2$\times$ baseline peak. HiLO complements existing service-level protections with product-level coordination and makes product quality measurable, testable and predictable.

</details>


### [114] [When and Why to Use TMA: A Workload-Grounded Characterization of Tensor Memory Acceleration on NVIDIA Hopper GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34907)
*Christin David Bose,Cesar Avalos,Junrui Pan,Timothy Rogers*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个实证性研究，旨在指导实践者何时选择 Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) 以及是否使用集群多播。通过分析 FlashAttention-3、CUTLASS 和 FlashInfer 等内核的实际负载机制选择，作者揭示了影响 TMA、多播、分布式共享内存和 cp.async 的关键因素，并提出了适用于 Hopper H100 的经验法则。这些规则被验证在 Blackwell (SM100) 上仍然有效，尽管绝对的交叉点会因算术密度和带宽的变化而有所不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然 TMA 在 Hopper GPU 上广泛应用于生产内核，但开发者通常仅将其应用于特定的张量操作，而对其他操作使用传统的加载机制。现有的文档和微基准测试研究既没有回答核心问题：当一个内核中有多个内存操作时，应选择哪种加载机制（尤其是是否使用 TMA 多播 ）？本研究旨在填补这一实践空白。

Method: 1. 对 H100 上 FlashAttention-3 的前向传播内核进行了详细分析，记录了 TMA、多播、DSM 和 cp.async 的使用情况。
2. 针对关键变量进行了微基准测试，包括传输大小的交叉点和多播扩展行为，以揭示不同加载机制的性能差异。
3. 建立经验法则，指导 Hopper 上负载机制的选择。
4. 将 TMA 多播与 DSM 进行了直接比较，考察它们作为集群广播原语的表现。
5. 在不同的页面大小、数据类型、模型形状和执行阶段下，比较了 FlashAttention-3 中分页的键-值 (KV) 操作的 TMA 与 cp.async 调度情况。
6. 扩展到 Blackwell，利用 B200 上的 CUTLASS GEMM 研究，验证了定性规则的通用性，并关注绝对交叉点如何随世代变化而变化。

Result: 研究产生了两个主要成果：
- 定量可预测的指导方针，明确指出在哪些条件下 TMA 是有利的，以及在哪些情况下集群多播对 TMA 是有利或不利的。
- 验证了 Hopper 的定性调度规则可以推广到 Blackwell，但具体的性能交叉点会因算术密集度和带宽的变化而有所不同。

Conclusion: 通过将实际内核观测与针对性的微基准测试相结合，论文为开发者提供了一个实用的决策框架，用于选择最适合特定张量操作的内存加载机制。在 Hopper 上验证并在 Blackwell 上扩展经验法则，表明了其广泛适用性，为下一代加速器上的优化提供了指导。

Abstract: The Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA), introduced on NVIDIA Hopper, is used pervasively in production GPU kernels yet its usage is conspicuously selective: expert kernel developers in CUTLASS, FlashAttention-3, and FlashInfer apply TMA to some tensor operands within a kernel but fall back to cp.async, direct global loads, or distributed shared memory (DSM) for others. Vendor documentation describes the API, and recent microbenchmarking work characterizes TMA throughput in isolation, but neither answers the question practitioners actually face: given a memory operand in a kernel, should it use TMA, and if so, with or without cluster multicast? We address this gap with a workload-grounded characterization study on H100. We catalog the TMA, multicast, DSM, and cp.async choices made inside FlashAttention-3 forward, isolate the discriminating features through targeted microbenchmarks (transfer-size crossover, multicast scaling), and produce rules of thumb for the load-mechanism choice on Hopper. We further compare TMA multicast against DSM as competing cluster-broadcast primitives, and isolate the FlashAttention-3 paged-key-value (KV) TMA-vs-cp.async dispatch on H100 across page size, datatype, model shape, and execution phase. Our results yield concrete, predictive guidance on when TMA pays off and when multicast helps or hurts on Hopper, and we extend the analysis to server Blackwell (SM100) via a CUTLASS GEMM study on B200 that shows the qualitative dispatch rules carry across generations while absolute crossovers shift with tensor-core and bandwidth scaling.

</details>


### [115] [FrontGI: Architecture-Aware Parallel GNN Inference on Chiplet-Based Multicore Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34911)
*Binglin Ji,Chenfeng Zhao,Roger Chamberlain*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FrontGI accelerates GNN inference on massive graphs by using graph- and architecture-aware parallelization, achieving up to ~3× speedups over DGL and PyG on a 128‑core AMD EPYC system.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard GNN libraries (DGL, PyG) suffer poor scalability on modern multicore CPUs as graph size and core counts increase, limiting efficient inference on large‑scale data.

Method: FrontGI employs graph‑property‑ and architecture‑aware design, tailoring parallelization strategies and cache hierarchy usage for diverse graph structures to maximize multicore utilization.

Result: On a 128‑core AMD EPYC, FrontGI yields up to 3.35×, 3.20×, 2.89×, and 3.10× speedups for GCN, GraphSAGE, GIN, and SGC over DGL, with even larger gains versus PyG across varied large‑scale, high‑dimensional datasets.

Conclusion: Architecture‑aware parallelization can substantially boost GNN inference performance, making FrontGI a scalable solution for high‑performance GNN workloads.

Abstract: The realm of high-performance computing has expanded to include machine learning tasks of many forms. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are becoming increasingly popular, with their applications expanding across diverse domains. As the scale of graph data continues to grow, including larger numbers of nodes, edges, and higher embedding dimensions, standardized libraries such as DGL and PyG have been developed to facilitate GNN computation. These libraries allow the computation to be described a a high level of abstraction, providing high productivity on the part of the developer. However, with the rapid increase in the number of processor cores and the evolution of multicore architectures, these libraries often show poor scalability and fail to execute GNN inference efficiently on the latest multicore systems, particularly those with upwards of a hundred cores. To address this limitation, we introduce FrontGI, a fast and scalable set of techniques that advances the frontier of GNN inference on large-scale graphs (over 100,000 vertices) by leveraging graph-property- and architecture-aware design. FrontGI employs different parallelization strategies optimized for diverse graph structures, maximizing the utilization of multi-level cache hierarchies in multicore systems. We evaluate a number of GNN models with FrontGI on a 128-core AMD EPYC system. Compared to state-of-the-art libraries, FrontGI achieves up to 3.35$\times$, 3.20$\times$, 2.89$\times$ and 3.10$\times$ speedups over DGL for GCN, GraphSAGE, GIN, and SGC models, respectively, and even greater improvements over PyG, across a range of large-scale, high-dimensional graph datasets with diverse structural properties.

</details>


### [116] [Waypoint: Analytically Modeling GEMM for CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34914)
*Srinidhi N,Sunanda Somwase,Hemant Kumar Chandrakant Chandavar,Eashan Dash,Varun Agrawal,Prakash Raghavendra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 Waypoint 分析模型，自动识别给定硬件和矩阵形状下的最优 GEMM 分块大小，并基于该模型构建 CPU GEMM 库 OPAM，在与最先进库相当的性能基础上将 CPU 上的 LLM 推理加速 1.36-1.98 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能 GEMM 依赖精心选择的分块才能达到峰值性能，但手动寻找最优分块成本高、泛化性差；此外，硬件设计师缺乏在实验性硬件优化上估算实际性能的方法。

Method: Waypoint 捕捉内存层级交互、指令级并行和多线程等全部架构影响，估算任意分块选择下的可实现性能，并以此为代理在分块空间中搜索最优解；基于 Waypoint 构建 OPAM 库自动完成 GEMM 分解。

Result: OPAM 的性能与最先进的 GEMM 库持平，并在 CPU 上加速 LLM 推理，实现 1.36-1.98 倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: Waypoint 可自动化地为不同硬件和输入形状识别最优分块，同时帮助硬件设计师评估实验性优化的现实性能，为高性能 GEMM 实现及 CPU 上的 LLM 推理提供实用方案。

Abstract: High-performance General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) implementations rely on breaking down GEMM into carefully selected tiles to achieve peak performance. We present Waypoint, a new analytical model to automatically identify the optimal tile size for a given hardware and input matrix shape. Waypoint estimates the achievable performance for a given tile choice by capturing all the architectural effects including interactions with memory hierarchy, instruction-level parallelism, and multi-threading. Using the estimates as a proxy, we search through the space of possible tile sizes to identify the one that maximizes performance. Additionally, Waypoint helps hardware designers estimate realistic performance on experimental hardware optimizations. We build a new GEMM library OPAM for CPUs that uses Waypoint to automatically break down GEMM and achieve performance that matches state-of-the-art GEMM libraries and helps accelerate LLM inference on CPUs achieving $1.36 - 1.98 \times$ speedups.

</details>


### [117] [Priority-Aware Scheduler for Serverless Functions Provision and Load Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35073)
*Cheryl Lee,Zishan Su,Zhouruixing Zhu,Tianyi Yang,Yuxin Su,Pinjia He,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SPES-Ext 是一个面向内存受限分布式工作节点的基于规则的优先级感知调度器，旨在解决无服务器计算中的冷启动问题、内存浪费和负载不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务器调度策略采用过度简化的预加载/卸载机制，未能充分利用调用模式，难以在启动延迟和资源效率之间取得最优权衡；同时忽视了内存受限多工作节点环境的实际约束，导致函数驱逐和负载不均衡问题加剧。

Method: 基于两个关键观察：无服务器系统呈现集中的调用模式，具有行为可预测性；这些模式允许对函数进行分类以实现细粒度实例管理。设计包含三个协同组件：预测性函数预置、驱逐优化和动态负载均衡。

Result: 实验验证显示 SPES-Ext 优于最先进方案：75 分位数冷启动减少 80%，内存浪费降低 83.92%，工作节点负载方差改善 52.49%。

Conclusion: SPES-Ext 通过特征驱动的优先级调度规则，有效解决了冷启动、内存浪费和负载不均衡问题，显著提升了无服务器系统在内存受限环境下的性能与资源利用率。

Abstract: Serverless computing has emerged as a pivotal cloud deployment paradigm due to its efficient utilization of on-demand resources. However, the persistent cold start problem continues to hinder system performance. Current strategies often employ oversimplified preloading/unloading mechanisms that inadequately exploit invocation patterns, failing to optimize the critical trade-off between startup latency and resource efficiency. Furthermore, existing approaches disregard practical constraints of memory-limited multi-worker environments, exacerbating function eviction and load imbalance issues. To address these limitations, we present SPES-Ext, a rule-based priority-aware scheduler designed for memory-constrained distributed worker nodes. Our methodology stems from two key observations: (1) serverless systems exhibit concentrated invocation patterns enabling behavioral predictability, and (2) these patterns permit function categorization for granular instance management. This insight posits that characteristic-driven prioritization enables optimal scheduling rules, leading to our system design, which integrates three coordinated components: predictive function provisioning, eviction optimization, and dynamic load balancing. Experimental validation demonstrates the superiority of SPES-Ext over state-of-the-art solutions: 80% reduction in $75^{th}$-percentile cold starts, 83.92% decrease in memory waste, and 52.49% improvement in worker load variance.

</details>


### [118] [Making the Telemetry Pipeline Measurable: An Edge IoT Testbed and Dataset for End-to-End Latency, Provenance, and Energy-Aware Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35591)
*Dimitris Gkoulis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个边缘物联网测量测试台及其数据集，该测试台捕获感知、序列化、消息代理、摄取和持久化等测 telemetry 管线的所有阶段的时间戳、溯源标识符和能量信号。这些数据以压缩 Avro 流和分析就绪的 Parquet 表的形式发布，支持重放和查询，为可重复的边缘物联网延迟、溯源和成本分析提供了一个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘物联网评估通常仅关注传感器值或平台功能，而忽略了测量端到端 telemetry 管线本身。由于缺少可测量的管线对象，可重复地评估时效性、溯源和运营成本变得困难，特别是在早期物联网设计阶段，缺乏真实部署和高品质开放数据。

Method: 设计了一个以测量为先的边缘物联网测试台，采用角色驱动的事件驱动架构（Source-Broker-Aggregator），使用 Avro 架构化的 TelemetryMessage 封装消息，嵌入了跨角色的时间戳和相关性标识符。通过向 MQTT 代理 (Eclipse Mosquitto) 插件添加高精度入口/出口时间戳来跟踪中介影响。创建了一个确定性的原始到表格的转换管道，生成两种表示形式：(i) 可重放的压缩 Avro 事件流，(ii) 分区化的长格式 Parquet 表。

Result: 该测试台和数据集提供了时效性、溯源和能量等三个 telemetry 维度的高保真度测量。演示展示了延迟摘要、测量上下文分布和能量聚合，但不建立新的归因方法或受控基准。

Conclusion: 通过以可重放和可查询的形式共同发布异构 telemetry 数据、管道时间戳和能量信号，该工件提供了一个可重复的探索性分析和后续基准研究的基线。

Abstract: Edge-centric IoT systems are increasingly evaluated through sensor values or platform features, yet the telemetry pipeline itself, the sequence of sensing, serialization, message mediation, ingestion, and persistence, is rarely released as a measurable object. This limits reproducible benchmarking of timeliness, provenance, and operational cost, especially in early-stage IoT design where real deployments and high-quality open data are often unavailable. I present a measurement-first edge IoT testbed and dataset that elevate timeliness, provenance, and energy to first-class telemetry dimensions. The testbed operationalizes a role-based event-driven architecture with explicit Source-Broker-Aggregator responsibilities and a schema-governed TelemetryMessage envelope (Avro) that embeds correlation identifiers and a uniform, cross-role vocabulary of stage-level timestamps. To trace mediation effects, I extend an MQTT broker (Eclipse Mosquitto) with a plugin that annotates messages with high-precision ingress/egress timestamps. I release the dataset in a dual representation: (i) replayable raw events as compressed Avro streams and (ii) analysis-ready long-format Parquet tables partitioned for efficient query and benchmarking, generated by a deterministic raw-to-tabular transformation pipeline. The contribution is an integrated engineering artifact and dataset built from established components. The demonstration describes one deployment through latency summaries, measurement-context distributions, and energy aggregates; it does not establish a new attribution method or a controlled performance benchmark. By co-releasing heterogeneous telemetry with pipeline timing and energy signals in both replayable and queryable forms, the artifact provides a basis for reproducible exploratory analysis and subsequent benchmarking studies.

</details>


### [119] [Context Is Not Free: Carbon-Aware LLM Routing for Multi-Turn LLM Web Services across Geo-Distributed Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35230)
*Xiaoyang Zhang,Yuejun Yan,Qingang Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CarbonRouter 是一个在线碳感知路由框架，针对大语言模型（LLM）的多轮对话场景，联合选择模型、GPU 类型和部署区域，通过缓存感知的碳成本模型和轻量级门控机制，最小化碳排放，同时提升回答质量和降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的 Web 服务产生巨大碳足迹，且运行在地理分布式数据中心，电网碳强度随地区和时间变化。现有碳感知路由器将对话的每轮查询视为独立请求，忽略了键值（KV）缓存复用带来的状态性：跨部署迁移需重新计算历史，其碳成本随对话增长可能抵消迁移收益。

Method: 提出 CarbonRouter 框架：1) 缓存感知碳成本模型，利用服务后端提供的缓存 token 计数，为每个候选部署计算含历史重算成本的碳排放；2) 轻量级门控机制，当预测下一轮可能迁回原部署并再次重建历史时，保持会话在当前缓存部署；3) 在线联合优化模型、GPU 类型和区域选择。

Result: 在两个真实对话数据集、五个 LLM、三种 GPU、四个区域的 trace-driven 实验中，CarbonRouter 实现最低碳排放。相比最先进的碳感知路由器 FairCarbon，平均减少 CO₂ 16.1%，同时提升预测回答质量和估计延迟；对于超过 30 轮的长会话，节省进一步上升至 23.1%。

Conclusion: CarbonRouter 通过显式建模 KV 缓存重算成本并引入会话粘性门控，有效解决了多轮对话中的碳感知路由难题，在显著降低碳排放的同时改善了服务质量，为绿色 LLM 部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The sustainability of the Web has drawn growing attention, as large language model (LLM)-based web services leave a substantial carbon footprint. These services typically run in geo-distributed data centers whose grid carbon intensity varies across regions and over time, so routing each query to a cleaner region or a smaller model can reduce emissions. A conversation, however, is a stateful web session: each turn reuses the key--value (KV) cache of its history, which resides only where earlier turns were served. Serving a turn on another deployment requires recomputing this history, whose carbon cost grows with the conversation and can outweigh the saving of the move. Existing carbon-aware LLM routers treat a conversation's queries as independent and ignore this cost. We present CarbonRouter, an online framework that jointly selects the model, GPU type, and region for each query to minimize carbon emissions. CarbonRouter introduces a cache-aware carbon cost model, which charges history recomputation to every candidate deployment using cached-token counts from the serving backends, and uses a lightweight gate that keeps a session on its cached deployment when it would likely move back at the next turn and rebuild its history again. We evaluate CarbonRouter in trace-driven experiments on two real-world conversation datasets with five LLMs, three GPU types, and four regions, where it achieves the lowest carbon of all policies. CarbonRouter reduces CO$_2$ by 16.1\% on average relative to FairCarbon, a state-of-the-art carbon-aware router with the same action space, while also improving predicted answer quality and estimated latency. Moreover, the saving rises further to 23.1\% for sessions longer than 30 turns.

</details>


### [120] [How Numerical Errors Scale with Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35413)
*Niklas Baumgarten*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过并行化法则结合算法复杂度估计,在限定相同计算资源或能耗预算下比较数值误差的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏统一框架公平比较不同算法、硬件及软件实现,因其在固定资源约束下评估数值误差的公平性与可行性有重要研究价值。

Method: 选取复杂度已知且可并行执行的算法,结合法线并行化定律(如 Amdahl 定律),在相同资源或能耗预算约束下运行,比较各配置所得数值误差。

Result: 文中以实例验证了该方法可在保证公平比较前提下量化数值误差与计算资源的关系,具一般化推广潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法为算法、硬件及软件实现间的公平性能评估提供了简单可行的框架,值得后续深入探索与扩展。

Abstract: In this short technical note, we outline how to bound numerical errors with respect to computational resources. We consider algorithms with known complexity estimates that can be executed in parallel and combine these estimates with parallelization laws. We suppose that the proposed approach generalizes beyond the presented examples and argue that it provides a straightforward and fair framework for comparing algorithms, hardware systems, and software implementations. This is achieved by imposing identical resource or energy budgets on each configuration, and comparing the resulting numerical error evaluations.

</details>


### [121] [Governing Enterprise AI Traffic at Scale: Design and Operation of a Policy-Driven, Multi-Vendor LLM Gateway on Envoy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35509)
*Mithun Sasidharan,Srinivasan Manoharan,Rupesh Thakur,Abhinav TK,Ashwin Hegde,Anand Gautam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PayPal设计并投入生产的Cosmos LLM Gateway，作为企业级大语言模型（LLM）流量的统一治理入口。它基于Envoy构建，通过进程内外分工协作的架构（进程外处理控制决策，进程内通过Rust WebAssembly进行格式转换和流式处理），解决了多供应商模型集成带来的凭证混乱、格式锁定和治理不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型组织同时使用来自多个供应商的LLM，每个供应商都有独特的API、凭证模型、流式协议和价格。直接集成会导致凭证蔓延、格式锁定、无序支出和治理不一致。为了解决这些异构性带来的挑战，需要一个统一、高性能且具备强治理和成本控制能力的网关。

Method: 架构基于Envoy，按延迟剖面拆分工作：I/O绑定的控制决策（认证、凭证代理、策略与预算评估）运行在进程外服务中；线格式转换、令牌计量和提示缓存优化运行在进程内的Rust WebAssembly过滤器中，避免流式响应等待进程外调用。治理采用基于Open Policy Agent的声明式策略，通过控制平面进行角色授权、审批、试点、影子模式发布及版本化管理。同时配备了DLP（数据防泄露）层进行敏感内容拦截与脱敏，并通过基于每模型计数器的“支出范围”实现预算无缝应用。

Result: 截至2026年9月，该网关已服务超过12,000名开发者和数百个业务用例，每日处理约2000亿个令牌，覆盖100多个模型，业务可用性达99.999%。性能基准测试显示，在两台4 vCPU实例上处理每秒超过2,200个请求时，核心请求路径的中位开销仅为4毫秒，p99开销为11毫秒。

Conclusion: Cosmos LLM Gateway通过创新的混合架构与声明式治理策略，成功实现了高性能、高可用、合规且成本可控的企业级LLM流量管理。该方案显著降低了多厂商集成的复杂度，提供了低延迟、高吞吐的透明化网关服务，为大型企业的AI治理提供了实践范例。

Abstract: Large organizations consume large language models (LLMs) from several vendors at once, each with its own API dialect, credential model, streaming protocol and pricing. Integrated directly, this heterogeneity yields credential sprawl, format lock-in, unbounded spend and inconsistent governance. We present the design and production operation of the Cosmos LLM Gateway, the governed entry point for enterprise LLM traffic at PayPal. Built on Envoy, it splits work by latency profile: I/O-bound control decisions (authentication, credential brokering, policy and budget evaluation) run in an out-of-process external-processing service, while wire-format translation, token metering and prompt-cache shaping run in-proxy as a Rust WebAssembly filter, so that, by default, streamed response body chunks never wait on an out-of-process call. Models, providers and prices are largely validated data rather than code. Governance is declarative policy evaluated by Open Policy Agent and managed through a control plane with role-scoped authoring, approvals, pilots, shadow-mode rollout, usage notifications and versioned policy bundles. For use cases that require it, a data-loss-prevention layer can block, redact or pseudonymize detected sensitive content before it reaches a provider. Spend scopes are views over per-model usage counters, so a new budget applies to spend already recorded in its window without migration. As of September 2026, the gateway serves more than 12,000 developers and hundreds of business use cases, handling roughly 200 billion tokens per day across more than 100 models at 99.999% availability to business. In a benchmark of the core request path against a synthetic upstream, it adds a median of 4 ms and a p99 of 11 ms of overhead at more than 2,200 requests per second on two 4-vCPU instances. We report the architecture, policy model, evaluation and lessons learned.

</details>


### [122] [WorldPager: Zero-Stall Tiered Memory for Long-Horizon World Models on a Single Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35529)
*Bingnan Liu,Ruini Xue*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WorldPager uses a GPU/DRAM/NVMe hierarchy and pose‑aware prefetching to store int4 KV memory, enabling 37.5 minutes of continuous video at 9.5 fps with zero stalls and constant host memory on a single RTX 4090.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current interactive world models hit memory capacity and positional horizon limits on consumer hardware, causing stalls and limiting session length.

Method: An analytical model predicts spill points; WorldPager places int4 KV in a tiered memory hierarchy, prefetches based on extrapolated camera poses, and writes back asynchronously, avoiding stalls.

Result: Achieves 37.5 minutes of zero‑stall video at ~9.5 fps with constant host memory; int4 storage shows no measurable quality loss and only ~1 MUSIQ point drop on long‑gap revisits.

Conclusion: Hierarchical memory management and
